--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
0.1  WHAT IS JEEMAXXING?
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

A client-side, offline-first JEE (Indian engineering entrance exam) grind
command center. It combines:

  - Deep-focus timers (Pomodoro / stopwatch / sprint widget) that pay a real
    x1.5 ELO bonus while you are locked in,
  - A question vault with AI-assisted ingestion (screenshots, Gemini Gem JSON
    dumps, answer-key mapping, diagram auto-crop),
  - An Elo-style cognitive matchmaking rating ("MMR") per subject + global,
    reverse-engineering question difficulty from your execution telemetry,
  - Spaced repetition (SuperMemo-2 variant) over an "error vault" of mistakes,
  - Accountability systems (checkpoints, night guard, bounty boss fights),
  - Recovery systems (CNS load meter, flow-state lifeline, deload days),
  - Gamification (a growing island/grove world, streaks, combo/shield economy,
    pixel-flame streak visualizers, emoji bursts),
  - Cloud backup to Google Drive, multi-tab sync, and full PWA/offline support.

Everything runs in the browser. No framework, no bundler, no build step.
All data lives in IndexedDB (+ localStorage caches), optionally mirrored to
Google Drive. Installable as a standalone PWA.

Identity (manifest.webmanifest):
  name          : "JEEMaxxing - Locked In"
  short_name    : "JEEMaxxing"
  description   : "JEE grind hub - focus timer, error vault, spaced repetition
                   and live forest."
  start_url     : ./index.html      scope: ./
  display       : standalone        orientation: any
  bg/theme color: #050508           lang: en
  categories    : education, productivity
  icons         : icons/icon-192.png, icons/icon-512.png,
                  icons/icon-512-maskable.png (+ apple-touch-icon-180.png)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
0.2  TECH STACK & ARCHITECTURE
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  - Vanilla ES6 modules (native import/export). Serve over HTTP; file:// is
    blocked by CORS for modules.
  - Layering:
      UI shell ......... index.html + styles.css (design tokens v4) + theme.js
                         + dashboard-clean.js
      Orchestrator ..... app.js (~11k lines): boot, practice, Elo, ingestion,
                         streaks, CK loop-rail, scratchpad, KaTeX engine
      Feature engines .. pomodoro.js, matrix.js, checkpoint.js, gallery-break.js,
                         cns-load.js, deload.js, lifeline.js, nightguard.js,
                         boot-sequence.js, metronome.js, focus-sound.js, fx.js
      Visual engines ... grove-islands.js, forest-bg.js, candlestick-engine.js
      Persistence ...... storage.js (IndexedDB + Google Drive sync + Gemini API)
      PWA .............. sw.js + manifest.webmanifest
      Vendored libs .... vendor/katex (KaTeX 0.16.x, offline math),
                         vendor/three (three.module.min.js; static fallback
                         poster used when WebGL/3D unavailable)
      Dev-only deps .... playwright-core (QA harnesses), freebuff (unused at
                         runtime)
  - Rules: AppState (storage.js) is the single source of truth; no circular
    imports - cross-module calls go through window.* bridges; append-only UI
    modules self-wire via guard flags (window.__dashCleanInit etc.).
  - Multi-tab reconciliation: BroadcastChannel 'jmax-tab-sync' merges solved /
    studySecs monotonically and adopts remotely-updated bank rows before commit.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
0.4  FEATURE INDEX (what you can DO with this app)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

STUDY & FOCUS
  - Pomodoro countdown + stopwatch mode; rounds; break actions (skip/+1/+5 min)
  - Dynamic subject switching mid-session (per-second attribution)
  - Ambient Sprint Widget docked bottom-right on every tab: idle pill with
    one-tap start, config popover (subject P/C/M, minutes +/-5 clamped [5,120],
    rounds +/-1 clamped [1,8], dynamic mode), progress ring, ends-HH:MM,
    live x1.5 badge, pause/skip/end controls, today tally
  - Real x1.5 Deep Work ELO bonus while body.pomo-active / window._pomoRunning
  - Focus ledger (jeemax_focus_ledger): blocks done, forfeits, chain, best
    chain, deep seconds; chain pips; quit-friction Break-the-chain confirm;
    stopwatch stops >= 5 min count as completed blocks
  - Session receipts (block length, chain state, deep time, ELO earned during
    block via snapshot diff); commitment projection text
  - Lock-In chamber: huge countdown, bar/beaker visualizer (opt-in beaker),
    setup strip dims while running, today burn strip, all-time per-subject
    pills; display settings modal (stopwatch toggle, blur timer, beaker)
  - Soundscape engine: 10 beds (Rain real dual-layer, Ocean, Stream, Fire,
    Cafe real, Wind, Drone, Brown, Pink, White), Volume/Depth/Brightness/Tone
    knobs, auto-play on lock-in, bell duck (-75%, 1.5 s hold), 1.4 s fade-in,
    0.9/0.55 s crossfades, grain-loop expansion of short recordings
  - Metronome FAB (M hotkey): BPM 30-250, beats 1-7, voices soft/wood/chime,
    TAP tempo, hold-to-repeat steppers, sample-accurate lookahead scheduler
  - Gallery Break ("Burn Reveal"): painting develops from center during breaks,
    radius tracks break progress, bundled quotes, Continue reverse-bloom,
    procedural aurora fallback art, paper-crackle audio
  - Scratchpad HUD: Apple-Pencil-only drawing overlay (perfect-freehand strokes,
    coalesced 240 Hz input, dual canvas, quick colors + palette + stashing,
    draggable toolbar, iOS gesture warfare)

QUESTION BANK & INGESTION (4 pipelines)
  1. Traditional Multicrop: upload screenshots -> Crop Studio (draw boxes,
     mark diagram vs question segments, stitch vertically/side-by-side),
     then OCR grid extraction (columns of 5 stitched into ONE master grid,
     single Gemini call, strict KaTeX prompt, all-or-nothing validation)
  2. Gemini Gem Text Track: paste/upload JSON dump; cleanAndParseJson fast path
     heals bare-backslash corruption; legacy regex path with schema-drift alias
     sanitizer; qElo stamping ([800,2550] -> gem-stamped else uncalibrated);
     anti-cheat audits (elite-share >= 80% + low chapter avg <= 1549 + batch
     >= 3 => suspiciousDistribution; stdev < 15 over >= 20 => suspiciousStdev);
     chapter-ceiling clamp +/-600; LaTeX repair + mathOk flags (never blocking)
  3. Answer Key mapping: image or pasted text; multi-answer arrays sorted;
     type inference mcq/numeric/text
  4. Manual creation + edit modal
  - Gem Diagram Map: imageRef/cropBox auto-crop for diagrams, per-option
    images (optionImages) and solution images (solutionImage), extensive
    aliases, pixel-coordinate auto-detection, one upload per tagged screenshot,
    copy-instructions block for the Gem, bbox overlays on canvas previews
  - Preview modal grid: verify/edit answer keys, add/wipe diagram (surgical
    crop), reopen Diagram Map, save all
  - Dedupe: chapter-independent keys (text >= 12 chars or image fingerprint),
    import-time skip summary, manual purge, silent boot-time auto-purge

PRACTICE & THE ELO ENGINE
  - Grind Station browsing: subject -> chapters (self-healing registry,
    delete-with-tombstones) -> question list with status filters (Untouched/
    Clutched/Fumbled), hit rate (FIRST-attempt accuracy only), avg speed,
    completion bar, lazy images (IntersectionObserver + +/-2000px unload sweep
    + prefetch concurrency 4)
  - Practice modal: MCQ single/multi, numeric (tolerance |u-c| < 1e-6),
    free-response self-report; hide-photo toggle; immersive mode; per-question
    timer frozen at reveal; solution popup with KaTeX; error-reason classifier
    modal (Brain Fade / Calc Fumble / Read Fail)
  - Practice modes: Standard, Flow State (P_win 0.75-0.85), Hardcore/Overclock
    (P_win 0.35-0.50, qElo floor 1800, wins x1.8 / losses x0.6, cap 8/day);
    adaptive picker (strict +/-0.10 window around EMA target, fallback band,
    closest-qElo bridge), adaptive throttle (win +0.06x fast, miss -0.10x),
    skip popover (4 reasons, retire after 3 skips/"already know", undo toast,
    no-regret), mode-rate gating prevents farming SR reviews
  - Bounty Hunt: daily boss fight on a marked-wrong question, 180 s clock;
    win = 3 supercharged payoffs + streak floor 5; loss = 24 h lockout,
    criticalDeficit flag, +5 target tax
  - Elo engine: subject baselines K=12/12/16, six rank tiers (NPC to Unhinged
    Gigachad), calibrated fast path (gem-stamped/learned: logistic P_win,
    tau time-divergence multiplier with FAST/SLOW/BALANCED pills, misfire
    stinginess x1.4, qElo drift K_q=6) and legacy R_perf path (chapter-average
    time, physics 0.85 tau buffer, chemistry slow-correct downgrade, mu_block
    distraction 0.75/1.5, omega_decay from chapter health, delta_error
    exp(-0.4N/15), asymmetric win/loss scaling, disparity filter >400, re-solve
    decay 0.25); global MMR = p-norm harmonic minus 0.15x spread penalty;
    anomaly flag at +/-600 from chapter average; CNS stepped multiplier on
    gains; NightGuard late-night multiplier; Lifeline x0.65; Elo shift chips +
    tier-change emoji bursts (40 emojis) + super sound
  - JEE AIR projection popup: AIR_adv = 10^(8.00-0.00214*Elo), AIR_main =
    10^(8.61-0.00236*Elo) against 250k/1.2M candidate pools

SPACED REPETITION & ERROR VAULT
  - Vault board grouped by due status (Ready / Due soon <=3d / Later /
    Mastered), weakest easeFactor first; status/type pill filters + slash-key
    search hotkey (150 ms debounce) + docked filter echo on scroll
  - Daily Core Queue: per-local-day snapshot, strict ready filter, weakest-EF
    order, caps {physics 5, maths 5, chemistry 10}, per-subject progress rails
  - SR practice drawer: flip/self-report flows, autonomy pills (Independent /
    Hint Used / Soln Read), friction-type multi-pills, stopwatch or manual
    minutes vs target time, hint/image toggles, result freeze, immediate Elo
    call + persisted chip, staggered rebuilds
  - SM-2 variant math: Wf = min friction weight (PERFECT 1.20 ... APPROACH
    0.15), q = clamp(A*3 + max(0, 2 - Rt), 0, 5), EF update floored 1.3
    (correct never lowers EF), intervals 1 -> 3 -> ceil(I*EF*Wf), lapse
    max(1, floor(I*Wf)), mastery = I > 30 && EF > 2.5 && correct
  - Chapter health (Bjork New Theory of Disuse): RS_i = e^(-ln2*dt/S_i),
    accessibility = sum(Q_Elo*RS)/sum(Q_Elo) * 100, clamp [10,100], neutral 50;
    drives Decay Grid tiers (>75 green / 45-75 yellow / <45 red), nav pulse
    (<45 lowest-health), checkpoint lockdown question choice
  - Chapter Progress card (weakest-first, top 10 + overflow footer), history
    panels with attempt dots (last 5 logs reversed)

ACCOUNTABILITY & RECOVERY
  - Checkpoints (Slump Sentry): 4 pillars, configurable times/idle/grace/PoW,
    absolute calendar-anchored grace deadlines, lockdown overlay with lowest-
    health due question + Proof-of-Work timer (drawing/typing activity with
    >4px movement threshold), Protocol Zero penalties scarred into graphs via
    checkpoint:penalty event + localStorage checkpoint:protocolZero, shield
    persistence, control-center hub. NOTE: the 1 s monitoring loop is currently
    REMOVED/deactivated in source (phase stays disarmed) - see 0.5.
  - Night Guard: post-23:00 three-tier ELO-degradation guard (Tier1 x0.80 /
    Tier2 x0.55 auto-dismissing / Tier3 x0.20 force-set after 03:00 with an
    uninterruptible modal + 3-second hold-to-override), sleep-debt mood
    penalty ceiling, session start/end logging, clock-cheat detection
  - CNS Load meter 0-1 gating demanding modes; reset bridge on pomodoro quit
  - Lifeline: CNS >= 0.80 shifts P_win windows easier (fallback widened),
    x0.65 ELO yield on assisted solves, auto-off below 0.40, per-solve opt-out,
    requires >= 3 unsolved questions in the easier band
  - Deload engine: forced/auto eligibility + manual scheduling ("Earned Rest"
    preserves streaks), 48h missed-day checks via full history ledger
  - Mood calibration ("Vibe Check"): sad x0.70 / neutral x1.0 / happy x1.20
    multipliers applied to active targets; sleep-debt ceiling

GAMIFICATION & GROWTH
  - Grove Islands world: growth driven by cumulative study seconds + solve
    difficulty (growSeconds = (5-4d)*3600 s; easy 5h -> tough 1h), ~10% dark
    oaks, maturity clamp01((cum - plantCum)/growSec), daily per-subject stores
    mirrored LS+IDB (jeemax_forest_daily_v1)
  - Streaks: computed from permanent daily ledger; deload continuity rule
    (counts iff previous 6 consecutive days active); 3650-iteration walk cap;
    STREAK AT RISK flash after 18:00 with zero solves
  - In-session pixel-flame streak visualizer: 16x16 char-art frames (yellow
    >=1, blue >=3, purple >=5 correct), particle spawns, cooling recolor chain,
    overheat chaos mode; wrong answers: 20% streak-shield else reset
  - CK loop-rail: TODAYS LOOP rings (P/C/M/fix), LOOP CLOSED state, Vault
    ready-badge, grind/focus beacons, combo + crit-charge + shield economy
    (crit at charge >=100 pays supercharged + shield; every 5th combo step
    grants a shield; miss always resets combo and consumes a shield if held)
  - Overheat: 15% chance on supercharged solves, 5-minute chaos window with
    double-count payout on first win
  - FX catalog: click/soft/confirm/delete/select/tab/modalClose/toggle/blip/
    tick/hover/success synth SFX, ripples, glow overlays, red flash, emoji
    bursts, haptics; settings card with test buttons

DASHBOARD & DATA VIZ
  - Bento cards: Today Progress triple ring (P/C/M arcs + total), Momentum
    candlesticks (cognitive-yield series Y_day = sum of (qElo/1200)*(2/(1+tau))*
    W_vault; target line weighted 50% maths + 30% physics + 20% chemistry;
    trend classes bull-market/under-liquidation; 5-day prediction), GitHub-style
    Daily Variance year heatmap (levels at <0.5/<1/<=1.25/>1.25 vs target),
    Chapter Progress, Retention Health decay grid
  - Bento layout controller v5: drag reorder, width handles (keyboard-accessible
    sliders), height handles with dblclick-fit, density presets (Compact/Cozy/
    Spacious), height-map masonry packer, persistence jeemax_bento_layout
  - Calendar modal (month navigation, day cells from ledger)
  - Candlestick engine shared by momentum charts (inverted green=errors-fell
    variant for the Vault)

CLOUD, SYNC & SETTINGS
  - Google Drive OAuth (drive.file scope), private folder JEEMaxxing_Cloud,
    system_state.json payload (bank minus images, chapters, solved, studySecs,
    elo + eloUpdatedAt last-write-wins, dailyHistory), media uploads Q_<id>.png
    / Diag_<id>.png with lazy fetchMediaFromDrive + Cache All Images,
    tombstones prevent resurrection, 30 s push throttle, 2-minute heartbeat,
    unified sync with cloud-preserve interlock (local 0 vs cloud >0 aborts
    upload), token expiry handling, multi-tab BroadcastChannel merge
  - Settings: username/pfp, daily quotas (targets 10/10/10, fix targets 5/5/5,
    both locked 24h via shared jeeTargetLockDate), Gemini API key + verifier,
    FX toggles, Anti-Slump launcher, Schedule Earned Rest button
  - AI Matrix Dump export: select chapters -> human-readable summary + raw JSON
    .txt download; purge duplicates
  - Boot sequence Daily Briefing (once/day cyberpunk overlay, pre-fills sprint,
    mood fallback), midnight rollover cycle (settle yesterday, zero counters,
    reset CNS/NightGuard/deload checks, briefing), persistence-failure banner

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
0.5  README-vs-CODE CORRECTIONS (verified against source)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The bundled README.md is slightly stale in places. Where they disagree, the
code (documented in the module sections below) is authoritative:

  1. nightguard.js is NOT an "overnight maintenance/flush scheduler". It is the
     post-23:00 three-tier ELO-degradation guard (x0.80/x0.55/x0.20) with a
     sleep-debt ledger and clock-cheat detection. Its resetDaily() is invoked
     externally by the midnight rollover in app.js.
  2. dashboard-clean.js does NOT inject the Time Bank card (grep-verified zero
     matches). It injects banner-hiding body classes (dc-active/dc-dash-active),
     reparents the header cloud cluster, adds the momentum legend, the pencil
     layout FAB adopting .bento-toolbar, and persists jeemax_sidebar_collapsed.
     (A Time Bank CSS layer exists in styles.css from the Dashboard v2 pass.)
  3. checkpoint.js init() has its 1-second monitoring loop DEACTIVATED in the
     current source ("TODAYS LOOP: REMOVED") - the tick interval never starts,
     restoreState() is not invoked at boot, and the phase remains disarmed.
     Stored Protocol Zero penalties survive only as graph history.
  4. The Daily Fix Queue has NO weighted paper-distribution slicing; curation
     is: strict ready status -> weakest easeFactor first -> caps {P:5, M:5,
     C:10}, snapshotted once per local day.
  5. The 15-day error-momentum sparkline was removed from the Vault stage; only
     the rail squashed-today counters remain (the candlestick chart re-chains
     after each render).
  6. index.html contains exactly ONE static canvas (#streak-canvas); all
     forest/grove/scratchpad/gallery canvases are created at runtime by their
     own modules.
  7. storage.js uses ZERO localStorage (all persistence is IndexedDB + Drive);
     matrix.js uses exactly one localStorage key (jeemax_daily_queue_snapshot).
  8. app.js dispatches NO custom events; it listens for exactly one custom
     event (checkpoint:penalty). All other cross-module signaling uses
     window.* function hooks.

  Separate database: "checkpoint-db" (checkpoint.js dual-write state)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
0.7  EVENTS & GLOBAL HOOKS (summary)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Custom events listened : window checkpoint:penalty (re-renders graphs)
  Custom events dispatched: none in app.js (window-hook signaling instead)
  BroadcastChannel ....... jmax-tab-sync (message type jmax-saved)
  Major window bridges ... calculateEloMigration, _getChapterHealth,
                           getRankTierDetails, renderEloMatrix, __ckBumpTodayFix,
                           __jmaxDataDirty, __scratchpad, __cnsLoad, __deload,
                           __lifeline, __nightGuard, __forestGrowth, __pomodoro,
                           _pomoRunning, FocusSound, Metro, FX, BootSequence,
                           GalleryBreak, JEEMaxTheme, __dashClean, __checkpoint
  (Full per-module inventories in the module sections below.)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
0.8  RUNNING / DEVELOPING
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  - Serve the folder over HTTP (native ES modules):
      python3 -m http.server 8000        ->  http://localhost:8000
      npx serve .
  - No build step; node_modules only needed for QA tooling (playwright-core).
  - package.json scripts:
      npm run smoke ....... smoke-answer-resolve + smoke-pomo-config +
                            smoke-boot-sequence + smoke-practice-nav (Playwright)
      npm run qa .......... qa-boot-sequence
      npm run qa-progress . qa-today-progress
      npm run qa-focus .... qa-focus-mode + qa-focus-layout + qa-focus-sound
  - More harnesses in scripts/: redesign-probe/layout/theme/interaction QA,
    qa-grove-shots/-verify, qa-metronome, qa-error-matrix, qa-variance-shots,
    seam/png probes, gen-icons.mjs, gen-ambient-sounds.mjs, analyze-wavs.mjs,
    v2-audit.mjs, loaders/esm-sh-stub.mjs.
  - Service worker registers only on https:// or http://localhost.

- Inline script #2 (lines 5-13, Theme pre-boot): reads localStorage 'jeemax_theme' and 'jeemax_mode' BEFORE first paint; valid themes array ['furnace','synthwave','glacier','overgrowth','bloodmoon','sakura','stealth'], valid modes ['midnight','dusk']; sets data-theme (fallback "furnace") and data-mode (fallback "midnight") on <html>. SECTION 4: SCRIPTS — FILES, ORDER, INLINE LOGIC

Head inline (parse-time, before paint): see Section 1 (FX pre-boot line 4; theme pre-boot lines 5-13).

Body inline, classic (execute immediately during HTML parsing, BEFORE any deferred/module script):
- Lines 1110-1181 — Vault filter glue (defines window.* helpers used by the neon pill UI):
  - window.setMatrixFilter(group, value, el): writes value into hidden carrier #filter-<group>, toggles .active among sibling .matrix-pill within .emf-pill-group, calls window.syncDockedEcho(), then window.filterErrors() if defined.
  - window.syncDockedEcho(): reflects the one active non-"all" filter chip onto .error-filters[data-active="<labels joined ' · '>"] so the docked/collapsed bar explains a shortened list while scrolled; removes attribute when everything is "all".
  - window.setMatrixSearch(value): writes #filter-tag, unhides #matrix-search-clear, debounces filterErrors() 150 ms trailing via window.__matrixSearchDebounce (perf fix for O(N) card scans on mobile).
  - window.clearMatrixSearch(): clears #matrix-search-input + #filter-tag, hides clear button, runs filterErrors().
  - window.clearMatrixFilters(): resets #filter-status/#filter-type to "all" and #filter-tag to "", re-activates the "all" pills, then clearMatrixSearch() + syncDockedEcho() (zero-result escape hatch).

External scripts, in document order (defer classics and type="module" both execute after parsing, in this order, before DOMContentLoaded handlers queued later):
1. <script defer src="./vendor/katex/katex.min.js" onerror="window.__katexLoadFailed=true;"> — vendored KaTeX; failure flag lets app.js inject a CDN fallback. Must precede app module.

3. <script type="module" src="./app.js"> — main controller module; imports storage / pomodoro / matrix / candlestick engines.

11. <script type="module"> INLINE (lines 1212-1560) — BENTO RESIZE CONTROLLER v5 (replaces v4; runs after app.js). Summary:
    - Constants: LS 'jeemax_bento_layout'; breakpoints BP=901 (masonry on/off), BP2=1101 (2-col ↔ 1-col default); V_MIN 140/V_MAX 1600 px heights; COLS=12; card IDS ['tracker','variance','momentum','progress','decay'] mapped to classes .dash-card-<key>.

Safety: drag move/release bound to WINDOW; blur + visibilitychange(forceEndAllDrags) + beforeunload(save); re-asserts after paint (double rAF + 160 ms timeout), on load, on matchMedia changes at 901/1101 px, and on resize; MutationObserver on #view-dashboard class repacks when the tab becomes visible.

SECTION 5: CANVAS ELEMENTS

- Exactly ONE static <canvas> in the whole file: canvas#streak-canvas, explicit width="16" height="16", inside div#streak-visualizer in the practice modal header, next to #streak-number and #elo-header-slot (streak sparkline/ring for the practice runner). Owning module is not annotated in the HTML (it belongs to the practice flow driven through app.js).
- No other canvas elements exist statically. The "live forest" visuals have NO canvas markup here — forest-bg.js and grove-islands.js (both deferred) create/manage their own layers at runtime.
- Chart surfaces are SVG, not canvas: svg#dynamic-graph (momentum chart, preserveAspectRatio="none", no width/height attributes — CSS-sized), the sprint ring svg.mini-ring (viewBox 0 0 36 36, circles r=15.5), and the dashboard triple ring svg (viewBox 0 0 120 120, arcs #tp-arc-physics r=50 / #tp-arc-chemistry r=40 / #tp-arc-maths r=30).

SECTION 6: INLINE SVG ICONS / TEMPLATE BLOCKS

- There is NO <symbol>/<use> sprite sheet and ZERO <template> elements in the file. Every icon is an individual inline <svg> with viewBox="0 0 24 24", fill="none", stroke="currentColor", stroke-linecap/linejoin round, mostly stroke-width 1.75 (nav menu, header chips, action icons; logo bolt uses 2.1, manual-sync 1.9) so glyphs follow theme ink.
- Icon subjects present: dashboard grid (4 rects), lightning bolt (logo + Focus Mode nav), shield (Vault nav), open book (Grind Station nav), sliders (Config nav), chevron-left (sidebar collapse), flame (streak chip), clock circle (focus chip), calendar rect, refresh arc (sync), search magnifier context (vault search styling), etc.
- Data-URI SVGs used as assets: favicon (⚡ text glyph, line 28) and the default profile avatar (64×64 dark rect with 🦊, line 156).
- Emoji glyphs are used extensively as functional icons (folder icons 🌌🧪📐, beaker/timer UI, subject cards, AI Dump 🧠, bounty ⚔️, night guard 🛌).

SECTION 7: ACCESSIBILITY ATTRIBUTES PRESENT

Roles:
- role="dialog" — #pomo-pop (Sprint setup popover)
- role="list" — ul.nav-menu (sidebar nav)
- role="img" + descriptive aria-label — .tp-ring (daily solved vs combined target ring) and #daily-variance-grid (per-day contribution grid)
- role="status" — .rail-today ("Bugs squashed today") and #matrix-meta (which additionally has aria-live="polite")
- Dynamically applied: aria-current="page" set/removed on the active .nav-item by the view-identity inline script (no static occurrences).

aria-label (named controls, ~40 instances): widget controls (Collapse widget, Start sprint, Pause or resume sprint, Skip break, End sprint), popover (#pomo-pop "Sprint setup"; #pop-dynamic "Toggle dynamic subject mode"; Decrease/Increase minutes by 5; Decrease/Increase rounds), Collapse sidebar, Open calendar, Force cloud sync, Timer display settings, Decrement/Increment physics|chemistry|maths counters, #chain-pips "Deep work chain", #sc-power "Play soundscape", soundscape ranges (#sc-volume/#sc-depth/#sc-brightness/#sc-density), vault nav "Error subjects", #matrix-search-input "Search the error matrix by chapter or tag", #matrix-search-clear "Clear search", Previous month / Next month, #text-add-terminal "Gem text dump JSON input", #new-chapter-input "New chapter name", every .close-btn "Close" (practice variant "Close practice").

aria-hidden="true" (decorative): mini-ring svg, .logo-mark, header chip icon spans (.chip-streak/.chip-time), tp-ring svg, daily-variance legend, #daily-variance-months, #emf-scroll-sentinel, .setup-divider.

Other a11y-relevant facts: native <label for> bindings on pomo-subject/pomo-study/pomo-break/pomo-sessions, set-username, set-pfp, all six quota inputs, gemini-key, err-img-input, upload-images, sc-auto, json-dump-file (nested label), answer-key-image; img alt texts on #display-pfp ("Profile"), #solution-image ("Solution diagram"), #lightbox-img ("Question full view"); #stopwatch-toggle-btn is a documented programmatic fallback target; the bento controller marks its injected resize handles role="slider" with aria-labels "Resize card width"/"Resize card height" and keyboard ArrowLeft/ArrowRight width stepping. Notably absent: focus traps on modals, aria-modal, tabindex management on overlays, and heading-level landmarks beyond h1/h2 usage.

1) DESIGN TOKENS — :root "DESIGN TOKENS v4" block (lines 6–108)
=====================================================================

Base surfaces:
- --bg-base: #08090d
- --bg-raise: #0d1017
- --bg-inset: #0a0c11
- --bg-surface: rgba(15, 19, 27, 0.72)
- --bg-surface-hover: rgba(24, 29, 41, 0.85)

Ink:
- --text-primary: #eef1f6

- --text-muted: #5b6478

Structural lines:
- --line: rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.10)
- --line-strong: rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.20)

Accent system (multi-channel, semantic):
- --furnace: #ffb224 (primary "grind energy")
- --furnace-hot: #ff7a1a
- --telemetry: #3ddcff (data/charts/focus rings)
- --vault: #a78bfa (SR/memory systems)
- Status colors: --glow-green #22c55e, --glow-red #f87171, --glow-yellow #eab308, --glow-orange #f97316
- --teal: #2dd4bf
- Gradients: --grad-furnace linear-gradient(100deg, #ffb224, #ff7a1a); --grad-flow linear-gradient(90deg, #ffb224, #ff7a1a, #3ddcff, #ffb224)

Accent aliases ("components consume THESE, never a hardcoded rgba(255,178,36,…)") so all seven themes re-skin:
- --accent: var(--furnace); --accent-2: var(--furnace-hot)
- Alpha ladder built with color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) N%, transparent): --accent-a3 8%, --accent-a6 14%, --accent-a10 22%, --accent-a18 36%, --accent-a30 55%
- Per-theme extras defined in the theme engine (consumed elsewhere with fallbacks): --accent-soft, --accent-ink, --aes-amber/--aes-amber2/--aes-cyan/--aes-violet, --aur-1/--aur-2/--aur-3, plus --aes-glass/--aes-line/--aes-ink/--aes-dim/--aes-display

Type scale:
- Fonts: --font-display 'Chakra Petch', sans-serif; --font-body 'IBM Plex Sans', system-ui, sans-serif; --font-num 'Space Grotesk', monospace
- --fs-2xs 9.5px (micro caps/kickers), --fs-xs 11px (captions/meta), --fs-sm 12.5px (secondary body), --fs-base 13.5px (body), --fs-md 15px (emphasized body), --fs-lg 17px (sub-heads), --fs-xl 20px (card titles), --fs-2xl clamp(22px, 2.2vw, 27px) (page titles)

Space scale (4px base):
- --sp-1 4px, --sp-2 8px, --sp-3 12px, --sp-4 16px, --sp-5 20px, --sp-6 24px, --sp-7 32px, --sp-8 40px

Radius scale:
- --r-sm 8px (chips/small controls), --r-md 10px (buttons/inputs), --r-lg 14px (inner rows/nested cards), --r-xl 20px (outer cards/modals), --r-full 999px (pills/dots)

Elevation (tinted to base hue, never pure black):
- --e-1: 0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,.35), 0 4px 14px -8px rgba(0,0,0,.5)
- --e-2: 0 2px 6px -2px rgba(0,0,0,.45), 0 14px 34px -18px rgba(0,0,0,.62)
- --e-3: 0 4px 12px -4px rgba(0,0,0,.5), 0 28px 64px -24px rgba(0,0,0,.72)

3) APPEARANCE MODES — [data-mode]
=====================================================================

Only one non-default mode exists: data-mode="dusk" (verified — distinct attribute value set = {"dusk"}). Default appearance is referred to in comments as "Midnight"/"midnight glass" and needs no attribute. Dusk is described as an "evening" appearance (~65% dark, warm ivory, heavy glass), orthogonal to accent themes ([data-mode] drives surfaces/ink/lines; [data-theme] keeps driving accents — all 7 themes work in both modes).

html[data-mode="dusk"] token retune (lines 5367–5382):
- --bg-base #262a3c, --bg-raise #2f3449, --bg-inset #202434
- --bg-surface rgba(255,250,240,0.075), --bg-surface-hover rgba(255,250,240,0.13)
- --text-primary #f5f0e4, --text-secondary #bcb5a6, --text-muted #837d70
- --line rgba(255,246,228,0.13), --line-strong rgba(255,246,228,0.24)
- --aes-ink #f8f3e8, --aes-dim #b8b1a2, --aes-line rgba(255,246,228,0.16), --aes-glass linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(255,250,240,0.14), rgba(255,250,240,0.055))

What Dusk changes:
- body::before becomes an evening sky: noise tile + theme auroras + warm horizon radial rgba(255,190,120,0.13) + linear-gradient(180deg, #2c3049, #272b40 48%, #21253a), background-attachment fixed
- .bg-grid-overlay grid re-tinted ivory rgba(255,246,228,0.05)
- Warm scrollbars: scrollbar-color rgba(255,205,130,0.42); webkit thumb gradient rgba(255,210,140,0.5) → rgba(255,160,90,0.4)
- Sidebar becomes brighter frosted rail (ivory gradient + blur(26px) saturate(165%) + inset hairline)
- Dashboard "glass turned all the way up": .dash-card uses var(--aes-glass) + blur(22px) saturate(155%); tracker card keeps conic-gradient border-box frame; modals/drawers/loading become floating glass slabs (blur 18–24px); inputs/terminal darken to rgba(16,18,32,0.5)-style fills
- Follow-on passes: "DUSK · TRUE-GLASS" (8611, every dashboard box transparent), "DUSK · MONOCHROME" pomodoro tab (8766, no colours, only transparency), v4-chrome Dusk compat (10936: warmer sidebar/icon-btn/nav hovers rgba(255,250,240,…)), and Dusk parity fixes for kickers (11178)

Glass / backdrop-filter:
- 97 backdrop-filter mentions (~48 paired rules incl. -webkit prefixes); blur range 3px–28px, frequently paired with saturate(125%–170%). Heavy users: Premium pass cards blur(18px) saturate(130%); theme panel blur(22px) saturate(150%); Dusk sidebar blur(26px) saturate(165%) and modals blur(24px)/drawers blur(22px); loading overlay blur(6px); scratchpad toolbar blur(14px)
- Graceful degradation: @supports not ((backdrop-filter…) or (-webkit-backdrop-filter…)) opaque fallback at line 5075
- Drag-performance layer: while a bento card is the live drag target backdrop-filter is forced to none !important (line 4776) to keep dragging smooth, then restored after drop via a higher-specificity selector (comment at 4816 explains the specificity math)

Canvas overlays (all styled in CSS, driven by JS):
- #streak-canvas — streak-flame ring visualizer (32px in drawer, 38px in practice modal)

- gi-* Grove Islands WebGL canvases, with a static fallback poster (.gi-fallback) shown when WebGL/the 3D engine is unavailable

Decorative background stack:
- body::before layered: inline SVG feTurbulence fractal-noise tile (opacity ~0.035) + 3 theme-driven radial auroras (--aur-1/2/3) + deep radial vignette; swapped wholesale in Dusk for the indigo sky + warm horizon
- .bg-grid-overlay 52px engineering grid masked by radial-gradient ellipse; two drifting .ambient-glow orbs (640px radial glows, will-change: transform)

Animated conic borders:
- Registered @property --aes-angle animated by aesSpin drives conic-gradient(from var(--aes-angle), furnace→telemetry→vault→furnace) border-box frames on the tracker/time cards; deliberately calmed or disabled in some passes (Time Bank under wallpaper, "perpetual conic rotation reads as noise", re-armed when a session runs via body.pomo-active)

Scrollbars:
- Base: thin + transparent track, #242b3a thumb, 8px radius, bg-colored border (lines 146–151); v4 override restyles to rgba(148,163,184,.26)/.45 hover, var(--r-full) radius, background-clip padding-box with transparent border (10498–10506); Dusk swaps to warm amber gradient thumbs

Selection & focus:
- ::selection furnace background with dark accent-ink text (twice-defined, theme-aware in v4); :focus-visible telemetry 2px outline early, replaced in v4 by var(--ring-focus) double-ring box-shadow; nav/badge/button focus outlines suppressed in favor of the ring

B. initApp() async order (L8392):
1. registerUiCallbacks({lockTargetsOnly, updateUI, updateStudyTimeHeader (dynamic-imports pomodoro.js), renderGraph (gated to active #view-dashboard), renderErrorMatrixFromBank (gated to active #view-errors)}) — view-gating prevents per-solve hidden-DOM rebuilds.
2. await loadDataAsync().
3. await restoreDailyForestFromIDB() — merges IDB copy of jeemax_forest_daily_v1 into localStorage (max-per-field merge), then restoreDailyCountsIntoSolved().
4. _autoPurgeDuplicateQuestions() — one-time silent cross-chapter duplicate cleanup (keeps first copy).
5. idbGet('jeeTargetLockDate'); if age < 1 day → lockTargetsOnly().
6. Fill #set-tgt-phys/#set-tgt-chem/#set-tgt-math from baseTargets.
7. Load error targets: idbGet('baseErrPhys'/'baseErrChem'/'baseErrMath') ?? 5 → baseErrorTargets + fill #set-err-* inputs.
8. Day rollover gate: todayStr = todayLocalKey(); lastSettled = await _readLastSettledDay() (IDB jeemax_last_settled_date → localStorage mirror → legacy jeemax_boot_seq_date). If lastSettled !== todayStr → await runNewDayCycle(todayStr); else activeTargets = round(baseTargets × moodMultiplier) per subject.
9. restoreDailyCountsIntoSolved(); await saveAllAsync(); startMidnightRolloverWatcher().
10. Hide #vis-beaker, show #vis-bar; write #top-date as "D Mon YYYY".
11. await renderGraph(); updateUI().
12. assignDailyBountyIfNeeded(); refreshBountyRail().
13. applyPomoConfig() then resetPomoUI(); updateStreakVisualizer().
14. renderEloMatrix().
15. renderErrorResolutionDashboard(); renderMomentumCandles().
16. window.addEventListener('checkpoint:penalty', …) → re-renders graph + resolution dashboard + momentum candles.
17. try { await initDrive(); } catch — Drive failure must never kill boot (console.warn '[initApp] Drive init skipped').
18. window.BootSequence.maybeShow() (self-gated Daily Briefing overlay; internal key jeemax_boot_seq_date; falls back conceptually to mood-modal inside runNewDayCycle only).

C. runNewDayCycle(todayStr): DeloadEngine.isForcedDeloadEligible()→triggerForcedDeload(); snapshotCumDayStart() (BEFORE zeroing); CNSLoad.resetDaily(); NightGuard.resetDaily(); settleDayCounters(yesterdayKey) (folds 23:59:59 solves into the ledger); zeroes solved.{physics,chemistry,maths} and studySecs.{physics,chemistry,maths}; saveAllAsync; _markDaySettled(todayStr) (LS+IDB jeemax_last_settled_date) ONLY after commit; updateUI(); BootSequence.maybeShow() with fallback openModal('mood-modal').
D. Midnight watcher: startMidnightRolloverWatcher — setInterval(checkRollover, 30000) comparing todayLocalKey(), plus visibilitychange(!hidden) and window focus triggers.

- calibrateMood(mood): sad→moodMultiplier 0.70, happy→1.20, else 1.0; NightGuard.getSleepDebtMoodPenalty() applied as ceiling (<1.0 min-clamps); mood 1.0 calls DeloadEngine.overrideForcedDeload(1.0); activeTargets = round(baseTargets × multiplier) per subject; idbSet jeemax_mood_multiplier + jeemax_last_calibrated_date (= plain timestamp, NOT the rollover gate anymore); closes mood-modal, renderGraph, resetPomoUI, rewrites #top-date.
- Favicon generation (IIFE above).
- Practice-image lightbox: window.openPracticeImageLightbox(src) — overlay #practice-image-lightbox (z-index 1000000, blur backdrop), pinch-zoom scale clamped [0.75, 8], single-pointer drag pan via Pointer Events, ✕ close button.

- updateUI(): per subject writes #{sub}-count, #{tgt-p/c/m}-lbl ("/ N"), #{sub}-bar width%; persistDailyCountsFromSolved(); sleep-debt penalty ceiling (NightGuard.getSleepDebtMoodPenalty()<1.0 → moodMultiplier=min(current,penalty)); variance strip #variance-val "(+/-)x.x%" green var(--glow-green)/red var(--glow-red); Today's Progress ring arcs #tp-arc-physics/#tp-arc-chemistry/#tp-arc-maths (stroke-dasharray from arc.r.baseVal circumference, dashoffset by clamped pct), hub #tp-total, #tp-total-tgt "/ N"; heatmap + Elo matrix/decay grid/chapter progress gated to visible #view-dashboard; debounced updateStreakDisplay via window.__streakRefreshTimer 800ms.

- Candlestick momentum graph renderGraph(): source getDailyHistory(); Protocol Zero hard-zero overlay reading localStorage checkpoint:protocolZero (array of dates; corrupt → removeItem + warn) passed as penaltyFlags; series = friction-inverse cognitive yield Y_day per day: granular _computeYieldForDate(date) matches solved bank questions whose lastReviewedAt.slice(0,10) === date; W_i = (qElo/1200) × (2/(1+τ)) × W_vault where τ = timeTaken/chapterAvgTime (untimed → τ=1 neutral) and W_vault = 0.25 when q.errorReason && q.firstAttemptResult (vault re-attempt) else 1.0; legacy days imputed count × C_macro where C_macro = Σ w_s·β_s·(Q̄_s/1200) (returns 1 on empty bank); target line targetYield = 0.50·maths + 0.30·physics + 0.20·chemistry of baseTargets; drawCandlesticks(#dynamic-graph, counts, {width:360,height:170,showPrediction:true,predDays:5,valueLabel:'Yield Points',valuePrecision:2,labelFn:"Mon 12"}); trend classes on #view-dashboard: slope < −0.1 → 'trend-under-liquidation'; slope > 0.1 && r² > 0.7 → 'trend-bull-market' + svg class 'graph-bull-run'; else cleared.
- renderMomentumCandles(): rAF-deferred after matrix.js dashboard render; extractCountsFromSvg(#error-momentum-svg-container); rebuilds compact candlestick SVG (height 70, width max(clientWidth,240), invert:true — green = errors fell); labelFn "Day N"; updates #erm-avg-label "avg X.X/day".
- Elo Matrix UI: renderEloMatrix() → _renderGlobalMmrRow (injects #global-mmr-row under .user-profile; badge "{icon} {rounded} ELO"; click → AIR popup), _renderSubjectEloMonitor per .compact-subject-card (h4 text match physics/chemistry/math(s); injects .elo-monitor "{icon} {elo}" + tier name into .tp-row-top before .tp-count, legacy fallback after .distribution-pill; data-tooltip "{elo} {tier} · N pts to {nextTier}" or "Peak tier hit"), _applyDeficitLockdown: if min(EP,EC,EM)/max < 0.65 → #view-dashboard class deficit-lockdown-active + .lowest-subject-pulse on weakest card; else removed.

--- 2.3 STREAK SYSTEM ---
- Daily streak updateStreakDisplay(): activeDates from ledger entries count>0; deload-day continuity — manualDeloads + non-overridden forcedDeloads count as active iff all previous 6 consecutive calendar days have solves; backward walk capped at 3650 iterations (10-year runaway guard; old hard cap of 30 removed — comment documents frozen "30 Days"); today-not-active → start from yesterday; writes #top-streak "{n} Day(s)" or "0 Days (start something)".
- In-session correct streak: AppState.practiceCorrectStreak drives pixel-fire visualizer; getConfigForStreak: ≥5 purple, ≥3 blue, ≥1 yellow, else null; canvases #streak-canvas (permanent in #practice-modal) and #sr-streak-canvas (matrix.js drawer), resolved per frame via _resolveActiveStreakCanvas (offsetParent check); 16×16 character-art frame arrays (YELLOW_FRAMES 12 frames, BLUE_FRAMES 10, PURPLE_FRAMES 10; palette letters D/R/O/Y/W mapped per-config hex colors); intensities array [0.62,0.70,0.66,0.76,0.82,1.00,0.84,0.90,0.68,0.62]; frame advance delay 160ms (50ms during overheatChaos); intensity smoothing 0.3 old + 0.7 target; CSS filter = 3-layer drop-shadow chain scaled by intensity; spawnParticles 0–3/frame (×3 chaos), color roll thresholds normal W<6%/Y<40%/O<75% else R (chaos W<30%/Y<70% else O), life 10–32 ticks; cooling recolor chain R→D<15%, O→R<30%, Y→O<45%, W→Y<55%; loop self-stops when no visible canvas or document.hidden (battery); restarted by MutationObserver + visibilitychange; canvas context options {alpha:true, desynchronized:true, willReadFrequently:false}.
- Wrong answer: 20% chance triggerStreakShield (span.streak-shield 🛡️ appended to #streak-visualizer or #sr-streak-visualizer; white-noise burst 0.15s, gain 0.15→0.001 through shared AudioContext) else practiceCorrectStreak=0; always sets AppState._ckComboBreak=true (CK engine consumes).
- updateStreakVisualizer(): writes #streak-number.

--- 2.5 INGESTION PIPELINE 1: MANUAL MULTI-CROP ---
- startManualCrop(): files #upload-images (alert "Select at least one image c'mon"), API key #gemini-key persisted idbSet gemini_api_key; #upload-progress bar + #upload-status-text; readFileAsBase64 all; opens #crop-modal synchronously BEFORE initCropSession (layout defense), then closeModalStr upload-modal. #upload-images change listener writes #file-selected-text "N files selected".

Click □ inside a box to mark it as a diagram."; buttons #crop-redraw (visible when segments exist), #crop-confirm-question ('✓ Lock Question'), #crop-next-question, #crop-finish visibility choreography.
- Drawing: mouse (onmousedown/move/up/leave) + touch (touchstart/move/end, preventDefault) per canvas; tap inside an 18×18 toggle button at rect origin flips seg.isDiagram (isInsideToggleButton); startDraw/draw (dashed live box, fill rgba(59,130,246,0.15))/endDraw (rejects boxes <5×5 px; converts canvas coords → normalized bbox via natural-size scale factors; cropImageFromBBox); redrawAllRectangles paints segments + Q/D toggle chips (roundRect with plain-rect fallback for old iPad Safari).
- confirmMultiCropQuestion(): requires ≥1 segment and ≥1 question (Q) segment; stitchSegmentsVertically(questionSegs) (white-background vertical PNG stitch; onerror resolves null — no spinner hang); diagram segs stitched separately; combineImagesSideBySide(questionStitched, diagramStitched) (side-by-side white canvas; decode failures tolerated); stores _cq.stitchedImage/questionOnly; advances button states.
- nextQuestionInSession()/finishAllQuestions(): finish maps locked questions → AppState.extractedItems items {imageDataUrl, questionOnlyDataUrl, diagramImageUrl:null, extractedText:"", options:[], correctAnswer:"", type:"text", timeTaken:0, solution:"", hint:"", qElo:_computeDefaultQEloForCurrentChapter(), isAnomaly:false}; failed stitches counted and alerted ("N locked question(s) could not be stitched"); resets cropSession; closeCropModal(); showPreviewModal().
- cancelCropSession(): confirm 'Nuke all crops? No going back.'; wipes session + extractedItems.
- closeCropModal(): detaches all canvas listeners, fades out 300ms, and if surgical mode was active restores the preview modal (Bug 1 fix).
- Surgical single-crop mode: window.triggerSurgicalDiagramUpload(index) creates a detached-but-DOM-attached <input type=file accept=image/*> (comment: orphaned input can be GC'd before picker returns), reads file → seeds cropSession {surgicalTargetIdx:index, sourceImages:[{id:0,...}]}; forceHideModal preview; opens crop-modal; refreshCropUI swaps instruction to "Surgical Crop: Draw a single box around the diagram to bind it to Question N." and hides the entire multi-crop button row; endDraw bypass assigns croppedDataUrl directly to AppState.extractedItems[index].diagramImageUrl (bounds-guarded), tears down listeners, forceHideModal crop-modal, showPreviewModal(). window.yeetSurgicalDiagram(index) nulls diagramImageUrl + re-renders preview.

--- 2.6 INGESTION 2: OCR GRID EXTRACTION ---
- extractTextForAll() "Grid Sheet Matrix Edition": pending = extractedItems without extractedText (alerts if none/all done); groups into columns of COLUMN_SIZE=5; stitches each column vertically concurrently; merges columns side-by-side into ONE master white grid canvas; dispatches exactly ONE callGeminiWithFallback(apiKey, prompt, masterGridImage,'image/png',null,true) with strict KaTeX tokenization prompt ($ inline / $$ display, double-escaped backslashes, banned raw unicode glyphs ≠≤≥±×∞≡→, unit formatting $3 \\times 10^8 ~ \\text{m/s}$); response MUST be a JSON array of exactly pendingItems.length objects else aborts with NO partial state write; hydration: repairLatex on text, option objects coerced via o.text ?? o.label ?? o.value, type = options.length ? 'mcq':'text'; mathOk belt-and-suspenders flags latexRepairFailed (never blocks); hideLoading; showPreviewModal; alert "Text extracted and stored."

--- 2.7 INGESTION 3: ANSWER KEY MAPPING ---
- processAnswerKey(): file #answer-key-image; guards (key present, buffer non-empty, all items extracted); prompt includes per-item "Target ID: i\nContent: ..." context; CRITICAL JSON escaping rule for LaTeX backslashes; MULTI-ANSWER rule: multi-correct → sorted array ["A","C"], single → string; parses via cleanAndParseJson; per item: arrays deduped/uppercased/sorted; repairLatex applied to answers + solution; mathOk → latexRepairFailed; type inference: Array→mcq; /^[A-D]$/i + has options→mcq; ^-?\d+(\.\d+)?$→numeric; else text; alert + preview.
- processAnswerKeyFromText(): identical flow fed from textarea #answer-key-text, no image payload.

--- 2.8 INGESTION 4: GEMINI GEM TEXT TRACK (JSON dump ingestion) ---
- switchIngestionTrack('multicrop'|'texttrack'): toggles panels #ingestion-panel-multicrop/#ingestion-panel-texttrack and buttons #toggle-multicrop/#toggle-texttrack.
- window.loadJsonDumpFile(input): FileReader → fills #text-add-terminal → auto-runs processGemTextDump().
- sanitizeGemTextDump(raw) (legacy-path sanitizer): schema-drift alias renames via regex — text aliases ['text','question','stem','body','problem']→extractedText; answer aliases ['answer','correctOption','correct','sol']→correctAnswer; solution aliases ['explanation','reasoning','work','derivation']→solution; hint aliases ['clue','tip','nudge']→hint; console.warn drift report "[sanitizeGemTextDump] Schema drift detected..." mapping each rename; "(B)"→"B" parenthetical unwrap on correctAnswer (single uppercase letter or digit only); Step 1/2/2b quote-escape repairs for extractedText/solution/hint using GENERIC next-key lookahead (arbitrary `"key":` terminators — earlier whitelists silently dropped items when Gem payloads ordered fields differently); Step 3 option-entry quote repair (next-option lookahead without trailing quote); Step 4 backslash-run normalization to exactly \\ skipping canonical key tokens (extractedText/options/correctAnswer/type/solution/mcq/numeric/text).
- _fixBackslashRuns(s): halves backslash PAIRS only (\\\\→\\ preserving align row separators), then converts \n NOT followed by a word char into real newlines (protects \nu \neq \ne \neg \nabla \notin — comment documents the old blanket replacement destroying them).
- Real-JSON fast path: cleanAndParseJson accepts top-level array or {questions|items|data:[...]}; _compileDumpObject maps field aliases (same lists as above), repairs via _repairLatexParsed (collapses leftover `\\frac`-style artifacts before macro-letter/{ then deterministic repairLatex), validates qElo ∈ [800,2550] → gem-stamped (else chapter-average fallback, qEloSource 'uncalibrated'), targetTimeMins ∈ (0,60] else _eloBandTargetTime(qElo), tags ≤5 strings, model string sliced to 64 chars, difficulty passthrough string, gemSubject normalized, gemChapter sanitizeChapterName (trim, slice 64, null when empty); parses gemImage/_parseGemOptionImages/_parseGemSolutionImage.
- Legacy regex path (only when JSON parse fails): splits on `"extractedText"\s*:\s*"` anchor; recovers leading metadata from previous segment tail after last '{' (fields emitted before the anchor were previously dropped); extracts options array strings, correctAnswer (string or bracket array), type (lowercased, e.g. "Subjective"→subjective), solution (generic terminator lookahead); synthetic rawQ built via _extractStringField/_extractNumberField/_extractStringArrayField (id/subject/chapter/qElo/targetTimeMins/difficulty/tags/model/hint); same qElo stamping ranges ([800,2550]); legacyGemImage = _compileLegacyGemImageRef(metadata) regex equivalent of the ref parser (per-option/solution refs parsed on fast path only).
- Anti-cheat audits: _auditGemBatchByChapter(items) buckets by subject|chapter (preferring gem provenance stamps; trim+toString normalization documented as MUST-match with consumer), computes eliteShare (qElo ≥2100 share) and population stdDev; flags suspiciousDistribution when eliteShare ≥ 0.80 AND prior chapter avg ≤1549 AND batch ≥3; suspiciousStdev when ≥20 entries and stdDev < 15 ("automation fingerprint"). Results stamped per item as stampBatchSuspiciousDistribution / stampBatchSuspiciousStdev (persisted onto bank objects).
- Chapter-ceiling clamp (DESTINATION chapter, not gem source): priorAvg=_getChapterAvgElo(subject,chapter); |qElo−priorAvg|>600 → qElo = round(priorAvg + 600·sign(delta)) and tag 'over-chapter-ceiling' appended.
- Post-parse: mathOk flags latexRepairFailed per item (console.warn '[latex-repair] gem segment failed post-repair'); AppState.extractedItems=parsedItems; _gemImageSources={} (fresh map); forceHideModal('upload-modal'); if any tagged refs → alert listing tags + openGemImageMappingModal(), else alert + showPreviewModal(). Errors alert "Ingestion failed: ...".

Re-exposed: window.processGemTextDump/window.showPreviewModal.

--- 2.10 GEM DIAGRAM AUTO-CROP MAP MODAL ---
- Alias tables (top-level consts): primary tags ['imageRef','imageTag','diagramRef','imgTag','sourceImage']; extended tags ['image','img','figure','figRef','sourceImg','imageNumber','figureNumber','imageIndex'] accepted ONLY when _looksLikeGemTag (short ≤24-char single token, no data:/http:/blob:/file:, no whitespace); bbox aliases ['cropBox','bbox','box','crop','region','coords','cropCoords'].
- _parseGemBbox accepts object ({x,y,w,h}/{left,top,width,height}/{x1,y1,x2,y2}/originX/Y) or flat array [x,y,w,h]; rejects zero-area/unparseable.
- _parseGemOptionImages: sources ['optionImages','optionImageRefs','optionsImages','optionsImageRefs','optionsImg']; array form [{option|opt|label|key|letter, imageRef, cropBox,...}] or object form {"A": {"imageRef","cropBox"} | "a1" shorthand (guarded by tag heuristic)}; keys normalized "A) 10 m"→"A"; FIRST entry wins per letter.
- _parseGemSolutionImage: keys ['solutionImage','solImage','solutionImageRef','solutionRef','answerImage'] (object or bare tag) with standalone bbox fallback ['solutionCrop','solutionCropBox','solCropBox','answerCrop'] (+ 'solutionBox()' listed in code's alias array).
- _gemTagEntries(item): unified {kind:'diagram'|'option'(with opt letter)|'solution', tag, bbox} list; _collectGemImageTags returns distinct tags in first-appearance order.
- _resolveGemBbox(bbox,imgW,imgH): treats coordinates as PIXELS only when NOT all within [-0.25,1.25] AND x,y≥0 AND w,h≥1 (auto-detect; sloppy normalized boxes like -0.02/1.05 stay fractions); divides by dims, clamps into unit square (x,y ≤0.9999; w≤1−x), rejects crops <0.001.
- GEM_MAP_INSTRUCTION_BLOCK: verbatim instruction text pasted into the Gemini Gem — two-phase workflow (PHASE 1 map pass scratch work; PHASE 2 emit values VERBATIM), CASE 1 no figure (omit fields), CASE 2 exactly one figure (stem imageRef+cropBox / option optionImages{"B":{...}} / solution solutionImage), CASE 3 ≥2 figures → ONE whole-question crop box (no optionImages), tagging a1,a2,…/s1,s2,… in upload order, fraction coordinates 0..1 top-left anchored, 4x4 sanity grid, outward rounding ~2-3%, keep x+w≤1/y+h≤1, example boxes.
- openGemImageMappingModal(): counts distinct tags; intro #gem-map-intro "The dump references N tagged source images: a1, a2..."; per-tag row .gem-map-row: chip .gem-map-tagchip (title lists referencing question numbers), upload label/input #gem-img-{idKey} (idKey = sanitized tag) + status span #gem-img-name-{idKey} ("No file yet — N questions reference this image (diagrams + option images...)"), preview #gem-map-preview-{idKey} with canvas #gem-map-canvas-{idKey} + caption legend (Q# diagram · ·A option · ·SOL solution); reopened sessions show "✔ Locked (kept in session)" + redraw overlay; forceHideModal('preview-modal') FIRST (z-order fix); instruction text into #gem-map-instruction-text; status #gem-image-map-status reset; openModal('gem-image-map-modal').
- handleGemImageFileSelect(tag,input): readFileAsBase64 → cached in module memory _gemImageSources[normalizedTag] (in-session only, never quota) → nameEl "✔ Locked: filename" → _drawGemBboxOverlay(tag).
- _drawGemBboxOverlay: draws image on canvas capped MAX_W=340/MAX_H=480, overlays EVERY crop region for that tag with kind colors diagram #22d3ee / option #facc15 / solution #e879f9, labels "#n", "#n·<opt>", "#n·SOL".
- applyGemImageCrops("enter"): jobs = every (item, tagged-ref) pair; showLoading; per job resolves dims (cached per tag), bbox, cropImageFromBBox; assignment: diagram → it.diagramImageUrl + it._gemAutoMapped=true; option → it.optionImageUrls[opt]; solution → it.solutionImageUrl; tallies counts/totals, collects missingTags and failed count; status line "✓ Auto-mapped d/d diagrams, o/o option images, s/s solution images." (+missing/failed notes) with class gem-map-status-ok/warn; setTimeout 1100ms → forceHideModal + showPreviewModal. skipGemImageMapping() skips straight through. window.openGemImageMappingModal/skipGemImageMapping/applyGemImageCrops/copyGemImageInstructions bridged (copy uses navigator.clipboard with textarea execCommand fallback + "✔ Copied!" flash on #btn-copy-gem-instr).

--- 2.11 IMPORT COMMIT & DEDUPE ---
- saveAllQuestions(): reentrancy lock _saveAllQuestionsInFlight (double-click duplicate import guard; buffer cleared only AFTER commit); per item: manual answer from #manual-answer-{i} overrides; comma-split ONLY bare letter lists (/^[A-Da-d](?:\s*,\s*[A-Da-d])*$/) — multi-part free-text "(a)..., (b)(i)..." never split; type inference (array+options→mcq; A-D+options→mcq; numeric regex→numeric; else text); newQ fields: id crypto.randomUUID() fallback Date.now+i; subject = _normalizeSubjectKey(session) — PLACEMENT ALWAYS SESSION CONTEXT, Gem stamps preserved only as gemSubject/gemChapter provenance; chapter trimmed-or-null; images (imageDataUrl, diagramImageUrl, optionImageUrls, solutionImageUrl), gemImage/gemOptionImages/gemSolutionImage provenance; extractedText/options/correctAnswer/type='unsolved'/errorReason:null/timeTaken:0/solution/hint/qElo (carried or chapter default)/isAnomaly:false/qEloSource ('uncalibrated' default)/qEloStampedBy/qEloStampedAt/solveCount:0/lastSolvedAt:null/tags/targetTimeMins (explicit >0, else band target from qElo, else 5)/stampBatchSuspicious*/difficulty.
- Dedupe _questionDedupeKey: text key subject+'|t:'+normalized text (lowercase collapsed whitespace) when ≥12 chars, ELSE image fingerprint subject+'|i:'+len+':'+last64chars when imageDataUrl >100 chars, ELSE null (always imported). Deliberately CHAPTER-INDEPENDENT (old chapter-scoped keys created one copy per chapter). Seed set preloaded from existing bank; intra-batch dupes caught; skippedDupes counted; origin map records first-copy location for the summary alert ("N duplicates skipped — already in Chapter (subj), ... +K more", up to 4 origins listed).
- Registers imported chapter into AppState.chapters (orphan prevention); auto-navigates to first-new chapter when different from current view (#detail-chapter-name innerHTML "Chapter (subject)", renderChaptersList, subview practice-chapter-detail-view, mode buttons, showQuestionList; console.log '[saveAllQuestions] Auto-navigated...'); forceHideModal preview-modal AND upload-modal (300ms fade would let upload-modal "reopen"); clears #text-add-terminal; success alert "Successfully imported N fresh problems into the local engine.{dupSummary}".
- Duplicate cleanup: _findDuplicateQuestionIndexes() groups by dedupe key keeping index 0; window.purgeDuplicateQuestions() (manual, confirm dialog, reports kept total); _autoPurgeDuplicateQuestions() runs once in initApp (silent, console.warn '[auto-purge] Removed N cross-chapter duplicate(s)', never breaks boot).

--- 2.12 GRIND STATION PRACTICE (standard flow) ---
- startPracticeWithQuestion(questions,index): forces AppState.practiceFlowMode='standard' + _modeAdaptive.targetPwin=null (armed-mode leak guard); practiceQuestions/currentPracticeIndex; practiceSubmittedFlags fresh array; kills + restarts 1s practiceTimer (AppState.practiceSeconds); renderPracticeQuestionModal + _renderModeFooter + openModal('practice-modal'); photoHidden=false; #hide-photo-toggle '📷 Hide Image'.
- renderPracticeQuestionModal(): AppState.currentQ = queue[index] (empty-queue guard); selectedMcq=null; scrollTop reset; question image #practice-modal-img (click → openPracticeImageLightbox; Drive fetch captured against _imgQ so Next/skip can't attach a stale image); diagram block .diagram-hint "📐 Diagram:"; text #latex-render.latex; SUBMITTED state: result-banner correct ("✅ Clutched! The answer was: X") / wrong ("❌ Fumbled...") / neutral reveal ("🔍 Answer revealed... were you right?") + "💡 Peep Solution" button (.show-solution-btn → showSolutionPopup) when solution non-empty; UNSUBMITTED: MCQ (multi header "Pick all that hit" when correctAnswer is array, else "Lock in your answer"; .mcq-option rows with escapeAttribute'd data-option; per-option figure images via _gemOptionImageUrl; submit btn 'Lock In Answer'), numeric (input #numeric-answer-input type number step any placeholder 0.00; 'Lock In Answer'), text ("This is a free-response question..." + 'Reveal Answer'). Synchronous processElementMath after each render.
- toggleMcqOption(el,text): single-select exclusive; multi toggles + rebuilds selectedMcq array from .mcq-option.selected dataset.
- decodeOption(): DOMParser entity-decode of data-option (encoded options were otherwise ungradeable).
- Grading logic practiceSubmit():
  * Guard: already-submitted index returns immediately (double-count protection relied on by time injection).
  * MCQ multi: collect .selected options; none → alert "Pick at least one option."; letters derived from option indexOf → String.fromCharCode(65+i); compare sorted selected letters vs resolveMcqCorrectLetters(q).sort() element-wise case-insensitive; equality = correct.
  * MCQ single: AppState.selectedMcq required ("Select an answer. No cop-outs."); valid index required; user letter = 65+indexOf; correct iff resolveMcqCorrectLetters(q).includes(letter) — handles full-string answers like "B) \frac{I}{4}" resolving to letter B.
  * Numeric: #numeric-answer-input; empty → refuse; parseFloat NaN → refuse grade ("formatting mistake, not a wrong answer"); stored correctAnswer non-finite → UNGRADEABLE → routes to _enterSelfReportFlow (previously auto-marked WRONG); tolerance |user − correct| < 1e-6.
  * Text/subjective/untyped (and mcq-with-no-options parity guard): _enterSelfReportFlow() — freezes timer into AppState._frozenTextQSeconds, kills timer, marks submitted, timeTaken=frozen, downgrades wrong/error → unsolved (neutral banner), stamps lastReviewedAt=now, hydrates easeFactor to 2.5, injects practice time, saves, re-renders, calls addTextQuestionFollowUp().
  * Graded path: submitted flag; timeTaken=practiceSeconds; _injectPracticeTimeIntoStudySecs() (deposits seconds into studySecs[normalizedSubject] — skips deposit when pomo-active ('pomo-active'/'timer-running' body classes or window._pomoRunning) to avoid double counting, repaints header via dynamic + direct updateStudyTimeHeader, saves); locks q.firstAttemptResult ONCE ('correct'/'incorrect'); correct → status 'solved' + changeCount(subject,+1) unless already solved or bountyMode; wrong → status 'wrong'; adaptive perf snapshot BEFORE mutations; calculateEloMigration(subject, seconds, 1|0, _getChapterHealth(subject,chapter), q); applyDifficulty(q,...); stampPlantCum(q,subject) when solved; saveAllAsync; clearInterval(practiceTimer) (stale-τ contamination fix); bountyMode → evaluateBountyOutcome(isCorrect); re-render; injectEloShiftChip(result); renderEloMatrix(); mode session → _modeNextTargetPwin(outcome) + _showModeContinueButton(); wrong → after 50ms appends 'Send to the Vault (Log Error)' btn → AppState.pendingWrongQ=q + openModal('error-reason-modal').
- addTextQuestionFollowUp(): buttons #text-correct-btn "Clean Lock ✅" / #text-wrong-btn "Skill Issue ❌"; correct branch mirrors graded path (score 1, frozen seconds, stampPlantCum, changeCount if newly solved, banner 'Clean lock. Marked correct.', chip, throttle); wrong branch (score 0, status wrong, banner 'Skill issue. Marked wrong.', log-error button). Both hide #practice-submit-btn.
- Error logging confirmErrorLog(): reads #error-reason-select; pendingWrongQ.status='error', errorReason=reason, lastReviewedAt=now, easeFactor hydrated 2.5; save; alert "Logged to the Vault. Error archived."; closes error-reason-modal; renderErrorMatrixFromBank + renderChapterDecayGrid; re-render practice.
- practiceNext/practicePrev: inert during Flow/Hardcore; navigate index, reset practiceSeconds, restart timer only for unsubmitted questions; end-of-queue → closePracticeModal + alert "Queue completely cleared! Flawless run..." + showQuestionList.
- closePracticeModal(): closeModalStr practice-modal; kills timer; window.__lastQuestionPickedWithLifeline=false; _clearModeHistory(); refreshes list if question-list view active.
- deleteQuestion(id): confirm "Permanently yeet this question from local AND cloud storage? Gone forever. No undo."; deletes driveImageId/driveDiagramId media via deleteMediaFromDrive; splice; recordCloudTombstone(id); save; re-render list or goToChapters when chapter emptied.
- First-attempt accuracy helper _firstAttemptResult(q): priority q.firstAttemptResult → earliest q.historyLogs entry result → legacy status fallback (solved→correct, wrong/error→incorrect) → null (excluded). Re-solves NEVER change accuracy.

--- 2.13 PRACTICE MODES: FLOW STATE & HARDCORE (adaptive picker) ---
- Mode buttons injected into #mode-buttons-row (idempotent; styles injected once as #mode-button-style): #btn-mode-feed '📸 Feed' (opens upload-modal), #btn-mode-flow '🎯 Flow' (title 'Flow State · P_win ∈ [0.75, 0.85]'), #btn-mode-hardcore '⚡ Hardcore' (title 'Hardcore / Overclock · P_win ∈ [0.35, 0.50]'); hover particle trails (4 dots, cyan rgba(0,200,255,.7) / red rgba(255,90,90,.7), @keyframes mode-particle-float), click ripple .mode-ripple; engage sounds _playModeSound (flow: sine 880→1320Hz, gain .12→.001 over .2s; hardcore: triangle 180→60Hz gain .18→.001 over .25s) through shared ctx window.__modeAudioCtx, FX-gated. window.startFlowPractice/startHardcorePractice/exitPracticeMode wired.
- _setPracticeMode(mode): validated against PRACTICE_MODES; persists practiceFlowMode; _clearModeHistory(); adaptive targetPwin := mode center; Hardcore daily cap: reset counters when LOCAL date rolls (hardcoreDailyDate/hardcoreDailyCount — UTC would grant quota at 05:30 IST); cap MODE_TUNING.hardcore.capPerDay || 8 → alert 'Daily Hardcore cap reached (8). Override tomorrow with full send.' and revert to standard; saves; refill; badge/footer; OPENS the practice modal + starts the 1s timer INSIDE the non-empty branch (a failed refill previously leaked a phantom timer that granted the ×1.5 deep-work ELO bonus forever); bail-out path clears stale timers.
- Picker _pickQuestionForMode(subject,chapter,mode,seenSet): pool = same subject + _chaptersMatch chapter + _isUnexecutedModeQuestion (status null/'unsolved'/'unexecuted' AND no historyLogs AND no firstAttemptResult AND solveCount==0 AND !lastReviewedAt AND !lastSolvedAt AND timeTaken≤0 AND !errorReason) + finite qElo + !isAnomaly + !modeRetired + not in seenSet; hardcore enforces cfg.minQeloFloor across ALL scans (strict/fallback/best-effort — previously best-effort handed Flow-easy questions); userElo = AppState.elo[subject] || 1200; candidate ranking fewest q.skips → smallest solveCount → oldest lastSolvedAt; two-pass scan: strict window targetP±0.10 around live adaptive target (fresh runs use midpoint of cfg.PwinMin/PwinMax), then fallback cfg.PwinFallbackMin/Max; idealQElo = userElo − 400·log10(targetP/(1−targetP)); best-effort closest-|qElo−ideal| bridge with console.warn diagnostics (userElo, chapter range, chosen gap) instead of silent dead-end; Lifeline integration: Lifeline.evaluateLifeline(...) may shift windows toward easier P_win, widen fallback proportionally, Lifeline.activateLifeline(), set window.__lastQuestionPickedWithLifeline=true (else false).
- Adaptive throttle _modeNextTargetPwin(mode,outcome): win → delta=+0.06×clamp01(1−(tau−winSweetSpot)) (fast & correct → harder next); miss → delta=−0.10×(1.5−clamp01((qElo−userElo+400)/800)) (miss easy → ease up more); bounds [center−0.10, center+0.15] clamped [0.05,0.95]; EMA blend 0.5 cur + 0.5 target.
- Skip system: openSkipPopover() (window-exposed) builds #skip-popover anchored above #practice-skip-btn with reasons 📈 Too hard / 📉 Too easy / ✅ Already know it / ⏳ Not now (styles #mode-skip-style; outside-click dismiss); skipQuestion(reason): stamps q.skips++, q.lastSkippedAt, q.skipReasons (kept to last 3), q.modeRetired=true when reason 'already know' or skips≥3; immediate non-EMA target nudge ±0.08 for too hard/too easy ('already know'/'not now' → no change); adds to seen set; saves; shows undo toast #skip-undo-toast for 5000ms restoring exact prior skip fields; refills and serves next, or tears down when pool exhausted. NO-regret: no Elo/qElo/status/time mutation.
- Continue: window.continuePractice = _modeAdvance (marks seen, refill, serve or exit). Exit: _exitPracticeMode resets everything, closes modal. _serveModeEntry(entry): one-item queue; submitted entries served WITHOUT restarting timer. _rebuildPracticeQuestionsForMode(seenSet): refill; empty pool → alert 'No more Flow/Hardcore-eligible questions in this chapter. Exiting to standard mode.' + full reset. Footer _renderModeFooter: Prev/Next hidden in mode, Skip shown, Continue hidden until a solve.

--- 2.15 ELO ENGINE (COGNITIVE MMR) ---
- Constants (app.js-local): ELO_SUBJECT_BASELINES = { physics:{K:12,defaultTime:180}, chemistry:{K:12,defaultTime:90}, maths:{K:16,defaultTime:240} }. ELO_RANK_TIERS: NPC 🧍 0–1199; Skill Issue 💀 1200–1599; Cooking 🍳 1600–1999; Let Him Cook 👨‍🍳 2000–2399; Diffed the Exam 💀 2400–2799; Unhinged Gigachad 🗿 2800–∞.
- Imported tunings (storage.js): ELO_GEM_STAMP_TUNING (uses .ceiling, T.K_user, T.K_q, T.timeMin, T.physicsTauBuffer, T.misfireExtraMult), CALIBRATED_SOLVE_THRESHOLD, ELO_BANDS/BAND_TARGET_TIME/getEloBand, MODE_TUNING (per-mode PwinMin/PwinMax/PwinFallbackMin/Max/winSweetSpot/lossFastGrace/winsMultiplier/lossMultiplier/minQeloFloor/capPerDay).
- calculateEloMigration(subject, actualTime, scoreOutcome(1|0), chapterHealth, questionObj) → result object {subject, deltaSubject, deltaGlobal, old/newSubjectElo, old/newGlobalElo, old/newQElo, tierChanged, oldTier, newTier, isAnomaly, cnsLoad/cnsMultiplier/cnsBadge/cnsLabel/cnsTauCap, nightGuardActive/nightGuardMultiplier, lifelineActive/lifelineMultiplier, timeBonusFactor, tauSeconds, timeBonusLabel, modeActive}.
- FAST PATH (calibrated: qEloSource==='gem-stamped' OR 'learned' with solveCount ≥ CALIBRATED_SOLVE_THRESHOLD): P_win = 1/(1+10^((Q−E)/400)); tau = max(T.timeMin, actual/targetSecs); physics tau ×= T.physicsTauBuffer; timeMult = _modeTimeMultiplier(win?1:0, tau, mode): wins 1/(1+max(0,tau−cfg.winSweetSpot)); losses 1+0.15×max(0,cfg.lossFastGrace−tau) below grace else 1+0.4×max(0,tau−grace); rawSubjectDelta = round(T.K_user × (S−P_win) × timeMult); misfire stinginess ×T.misfireExtraMult when S=0 && P_win≥0.7; qEloDrift = round(T.K_q × ((1−S)−P_q_win)); mode multiplier winsMultiplier/lossMultiplier applied to delta BEFORE rating write (Hardcore ≈ 1.8× win / 0.6× loss per comments; Flow 1.0 passthrough); gating via _activeModeForQuestion(questionObj) — only the queued modal question gets mode rates (reference identity then id match), matrix/SR reviews settle standard even mid-mode (anti farming); ceiling clamp ELO_GEM_STAMP_TUNING.ceiling; CNS stepped multiplier on gains only via CNSLoad.computeCnsLoad(subject,studySecs) (SKIPPED when NightGuard.shouldForceCns() to avoid multiplicative stacking with the ×0.20 Tier-3 force-set: cnsMultiplier 0.20, badge 🛌, label 'CNS force-set: late-night Tier 3'); Flow Lifeline ×0.65 on gains when __lastQuestionPickedWithLifeline (+ q._lifelineAssisted tag, Lifeline.resetAfterSolve(), flag consumed); NightGuard.getMultiplier() post-23:00 diminishing returns applied on gains; hardcore win increments hardcoreDailyCount (LOCAL day); qElo += drift; anomaly when |newQ − chapterAvg(_getChapterAvgElo, non-anomaly questions, fallback 1200)| > 600 → isAnomaly; easeFactor +0.15 (cap 3.0) on win / −0.20 (floor 1.3) on loss; solveCount++, lastSolvedAt stamped; global = _computeGlobalMetaMMR(eP,eC,eM) = p-norm harmonic ((P⁻²+C⁻²+M⁻²)/3)^(-1/2) minus 0.15×Σ positive deviations from mean, floored 0; AppState.eloUpdatedAt = Date.now() (LWW cloud clock); timeBonusLabel FAST ×n.nn when timeMult≥1.10, SLOW when ≤0.85, else BALANCED.
- LEGACY R_perf PATH (uncalibrated): τraw = actual/chapterAvgTime; physics τ ×0.85 (calculation buffer); chemistry slow-but-correct downgrade S_forPerf = max(0.1, 1 − 0.4(τ−1.25)) when τ>1.25; R_perf = E_s + 400(S_forPerf·ln(1+τ) − (1−S_forPerf)/τ); E_score logistic; K_system = K_base × μ_block × ω_decay × δ_error where μ_block = 0.75 (window._eloDistractionFlag) | 1.5 (practiceTimer live OR pomo-active) | 1.0; ω_decay = 1 + ln(max(0.0001, 2 − H_ch/100)); δ_error = exp(−0.4 × N_activeErrors/15); asymmetric scaling Ω_win = 2/(1+10^((E_s−1200)/800)), Ω_loss = 2/(1+10^((1200−E_s)/800)); re-solve decay: vault items (errorReason && firstAttemptResult) correct re-solves ×0.25, wrong still ×1.0; disparity filter spread>400: win hard-capped 0.2 pts, loss ×2.0 liquidation; mode multipliers; hardcore daily counter (legacy path too — previously uncapped via uncalibrated questions); newE_s soft cap 2999.99; qElo += 0.05×(R_perf − Q_Elo) convergence (learning rate lowered from 20 — hyper-inflation crash note); anomaly threshold 600; solveCount++ and qEloSource flip to 'learned' unless gem-stamped; lastReviewedAt permanently stamped (write-side counterpart to JIT hydration); easeFactor nudges identical; CNSLoad.logSolve(subject, correctBool, actualTime, T_avg) telemetry (fixed: compared scoreOutcome to string 'correct' — always false, logged every solve incorrect).
- Chapter health _getChapterHealth(subject,chapter): Bjork New-of-Disuse retrieval strength RS_i = e^(−ln2·Δt/S_i), Δt in float days since lastReviewedAt, S_i = max(0.5, easeFactor); items = bank rows with errorReason and status error/solved/wrong; accessibility = Σ(Q_Elo·RS)/ΣQ_Elo × 100; NEUTRAL 50 when no tracked items; clamped [10,100]. PURE READ (never mutates — safe at high frequency).
- _hydrateMemoryFields(q) JIT: easeFactor default 2.5; qElo fallback 1200; lastReviewedAt resolved latest historyLog timestamp → solved=24h ago → error/wrong=now → now.
- Helpers: getRankTierDetails(rating) {name,icon,badge,rating}; _getNextTierThreshold/_getNextTierName; _getChapterAvgTime (timed, non-anomaly, baseline defaultTime fallback); _getActiveErrorBankCount (status error|wrong); _normalizeSubjectKey (math/mathematics→maths); _chaptersMatch (trim+lowercase); _computeDefaultQEloForCurrentChapter (rounded chapter avg else 1200); _safeQElo (NaN-safe, fallback 1200); _eloBandTargetTime(qElo) maps storage bands T1..T7 to BAND_TARGET_TIME, fallback 5; _eloTargetSeconds(q)=targetTimeMins×60; sanitizeChapterName.
- UI feedback injectEloShiftChip(result): chip appended to #practice-modal-content (delta sign, subject, tier, FAST/SLOW pill with τ seconds, [🎯 FLOW]/[⚡ HC] mode tag; auto-remove 4200ms) AND header slot #elo-header-slot or #sr-elo-header-slot (dedicated prominent chip, wipe-timer race handled); tierChanged && delta>0 → burstEmojis(center of practice modal or SR drawer, 40 emojis ['🎉','😄','🔥','✨','🥳','🎊','💯','🌟','😎','🏆'], scale 1.6) + playSuperSound. Exposed via window (getRankTierDetails/calculateEloMigration/renderEloMatrix/injectEloShiftChip/_getChapterHealth) for matrix.js without circular imports.

--- 2.20 SIDEBAR LOOP RAIL (CK ENGINE) ---
- buildNav(): inserts .nav-ck-loop after sidebar .logo-container — title "TODAY'S LOOP" + span#nav-ck-risk; four rings .ck-ring.nav-ck-ring ids nav-ck-arc-p/c/m/f (labels P/C/M/✓; --ring-c #3ddcff/#22c55e/#ffb224/#a78bfa; progress via CSS var --p clamped [0,100]); appends #nav-ck-ladder to .user-profile.
- Rings: p/c/m = solved/target per subject (AppState.activeTargets fallback baseTargets); f = today's fixed errors / total baseErrorTargets (fixed-today computed by scanning historyLogs result==='correct' timestamps within today, O(1)-cached per day via _fixCache and bumped by window.__ckBumpTodayFix(subject) which matrix.js calls on each correct SR log commit).
- Loop-done: all three subjects ≥ targets AND fixes ≥ error targets → .ck-loop-done.
- Streak-at-risk warning: hour ≥ 18 && totalSolved()==0 → #sidebar gets .ck-streak-danger and risk text '🚨 STREAK AT RISK — solve 1 now'; else '🌌 LOOP CLOSED' (done) or default 'close it to keep the streak'.
- Tier ladder mini-map: prev/current/next ELO_RANK_TIERS rungs around global Elo; DOM rewritten only when rung HTML changes (data-rungs attr memo — was firing the math observer every second).
- Beacons/badges: focus beacon on 'Focus Mode' nav item while #pomo-mini-widget visible; grind beacon on 'Grind Station' while a practice session has progress; The Vault nav badge = ckReadyCount (count of errorReason-bearing items with getDueStatus(q).status==='ready'), glow class; practice nav pulse .nav-ck-pulse when worst chapter health <45 (ckLowHealth scans error-bearing chapters).
- Combo/shield economy (fastTick 250ms, only while #practice-modal.active): combo grows with practiceCorrectStreak deltas; crit charge +34 per correct, primed at ≥100 → next correct fires critPayout (window.showSupercharged + playSuperSound + shields+=1 persisted); every 5th combo step grants +1 shield; a miss ALWAYS resets combo (AppState._ckComboBreak event-driven) and consumes 1 persisted shield (saving the crit charge) else crit −50. Shields persisted in localStorage jeemax_ck_shields. Heavy derivations memoized: NAV_HEAVY_TTL=30000ms, NAV_HEAVY_MIN_INTERVAL=2000ms, invalidated by window.__jmaxDataDirty counter bumps. Errors throttled to one console.error('[CK engine tick]') per 10s.
