- saveTargets(): parseTarget ints (>0 else 10); idbSet basePhys/baseChem/baseMath/jeeTargetLockDate(now ISO); activeTargets recomputed with mood multiplier; lockTargetsOnly(); alert "Symmetry constraints locked for the next 24h..."
- window.saveErrTargets(): ints (>0 else 5 — comment: parseInt||5 accepted negatives); idbSet baseErrPhys/baseErrChem/baseErrMath + SHARED jeeTargetLockDate; lock err inputs; re-render resolution dashboard + momentum candles.
- lockTargetsOnly(): disables #set-tgt-phys/chem/math + #btn-save-settings, shows #target-lock-lbl.visible; disables #set-err-* + #btn-save-err-settings, #err-target-lock-lbl.visible. Boot enforces while lock age <1 day.
- testGeminiKey(): #gemini-key → idbSet gemini_api_key; GET generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models?key=...; #key-test-result '✅ Key verified...' / '❌ Key rejected...' / '⚠️ Network ded.'
- Smart Mistake Report (replaces AI Matrix Dump): report.js is the pure engine (Node-testable; smoke scripts/smoke-report-engine.mjs). buildQuestionFacts normalizes each bank question (tags or 'untagged', getEloBand difficulty bucket or 'Unrated', mistake = errorReason set OR any incorrect historyLog, friction counts from JSON-string logs w/ errorReason fallback, slow/fast time signal vs personal pace then BAND_TARGET_TIME, overconfident sure-but-wrong, repeat offender ≥2 wrongs, 30d-vs-prior-30d trend). aggregateTags folds each question into EVERY tag; rows ranked by mistakes then lossMass (Σ pWin(userElo, qElo) of lost questions). aggregateBands buckets T1–T7 + Unrated; leak profile splits foundation ≤T2 vs stretch ≥T5. buildMistakeReport adds KPIs, subject rollup, deterministic signals (foundation crack / stretch wall / blind spots / slow-wrong vs fast-fail clusters / worsening tags) and rule-based next actions per weakest tags. Renderers: renderReportText (bounded fixed-width download jeemaxxing-smart-report-YYYY-MM-DD.txt) and renderReportHtml (inline #ai-dump-report-preview, all values escaped). app.js wiring: _gatherDumpScopeQuestions(silent) collects checked chapters → {selected, results(enriched), raw(bank objects)}; preview/smart path consumes RAW objects (historyLogs preserved), JSON export keeps enriched shape (jeemaxxing-raw-bank-YYYY-MM-DD.json).
- Mock autopsy: buildMockAutopsy(wrongIds, qById) groups a finished paper's wrong questions by tag × difficulty band (+ likely cause from frictions); showResults renders it in .mr-autopsy under the scorecard, caches m.autopsy {total, byTag, byBand, text, generatedAt} so reopening a done paper shows the same breakdown, and offers window.mockCopyAutopsy() clipboard copy.

--- 2.22 NOTIFICATIONS / HAPTICS / FX ---
- app.js itself makes NO Notification-API or navigator.vibrate calls; notification functions (confirmTimerNotification, notifyKeepGoing, initAudioContext) are pomodoro.js exports merely re-exposed on window.
- FX gating everywhere via window.FX.wantEffects() / window.FX.wantSound() (absent FX object ⇒ effects ON). Shared AudioContext singleton window.__jmaxSharedAudio via _sharedSfxCtx() (fresh-context-per-call used to exhaust the browser concurrency cap). Sounds: playCorrectSound sine 523.25/659.25/783.99 staggered 80ms; playWrongSound square 600/300; playSuperSound square arpeggio 523.25/659.25/783.99/1046.50 with echo voices; streak-shield noise burst; mode engage chime/thud.
- Visuals: burstEmojis(origin,count,emojis,scale) GPU-transform particle system (translate3d + rotate + death shrink; gravity 0.12×scale; life decay 0.008–0.023/frame); showNormalGlow (.green-glow-overlay); showSupercharged (.supercharged-glow-overlay + 40-emoji burst centered on practice modal/SR drawer + sound + Math.random()<0.15 → activateOverheat — deliberately NOT FX-gated, gameplay); triggerRedFlash (.red-flash-overlay); triggerStreakShield (visual + audio, FX-gated halves); activateOverheat/deactivateOverheat: 300000 ms (5 min) window.overheatChaos + body.overheat-active; overheatUsed single-shot double payout (patched submit grants changeCount(subject,+2) on the first overheat win).

--- 2.23 GLOBAL KATEX ENGINE ---
- processElementMath(element): TreeWalker over text nodes (skips SCRIPT/STYLE/.katex); delimiter regex $$…$$ | \[…\] | $…$ | \(…\); _wrapBareLatex wraps bare \command sequences ($-less Gem dumps) including nested-environment-aware \begin{env}…\end{env} blocks as $$…$$; output rebuilt segment-by-segment with escapeHtml on non-math gaps (stored-XSS fix); katex.renderToString(throwOnError:false) per fragment; malformed fragments preserved escaped; container stamped data-math-rendered (recursion guard); hard error boundary never breaks state.
- globalMathObserver: watches body subtree; element nodes processed unless SCRIPT/STYLE/.katex/stamped; raw text-node insertions clear the parent's stale stamp first (dynamic re-renders like #solution-content picked up).
- Arrival watchdog + CDN fallback + failure banner (described in boot section). window._wrapBareLatex and shim window.renderLatexInElement exposed.

7) TODOs / HACKS / NOTABLE COMMENTS
======================================================================
- "dead wiring — audit CRITICAL" (L4514): daily bounty was assigned at boot but NOTHING could open it; fixed by refreshBountyRail + rail button.
- jeemax_last_calibrated_date intentionally demoted (L949): it is only a mood-prompt timestamp; keying the counter reset off it wiped same-day counters when the vibe check was skipped. Dedicated sentinel jeemax_last_settled_date added; pre-fix installs seeded from jeemax_boot_seq_date.
- Streak walk: old hard cap of 30 iterations froze every real streak at "30 Days" forever; replaced with 3650-iteration (10-year) runaway guard ending at first gap.
- Pixel-flame: streak canvas/context deliberately NOT cached globally — the SR drawer builds/destroys its own #sr-streak-canvas, so renderLoop re-resolves the canvas every frame (L7874).
- Wrong answers never call changeCount() (L8174) — protects daily counters/error totals/streak ledger.
- CK fixes: combo ALWAYS breaks on a miss (event-driven via AppState._ckComboBreak, works even when a shield saved the flame); persisted shields save the CRIT charge, never swallow combos silently.
- Elo engine TDZ bug (comment L6237): Sefc referenced ~25 lines before declaration → ReferenceError silently killed every calibrated solve via caller try/catch; declared-first now. Same class of fix for timeMult/tauSeconds (were read from an out-of-scope local → TypeError per calibrated solve; now returned by _deltaBasedUserAndQuestionReward).
- CNS telemetry bug (L6599): scoreOutcome compared to string 'correct' was ALWAYS false — every solve logged as incorrect, dead accuracy component and trivially-satisfied anti-cheat veto.
- Error-target parsing: parseInt||5 accepted negative numbers verbatim; now Number.isFinite(n)&&n>0 else 5.
- Chapter-scoped dedupe created one copy per chapter ("my uploads randomly duplicate"); dedupe keys now chapter-independent + boot-time _autoPurgeDuplicateQuestions + manual purge + import-time dupes summary pointing at origin chapters.
- extractTextForAll aborts on payload size mismatch with ZERO partial state writes (hydration only after validation).
- Modal stacking: closeModalStr's 300ms deferred display:none let dismissed overlays bury newly opened ones (preview↔crop↔upload↔gem-map); forceHideModal used at every handoff; monotonic _modalOpenTokens fixes the open→close-in-one-frame zombie modal.
- iPad bugs: crop overlay canvas 0×0 because clientWidth is 0 while modal hidden (deterministic natural-size sizing); scratchpad coordinate gap growing linearly (CSS 100vh ≠ innerHeight on iPadOS — JS-sized canvases); iOS ignoring user-scalable=no (gesture-event blockers); dropped pointerdown from pointer capture state machine (capture deliberately omitted, commented twice L9938/L10034); roundRect fallback for old WebViews.
- LaTeX traps: blanket /\+n/ replacement destroyed \nu \neq \ne \neg \nabla \notin (fixed in _fixBackslashRuns with (?!\w)); \\ row separators in align/matrix blocks preserved (halving pairs, not collapsing runs); sanitize lookahead widened from partial key whitelist to ANY `"key":` terminator (partial lists ate qElo stamps and whole questions); option-array lookahead without trailing quote (real same-line options collapsed into one giant match previously); leading-metadata recovery from the previous split segment (fields emitted before extractedText anchor were dropped, killing "type":"subjective" and qElo stamps).
- XSS hardening: math hydration escapes all non-KaTeX segments (old single .replace fed raw bank content to innerHTML); _safeImgSrc allows only data:image/, https://, blob: URLs.
- Phantom ×1.5 deep-work bonus: a mode-refill failure left practiceTimer running with no modal, granting permanent bonus; timer start moved inside the non-empty-queue branch.
- Hardcore cap: daily reset keyed to LOCAL date, not UTC (UTC rolled at 05:30 IST, granting quota pre-midnight); legacy R_perf path also increments the counter (was farmable via uncalibrated questions).
- Mode-rate gating: _activeModeForQuestion ensures matrix/SR-drawer solves settle at standard rates even while Flow/Hardcore armed (can't milk 1.8× wins + escrow on easy error reviews).
- Overheat 15% random trigger deliberately never FX-gated ("gameplay — never gated", L7829).
- getDailyHistorySync must return the FULL ledger — "do NOT truncate to 15" (deload windows need depth).
- AudioContext reuse comments: fresh contexts per SFX accumulated toward the browser's ~6–12 instance cap until ALL app audio died; shared window.__jmaxSharedAudio/__modeAudioCtx now.
- Practice-grid lazy loading: "free at 600px" caused decode churn (main scroll lag); unload moved to ±2000px sweep.
- updateUI heavy renders gated to visible dashboard (bank-growing DOM rebuilds ran per solve inside the practice modal).
- Nav-heavy derivations memoized (30s TTL / 2s floor / dirty counter) — constant O(bank×logs) rescans on 1s ticks eliminated.
- renderCalendar single innerHTML write (innerHTML += inside loops was O(n²) reparse per cell opened).
- Misc naming: errors tab = "The Vault", practice tab = "Grind Station", dashboard ring = "Today's Progress", import buffer = extractedItems, error log = "Send to the Vault (Log Error)".

JEEMAXXING - MODULE DOCUMENTATION: storage.js + matrix.js
Sources fully read: C:\Users\Chaksh\Desktop\JEEmaxxing\storage.js (2169 lines), C:\Users\Chaksh\Desktop\JEEmaxxing\matrix.js (2352 lines). Everything below is present in those two files; items asked for but NOT in these files are explicitly flagged.

=======================================================
A. storage.js - persistence core (2169 lines)
=======================================================

A.1 AppState (export const AppState) - every field, default, owner
--------------------------------------------------------------------
UI/practice session state (owned by app.js practice flows):
- currentSubject: 'physics' - active grind-station subject
- currentChapter: '' - active chapter name
- currentChapterQuestions: [] - questions loaded for the chapter view
- practiceQuestions: [] - practice-mode queue
- currentPracticeIndex: 0 - index into practiceQuestions
- practiceSeconds: 0 - practice timer seconds
- selectedMcq: null - currently selected MCQ letter in main modal
- currentQ: null - question object shown in main modal
- pendingWrongQ: null - wrong answer awaiting resolution
- photoHidden: false - main modal photo visibility
- practiceSubmittedFlags: [] - per-question submitted markers
- currentFilter: 'all' - practice list filter
- bountyMode: false - bounty toggle
- profilePicData: null - base64 profile picture (persisted under 'jeemax_profile_pic')
- newErrorPicData: null - pending photo for the add-error modal (consumed by matrix.js addErrorBlock)
- moodMultiplier: 1.0 - persisted 'jeemax_mood_multiplier'
- currentErrorSubject: 'physics' - active Errors-view subject (written by matrix.js openErrorMatrix)
- calMonthOffset: 0 - calendar month navigation offset
- geminiApiKey: '' - persisted 'gemini_api_key'; mirrored into #gemini-key input on load
- practiceCorrectStreak: 0 - incremented/decremented by matrix.js _applyResult and app.js
- extractedItems: [] - AI dump extraction staging
- practiceFlowMode: 'standard' - one of PRACTICE_MODES ['standard','flow','hardcore']; persisted 'jeemax_practice_mode'
- hardcoreDailyCount: 0 / hardcoreDailyDate: null - hardcore anti-grind counter, persisted 'jeemax_hardcore_daily' ({date,count}); on load reset to 0 unless stored date === todayLocalKey()

Cross-module mutable state:
- questionBank: [] - THE central store of question objects; persisted 'jeemax_question_bank' (images stripped)
- practiceTimer: null - interval handle (owned by app.js pomodoro/practice timers)
- chapters: { physics:['Kinematics','Thermodynamics'], chemistry:['Mole Concept'], maths:['Calculus'] } - chapter registry; persisted 'jeemax_chapters'
- bounty: { date:null, questionId:null, timeLimit:0, active:false, payoffCount:0, done:false } - persisted TWICE every save: 'jeemax_bounty' and 'bounty_data' ('bounty_data' is the key loadDataAsync reads back)
- activeTargets: { physics:10, chemistry:10, maths:10 } - live daily targets (runtime; not itself persisted)
- driveAccessToken: null - OAuth access token (token itself persisted under 'jeemax_drive_token')
- cloudFolderId: null - Drive folder id for 'JEEmaxxing_Cloud' (not persisted; re-resolved each boot)
- tokenClient: undefined - google.accounts.oauth2 token client handle
- imageFetchCache: {} - fileId -> fetch-promise cache for fetchMediaFromDrive (session-only)
- cropState: { currentBase64:null, questions:[], resolve:null, canvas:null, ctx:null, imageElement:null, startX:0, startY:0, drawing:false, rect:null, isDiagramCrop:false } - crop tool session state
- visualMode: 'bar' - graph visualization mode
- activePracticeDrawerId: null - declared for the matrix drawer; not referenced inside these two module bodies

Cognitive MMR / Elo:
- elo: { physics:1200, chemistry:1200, maths:1200, global:1200 } - hydrated in loadDataAsync with per-axis guard (missing/non-finite falls back to 1200); persisted 'jeemax_elo'
- eloUpdatedAt: 0 - epoch-ms stamp of last local Elo write; persisted 'jeemax_elo_updated_at'; drives last-write-wins cloud merge

Module-level exported singletons (not part of AppState):
- baseTargets = { physics:10, chemistry:10, maths:10 }
- baseErrorTargets = { physics:5, chemistry:5, maths:5 }
- solved = { physics:0, chemistry:0, maths:0 } - persisted 'jeemax_solved'
- studySecs = { physics:0, chemistry:0, maths:0 } - persisted 'jeemax_study_secs'

UI-callback bridge: registerUiCallbacks(callbacks) is called once by app.js; internal _ui(name,...args) lazily invokes 'updateUI', 'updateStudyTimeHeader', 'renderGraph', 'renderErrorMatrixFromBank', 'lockTargetsOnly'. Keeps storage.js unit-testable without DOM.

Multi-tab reconciliation (BroadcastChannel):
- Channel literal 'jmax-tab-sync' (TAB_SYNC_CHANNEL); per-tab identity _tabId = crypto.randomUUID() or 'tab-'+Date.now().toString(36)+random suffix.
- After every commit _broadcastTabSaved() posts { type:'jmax-saved', tabId, at: Date.now(), solved:{...}, studySecs:{...} }.
- Receiver (_listenTabSync, wired at module eval): ignores own echo by tabId; merges sibling solved/studySecs upward (monotonic within a day); pulls in question ids present in sibling's committed 'jeemax_question_bank' but missing locally, skipping tombstoned ids; if changed fires _ui('updateUI','updateStudyTimeHeader','renderGraph','renderErrorMatrixFromBank').
- Full-bank clobber guard: _foreignCommitPending set on any sibling ping; _doSaveAll then runs _adoptForeignBankUpdates(): re-reads 'jeemax_question_bank' and copies remote fields onto local questions whose signature is unchanged since our last load/commit. Signatures (_bankSig) are JSON fingerprints excluding imageDataUrl/diagramImageUrl/optionImageUrls/solutionImageUrl; baseline map _bankSigs rebuilt by _captureBankSigs() after load and after each commit. Logs '[tab-sync] adopted N remotely-updated question(s) before commit'.

A.2 IndexedDB layer
--------------------
- DB_NAME = 'jeemaxxing_db' (export), DB_VERSION = 1 (export). ONE object store: 'storage' with keyPath 'key'; records shaped { key, value }; created in onupgradeneeded if absent.
- openDB(): caches handle in dbPromise; open error resets dbPromise=null ('a failed open must NOT poison the session'); db.onversionchange closes handle + drops cache (another tab may upgrade/delete); onblocked waits.
- idbSet(key,value): one readwrite tx, store.put; tx error -> _reportPersistFailure(key,err) then reject.
- idbSetMany(entries): many [key,value] puts in ONE readwrite tx; failure reported on onerror AND onabort; rejects.
- idbGet(key): readonly get; resolves value or null; rejects on error (no banner).
- idbGetMany(keys): ALL ~20 boot reads collapsed into ONE readonly tx (iPad Safari fixed per-tx cost; cuts cold start); resolves { key: value|null } map.
- idbRemove(key): readwrite delete; rejects on error/abort.
- Persistence-failure surfacing (_reportPersistFailure): console.error('[persist] write failed for "<key>":', err); sets window.__jmaxPersistFailed = true; ONCE per session appends fixed bottom banner div#jmax-persist-banner (bg #7f1d1d) reading "⚠ Local storage is failing (private mode / disk full). Your progress cannot be saved on this device." with an X closer; retried after 500 ms if body not ready.

Save coalescing:
- saveAllAsync(): trailing-edge debounce 600 ms (setTimeout(_commitCoalescedSave, 600)). All callers within one window share one promise (_saveBatch); await still resolves only after the real commit lands (durability unchanged).
- _commitCoalescedSave(): serializes commits on _saveChain (failed commit never wedges future saves), then settles the shared batch promise.
- flushSaves() (export): clears pending timer, commits immediately, returns batch promise. Wired: window 'pagehide' and document 'visibilitychange'(hidden) both flush.

_doSaveAll() single commit:
1. If foreign commit landed (_foreignCommitPending), adopt remote bank updates first.
2. lightweightBank = bank.map(q => ({...q, imageDataUrl:null, diagramImageUrl:null})) - NOTE: only the two big screenshot fields nulled here; solutionImageUrl and optionImageUrls stay inline in the bank record.
3. ONE idbSetMany with 15 fixed entries (+1 conditional): 'jeemax_question_bank', 'jeemax_chapters', 'jeemax_solved', 'jeemax_study_secs', 'jeemax_bounty', 'jeemax_mood_multiplier', 'jeemax_elo' ({physics,chemistry,maths,global} each ??1200), 'jeemax_elo_updated_at', 'jeemax_practice_mode', 'jeemax_hardcore_daily' {date,count}, 'jeemax_username' (read from #display-username textContent), 'bounty_data' (duplicate), 'baseErrPhys'/'baseErrChem'/'baseErrMath' (baseErrorTargets), plus 'jeemax_profile_pic' only when AppState.profilePicData set.
4. _captureBankSigs() (new multi-tab baseline).
5. persistImageCacheIfChanged() in try/catch (vault failure must not stall ledger/sync/broadcast).
6. await updateDailyHistory().
7. Bumps window.__jmaxDataDirty = (window.__jmaxDataDirty||0)+1 - invalidation signal so memoized derivations (nav rings/badges) invalidate instantly instead of polling.
8. If AppState.driveAccessToken -> syncStateToCloud() (auto push, throttled).
9. _broadcastTabSaved().

Image vault (persistent, unbounded):
- IMAGE_CACHE_KEY = 'jeemax_image_cache'. Entry per question id: { imageDataUrl, diagramImageUrl, optionImageUrls, solutionImageUrl, ts }; an image qualifies only if string length > 100.
- _collectImageCacheFromBank() rebuilds map from bank; keeps previous ts when content unchanged (prevents recency churn).
- Signature gate: _imageCacheSignature() cheap fingerprint (sorted ids; per image length + first/last 24 chars; optionImageUrls sorted-key sample). persistImageCacheIfChanged() writes FIRST then advances the persisted signature so quota failures retry next save.
- hydrateImageCache(): cold-boot-only IDB read (flag _imageCacheLoaded; skips up to ~60 MB re-read on tab switches); fills ONLY empty fields on bank questions (never clobbers fresher in-session images).
- _stripBankImages(q): copy with all four image fields null (used for cloud payloads and direct bank writes).

loadDataAsync():
- One idbGetMany of 20 keys: 'jeemax_question_bank','jeemax_chapters','bounty_data','jeemax_solved','jeemax_study_secs','jeemax_mood_multiplier','jeemax_elo','jeemax_elo_updated_at','jeemax_practice_mode','jeemax_hardcore_daily','jeemax_username','jeemax_profile_pic','gemini_api_key','jeeTargetLockDate','basePhys','baseChem','baseMath','baseErrPhys','baseErrChem','baseErrMath'. Read failure -> _reportPersistFailure('loadDataAsync') + clean slate (g={}).
- Bank assigned raw then _repairQuestionBank(); hydrateImageCache(); _captureBankSigs().
- _repairQuestionBank() (cold-load, idempotent): lowercases/trims q.type; mcq WITHOUT options downgraded to 'text'; array correctAnswer without options collapsed to join(', '); repairLatex() applied to extractedText, correctAnswer, solution, hint and to each element of array correctAnswer/options.
- solved/studySecs coerced Number(x)||0 (guards string-'5'+1 corruption). mood parseFloat fallback 1.0. Elo axes guarded to 1200; eloUpdatedAt guarded to 0. Practice mode validated against PRACTICE_MODES; hardcore counter zeroed unless hcDaily.date === todayLocalKey() (local-day bucketing, not UTC).
- username -> #display-username + #set-username; pfp -> #display-pfp src; API key -> AppState.geminiApiKey + #gemini-key value.
- Targets/lock handling (A.6). Finally migrateQuestionBankSR().

A.3 Google Drive sync
----------------------
Constants: CLIENT_ID = '463564668669-2vplpgdd8li1kn47f65f1d0t1q3bb57p.apps.googleusercontent.com' (export); SCOPES = 'https://www.googleapis.com/auth/drive.file' (export); MODEL_FALLBACK = ["gemini-2.5-flash","gemini-2.5-pro","gemini-2.5-flash-lite"] (export).

OAuth flow:
- initDrive(): bails when Google Identity Services missing (typeof google undefined / accounts / oauth2) so initApp never aborts before the math watchdog. Builds AppState.tokenClient = google.accounts.oauth2.initTokenClient({ client_id: CLIENT_ID, scope: SCOPES, callback }). Callback: stores access_token in AppState.driveAccessToken; idbSet('jeemax_drive_token', token); hides #btn-drive-auth / shows #drive-status; initializeCloudFolder(); setupSyncHeartbeat().
- Boot restore: reads saved 'jeemax_drive_token'; validates in background via isDriveTokenValid(); valid -> same UI swap + folder init + heartbeat; invalid -> token cleared. No saved token -> show #btn-drive-auth, hide #drive-status.
- handleDriveAuth(): AppState.tokenClient.requestAccessToken({ prompt:'consent' }).
- isDriveTokenValid(): GET https://www.googleapis.com/drive/v3/about?fields=user with Bearer; ok -> true. Non-ok -> idbRemove('jeemax_drive_token'), clears driveAccessToken/cloudFolderId, resets buttons, sets #sync-sub-text "Drive disconnected - reconnect in Settings" colored var(--glow-red), returns false. Network exception -> logged, false (token kept).
- handleAuthExpiry() (export): idbRemove('jeemax_drive_token'), nulls token+folder, shows #btn-drive-auth, hides #drive-status, #sync-sub-text "Session Expired. Reconnect Drive." in var(--glow-red), hideLoading().
- waitForDriveToken(callback): immediate callback if token present; else poll every 500 ms, max 20 attempts (~10 s), then warn 'Drive token never arrived - lazy loading aborted'.

Folder: initializeCloudFolder() queries q="mimeType='application/vnd.google-apps.folder' and name='JEEMaxxing_Cloud' and trashed=false". Found -> cloudFolderId = files[0].id then loadStateFromCloud(). Not found -> POST files {name:'JEEMaxxing_Cloud', mimeType:'application/vnd.google-apps.folder'} then syncStateToCloud(). Errors logged 'Cloud Folder Init Failed:'.

Heartbeat: setupSyncHeartbeat() clears prior interval; setInterval 120000 ms (2 min): if token && folderId -> loadStateFromCloud(true) (background, no overlay).

Cloud state file: system_state.json, searched with q="name='system_state.json' and '<cloudFolderId>' in parents and trashed=false".

Payload shape (identical in syncStateToCloud and executeUnifiedSync):
{ date: <YYYY-MM-DD local>, questionBank: [questions minus all four image fields], chapters, solved, studySecs, elo: {physics,chemistry,maths,global}, eloUpdatedAt: <epoch ms|0>, dailyHistory: [ {date, physics, chemistry, maths, count} ascending ] }

syncStateToCloud(force=false) - auto push:
- Guards: no token/folder -> silent return; _cloudSyncInFlight -> set _cloudSyncQueued, return; unless force: throttle CLOUD_PUSH_THROTTLE_MS = 30000 (one push/30 s; manual bypasses) and skip when document.hidden; stamps _lastCloudPushAt.
- Media pass: imageDataUrl length>100 without driveImageId -> uploadMediaToDrive(image,'Q_<qid>.png') -> q.driveImageId; same for diagramImageUrl -> 'Diag_<qid>.png' -> q.driveDiagramId. newlyUploaded -> rewrite stripped 'jeemax_question_bank' + persistImageCacheIfChanged().
- Finds system_state.json; missing -> POST create {name:'system_state.json', parents:[folderId]}; then PATCH https://www.googleapis.com/upload/drive/v3/files/${fileId}?uploadType=media with JSON body. Search status 404 -> throw 'Target cloud storage folder directory not found.'; upload failure -> throw 'State content array matrix synchronization delivery fault.'
- Status text in #sync-sub-text: "Processing media files..." -> "Syncing system state..." -> "Sync Complete ✔" (var(--glow-green)) or "Sync Failed ✖" (var(--glow-red)); success reverts to "System Idle" (white) after 3000 ms.
- finally: _cloudSyncInFlight=false; if queued, zeroes throttle and re-runs syncStateToCloud(true).

loadStateFromCloud(isBackground=false) - download/merge (also the 2-min heartbeat):
- Overlay "Syncing with cloud architecture..." unless background; hidden in finally.
- Merge/conflict rules:
  * questionBank: cloud questions appended only if id unseen AND not tombstoned; existing ids updated ONLY one-way - if cloudQ.status==='solved' && local.status!=='solved' then local.status='solved'. Merged bank immediately persisted (stripped) so a crash cannot lose the merge and the heartbeat won't redo it.
  * chapters: per-subject union (append missing names).
  * dailyHistory: folded into the permanent ledger - per-date/per-subject Math.max when subject fields present; total-only entries attribute surplus to physics; count recomputed; BOTH 'jeemax_solved_by_date_v1' and 'jeemax_daily_history' rewritten (sorted array).
  * Daily counters SAFE ROLLOVER: cloudState.date === todayLocalKey() -> solved/studySecs merged per-subject Math.max (high-water mark). Stale cloud date -> intentional NO-OP (older destructive zeroing branch removed; local authoritative for the current day).
  * Elo: _mergeEloFromCloud(cloudElo, eloUpdatedAt) - LAST-WRITE-WINS: strictly newer stamp replaces all four axes wholesale (real downward changes propagate); both stamps 0 (legacy) -> per-axis Math.max high-water merge; equal/local-newer -> keep local. Ratings are not date-scoped.
- Refreshes UI via _ui('updateUI','updateStudyTimeHeader','renderGraph','renderErrorMatrixFromBank').

executeUnifiedSync() - manual pipeline:
- Validates isDriveTokenValid() first; failure -> alert("Google Drive connection lost. Please reconnect in Settings.") + abort. Adds class 'spinning' to #manual-sync-btn. Status texts: "Downloading cloud dataset..." -> "Merging runtime variables..." (same merge rules as loadStateFromCloud) -> "Updating interface fields..." (persists stripped bank, vault, chapters, solved, studySecs; updateDailyHistory(); four _ui refreshes) -> "Uploading consolidated data...".
- Safety interlock: local solved total 0 while getCloudSolvedTotal() > 0 -> ABORT upload ("Sync skipped – preserving cloud data", amber #fbbf24, revert 3000 ms).
- Creates system_state.json if absent; PATCHes standard payload; success "Synced & Secured ✔" (green, revert 3000 ms); exception -> "Execution Error ✖" red; finally removes 'spinning'.

getCloudSolvedTotal(): downloads system_state.json, returns solved.physics+chemistry+maths (0 on failure).

Tombstones:
- CLOUD_TOMBSTONE_KEY = 'jeemax_cloud_tombstones' - IDB array of stringified deleted question ids. Lazy Set via _getTombstones(). recordCloudTombstone(id) (export) adds + persists (best-effort). _isTombstoned(id) consulted by BOTH cloud merges AND the multi-tab id pull so deleted questions never resurrect.

Media helpers:
- uploadMediaToDrive(base64, filename, folderId, token): multipart/related upload, boundary '-------314159265358979323846', metadata {name, mimeType, parents:[folderId]}, POST https://www.googleapis.com/upload/drive/v3/files?uploadType=multipart; returns data.id.
- fetchMediaFromDrive(fileId, token): memoizes the PROMISE in AppState.imageFetchCache[fileId]; GET .../files/${fileId}?alt=media -> blob -> FileReader.readAsDataURL; non-ok or thrown -> null AND cache entry deleted (lazy-loaders retry after offline/token recovery).
- cacheAllDriveImages(): alert-guard if no token; overlay 'Caching all Drive images locally...'; fills missing imageDataUrl from driveImageId and missing diagramImageUrl from driveDiagramId across the bank; saveAllAsync(); finally hideLoading(); alerts "✅ Cached N images locally — they're kept on this device for good." or 'All images are already cached locally.'
- deleteMediaFromDrive(fileId, token): DELETE .../files/<id>; 404 logged as already-absent; faults logged, never thrown.

A.4 Gemini API integration
----------------------------
- Endpoint: POST `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/${model}:generateContent?key=${apiKey}`; models tried in MODEL_FALLBACK order: gemini-2.5-flash -> gemini-2.5-pro -> gemini-2.5-flash-lite. First success wins; each failure reports statusCallback('⚠️ <model> failed, trying next...'); all fail -> throw "All models failed. Last error: <msg>". Returns { text, model }.
- callGeminiWithFallback(apiKey, prompt, imageBase64Data, mimeType, statusCallback, isJson): body { contents:[{parts:[{text:prompt}(,{inline_data:{mime_type:mimeType, data:<base64-after-comma>}})]}] }; when isJson adds generationConfig { responseMimeType:'application/json', temperature:0.0 }. Parsing: data.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text; empty -> throw '<model> returned empty response'.
- PROMPTS ARE NOT IN THIS FILE: prompt is a caller-supplied parameter (ingestion prompts live elsewhere, e.g. app.js). storage.js transports and repairs responses.
- cleanAndParseJson(rawText): strips ```json / ``` fences; PARSE-CLEAN-FIRST - JSON.parse; if any decoded string recursively matches control-char class [\x08 \x0c \t \r] (form-feed/backspace/tab/CR - fingerprint of Gemini bare-backslash corruption: a raw single-backslash frac command decoding to formfeed+"rac"), re-parse with preEscape(sanitized), where preEscape doubles backslashes before b/f/n/r/t/u macro starts: regex /(^|[^\\])\\([bfnrtu])(?=[a-zA-Z])/g replaced by '$1\\\\$2'. Clean parse THROWS -> fallback parses preEscape(...) then additionally doubles every bare backslash not part of a valid JSON escape (regex /\\([^"\\\/bfnrtu])/g). Final failure -> throw "Unable to parse JSON: <msg>". Documented limitation: backslash-n-eq corruption indistinguishable from a real newline (whole-document check by design; the "\n"+Capital artifact healed later by repairLatex rule 3).
- Deterministic LaTeX repair (runs at EVERY ingest and again on EVERY cold load via _repairQuestionBank; idempotent). LATEX_UNICODE_MAP: U+2260 and U+2262 -> \neq; U+2264 -> \le; U+2265 -> \ge; U+00B1 -> \pm; U+00D7 -> \times; U+221E -> \infty; U+2261 -> \equiv; U+2192 -> \to; degree sign -> \degree; micro sign -> \text{μ}.
  repairLatex(s) rules in order:
    1. Unicode glyphs -> LaTeX commands first (so introduced backslashes precede letters and survive rule 2).
    2. Row-break repair, lookbehind-FREE (Safari <16.4 SyntaxError guard): regex /(^|[^\\])\\(?=[\s\d-])/g -> prefix + doubled backslash - lone backslash before space/digit/hyphen is a collapsed row separator; already-doubled untouched; backslash-before-letter never touched.
    3. Literal \n before a CAPITAL letter -> real newline.
    4. KaTeX env normalization: begin/end of align|equation|eqnarray|gather|flalign|multline (starred too) -> aligned (KaTeX lacks the others; renders env name as red raw source otherwise).
  mathOk(s): renders every math segment ($$..$$, dollar-delimited inline, \[..\], \(..\) ) through window.katex.renderToString(body,{throwOnError:true}); ALSO validates bare begin/end environment blocks OUTSIDE delimiters (delimiter spans masked in a boolean array; depth-matched scan finds the matching end). Returns true trivially when window.katex absent/not loaded (async watchdog) or s empty/non-string. Non-blocking: callers flag rows instead of aborting ingest.
- cropImageFromBBox(originalDataUrl, bbox): bbox {x,y,w,h} FRACTIONS of image size; canvas crop; resolves PNG data URL; img.onerror resolves null (previously hung spinner forever).

A.5 Daily history ledger (permanent)
--------------------------------------
- DAILY_SOLVED_LEDGER = 'jeemax_solved_by_date_v1': OBJECT map dateKey(YYYY-MM-DD local) -> { date, physics, chemistry, maths, count }. Never pruned.
- Mirror 'jeemax_daily_history': SORTED ARRAY [{date, physics, chemistry, maths, count}] written by getDailyHistory() and by the cloud dailyHistory merge.
- persistSolvedByDate(): (1) rebuilds dates from bank - q.status==='solved' timestamps read as q.lastReviewedAt || q.solvedAt || q.ts || q.date, bucketed formatDateKey, subject normSubjKey; (2) folds TODAY'S live solved counters via Math.max; (3) merges into stored ledger per-subject Math.max over union of dates; recomputes count = phys+chem+maths; writes ledger; returns it.
- getSolvedByDate(): raw idbGet, {} on failure.
- settleDayCounters(dateKey) (export): Math.max-folds CURRENT live counters into ONE day entry, count recomputed. Called by midnight rollover with YESTERDAY'S key BEFORE zeroing (so a 23:59:59 solve whose save lands late is credited).
- getDailyHistory(): persistSolvedByDate() -> sorted array -> writes 'jeemax_daily_history' -> caches window._dailyHistoryCache -> returns array.
- updateDailyHistory(): alias for getDailyHistory(); invoked after EVERY save commit and after cloud merges.
- Consumers: cloud payloads (dailyHistory field in both upload paths), loadStateFromCloud merge (A.3), window._dailyHistoryCache (external readers), IDB mirror array.
Date utilities: formatDateKey(date) manual ICU-safe local YYYY-MM-DD (avoids en-CA ICU variance); todayLocalKey(date); normSubjKey(s) lowercases/trims, maps 'math'/'mathematics'->'maths', unknown->'physics' (_SUBJ_KEYS ['physics','chemistry','maths']); monthNamesCal January..December.
changeCount(subject, delta): canonicalizes via normSubjKey (raw mixed-case subject would NaN-poison), solved[key]=max(0,current+delta), saveAllAsync(), hot-reloads UI (_ui updateUI/updateStudyTimeHeader/renderGraph/renderErrorMatrixFromBank).

A.6 Targets config
--------------------
- Defaults: baseTargets {10,10,10}; baseErrorTargets {5,5,5}; AppState.activeTargets seeded 10/10/10 runtime-only.
- Persistence: solve targets IDB keys 'basePhys','baseChem','baseMath' (integers); error targets 'baseErrPhys','baseErrChem','baseErrMath' (written EVERY save; loaded with parseInt guarded by !== null). Reflected into inputs #set-tgt-phys/-chem/-math and #set-err-phys/-chem/-math.
- Lock mechanic: IDB key 'jeeTargetLockDate'. On load, if stored and (now - lockDate) < 1 day -> _ui('lockTargetsOnly') (action injected from settings/ui modules via registerUiCallbacks). storage.js READS this key; writers live elsewhere.
- NOTE: these are IndexedDB keys. storage.js contains ZERO localStorage usage - all persistence is IDB (+ image vault + Drive).

A.7 Complete key inventory (storage.js)
-----------------------------------------
IndexedDB keys (store 'storage'):
'jeemax_question_bank'; 'jeemax_image_cache' (IMAGE_CACHE_KEY); 'jeemax_chapters'; 'jeemax_solved'; 'jeemax_study_secs'; 'jeemax_bounty' (write-only here); 'bounty_data' (read+write duplicate); 'jeemax_mood_multiplier'; 'jeemax_elo'; 'jeemax_elo_updated_at'; 'jeemax_practice_mode'; 'jeemax_hardcore_daily'; 'jeemax_username'; 'jeemax_profile_pic' (conditional write + read); 'gemini_api_key' (read; written by settings); 'jeeTargetLockDate' (read); 'basePhys','baseChem','baseMath' (read); 'baseErrPhys','baseErrChem','baseErrMath' (read+write); 'jeemax_drive_token' (write/read/remove); 'jeemax_cloud_tombstones'; 'jeemax_solved_by_date_v1'; 'jeemax_daily_history' (array mirror).
localStorage: NONE in storage.js.
window.* written: __jmaxPersistFailed, __jmaxDataDirty, _dailyHistoryCache.
Events consumed: window 'pagehide', document 'visibilitychange'(hidden) -> flushSaves; BroadcastChannel 'jmax-tab-sync' posts+listens (message type 'jmax-saved').
DOM ids touched: #btn-drive-auth, #drive-status, #sync-sub-text, #manual-sync-btn, #display-username, #set-username, #display-pfp, #gemini-key, #loading-overlay, #loading-text, #set-tgt-phys/-chem/-math, #set-err-phys/-chem/-math, #jmax-persist-banner.

A.8 Exported surface of storage.js (one line each)
----------------------------------------------------
- registerUiCallbacks(callbacks): inject {lockTargetsOnly, updateUI, updateStudyTimeHeader, renderGraph, renderErrorMatrixFromBank}.
- DB_NAME / DB_VERSION constants; openDB(); idbSet/idbSetMany/idbGet/idbGetMany/idbRemove primitives.
- IMAGE_CACHE_KEY constant; persistImageCacheIfChanged(); hydrateImageCache().
- AppState / baseTargets / baseErrorTargets / solved / studySecs singletons.
- formatDateKey(date)/todayLocalKey(date)/normSubjKey(s)/monthNamesCal.
- changeCount(subject,delta): counter mutate + save + hot reload.
- SR_FRICTION_WEIGHTS {PERFECT:1.20, CALC:0.85, FORMULA:0.60, CONCEPT:0.35, APPROACH:0.15}; SR_FRICTION_LABELS (Perfect Execution / Calculation Error / Formula-Property Lapse / Conceptual Gap / Application-Approach Blank); SR_AUTONOMY_SCORES {independent:1.0, hint_used:0.5, solution_read:0.0}; SR_FRICTION_TYPES enum array.
- ELO_BANDS: T1_FOUNDATION[800,1099] T2_CORE_MAINS[1100,1299] T3_STD_MAINS[1300,1549] T4_ADV_EASY[1550,1799] T5_PAPER_ADV[1800,2099] T6_ELITE[2100,2299] T7_OLYMP[2300,2550]; getEloBand(qElo) -> band key or null (<800/>2550/non-number).
- BAND_TARGET_TIME {T1:2,T2:3,T3:5,T4:7,T5:9,T6:11,T7:15} minutes.
- ELO_GEM_STAMP_TUNING {K_user:32, K_q:6, misfireExtraMult:1.4, timeMin:0.5, physicsTauBuffer:0.85, chemistrySlowThreshold:1.25, chemistrySlowPenalty:0.4, ceiling:2999.99} - tuning for app.js calculateEloMigration calibrated branch; canonical updates rawSubjectDelta = K_user*(S - P_win)*timeMult; qEloDrift = K_q*((1-S) - P_q_win); P_win = 1/(1+10^((Q-E)/400)).
- CALIBRATED_SOLVE_THRESHOLD = 1 (solves before an 'uncalibrated' qElo is trusted/'learned').
- PRACTICE_MODES ['standard','flow','hardcore'].
- MODE_TUNING: standard {wins/loss/escrow multipliers 1.0, winSweetSpot 0.8, lossFastGrace 0.5}; flow {PwinMin 0.75, PwinMax 0.85, PwinFallback 0.65-0.90, mults 1.0, winSweetSpot 0.6, lossFastGrace 0.5, label '🎯 FLOW STATE'}; hardcore {PwinMin 0.35, PwinMax 0.50, PwinFallback 0.20-0.65, minQeloFloor 1800, winsMultiplier 1.8, lossMultiplier 0.6, escrowBonusMultiplier 2.0, winSweetSpot 0.45, lossFastGrace 0.3, capPerDay 8, label '⚡ HARDCORE / OVERCLOCK'}. P_win windows: Flow ~= qElo [userElo+50, userElo+120]; Hardcore ~= [userElo+300, userElo+500].
- calculateFrictionWeight(frictionTypes): Wf = MIN selected weight; empty/unknown -> 0.60.
- calculatePerformanceQ(autonomy,timeSpentMins,targetTimeMins): q = clamp(A*3.0 + max(0, 2.0 - Rt), 0, 5); Rt = time/target, target<=0 -> Rt=1.0.
- calculateNewEaseFactor(currentEF,q): EF' = EF + (0.1 - (5-q)*(0.08 + (5-q)*0.02)); adjustment floored at 0 when q>=3.0 (correct NEVER lowers EF); floor 1.3.
- calculateNextInterval(currentInterval,result,newEF,Wf): correct: I=0->1 d, I=1->3 d, else ceil(I*EF*max(Wf,0.35)) (SR_MIN_GROWTH_WEIGHT=0.35 lets APPROACH cards reach mastery); incorrect: max(1, floor(I*Wf)).
- computeSR(question,attempt): master pipeline; corrupt-input guards (interval NaN->0, EF NaN->2.5, target<=0->5); nextReviewAt = now + In days ISO; isMastered = In>30 && EF>2.5 && correct; returns {newInterval, newEaseFactor(3dp), performanceQ(2dp), frictionWeight, nextReviewAt, isMastered}.
- getDueStatus(question): {status,label,daysUntil}: mastered override '💤 Mastered'/Infinity; invalid date -> 'ready'; daysUntil = ceil((next-now)/day); <=0 'ready' (🟢 Ready); <=3 'due_soon' (⏳ Due in Nd); else 'scheduled' (📅 Due in Nd); nextReviewAt falls back to createdAt.
- formatSRDate(iso): en-IN, 2-digit day/short month/numeric year/2-digit hh:mm.
- migrateQuestionBankSR(): backfill per question - currentInterval 0, easeFactor 2.5, targetTimeMins 5, isMastered false, nextReviewAt now, historyLogs [], cap historyLogs 30, qElo 1200, isAnomaly false, qEloSource 'uncalibrated', qEloStampedBy/qEloStampedAt null, solveCount 0, lastSolvedAt null, skips 0, lastSkippedAt null, skipReasons [], modeRetired false, stampBatchSuspiciousDistribution/Stdev false, tags []; saves if dirty.
- MODEL_FALLBACK/CLIENT_ID/SCOPES; waitForDriveToken(cb); handleDriveAuth(); handleAuthExpiry().
- cleanAndParseJson(rawText); repairLatex(s); mathOk(s); callGeminiWithFallback(...); cropImageFromBBox(dataUrl,bbox).
- showLoading(msg)/hideLoading(): overlay 'active' class, 10 s auto-hide hang watchdog; readFileAsBase64(file).
- escapeHtml(str) (& < > " ') ; escapeAttribute(str) (& " ' < >).
- formatTime(sec) MM:SS; formatStudyDuration(secs) 'Hh Mm'/'Mm'.
- saveAllAsync()/flushSaves(); loadDataAsync().
- uploadMediaToDrive/fetchMediaFromDrive/cacheAllDriveImages/deleteMediaFromDrive/initDrive/setupSyncHeartbeat/isDriveTokenValid/initializeCloudFolder/getCloudSolvedTotal/executeUnifiedSync/recordCloudTombstone/syncStateToCloud(force)/loadStateFromCloud(isBackground).
- persistSolvedByDate/getSolvedByDate/settleDayCounters(dateKey)/getDailyHistory/updateDailyHistory.
Internal notable: _broadcastTabSaved/_listenTabSync, _bankSig trio, image-signature helpers, _reportPersistFailure, _commitCoalescedSave/_doSaveAll, _repairQuestionBank, _mergeEloFromCloud, tombstone helpers; CLOUD_PUSH_THROTTLE_MS=30000; TAB_SYNC_CHANNEL='jmax-tab-sync'.

=======================================================
B. matrix.js - spaced repetition UI + error vault (2352 lines)
=======================================================
Imports from './storage.js': AppState, saveAllAsync, changeCount, fetchMediaFromDrive, formatTime, waitForDriveToken, escapeHtml, studySecs, computeSR, getDueStatus, SR_FRICTION_TYPES, SR_FRICTION_LABELS, SR_FRICTION_WEIGHTS, formatSRDate, recordCloudTombstone, normSubjKey. No circular deps - app.js features reached via window.* bridges.

B.1 Error vault data model
----------------------------
An error entry = question object in AppState.questionBank with q.errorReason set AND q.status in ('error'|'wrong'|'solved') - that predicate gates matrix render, decay grid, dashboard, queue, folder counts.
Fields consumed/written by matrix.js:
- Identity/meta: id (string|number; percent-encoded in inline handlers via _jsId), subject (canonicalized normSubjKey/_normSubj), chapter (drawer fallback 'Unknown'; grid/manual fallback 'Uncategorized').
- Content: extractedText, hint, solution, options[] (MCQ), correctAnswer string OR array (array => multi-select MCQ), type ('mcq'|'text'|other).
- Media: imageDataUrl, diagramImageUrl, driveImageId, driveDiagramId, optionImageUrls{letter:url}, solutionImageUrl (>100-char threshold for 'real image').
- Status: status ('error'|'wrong'|'solved'), firstAttemptResult (locked ONLY on very first practice log), errorReason (card tag vocabulary 'calculation'|'conceptual'|'misread').
- SR: currentInterval (days), easeFactor, nextReviewAt ISO, targetTimeMins, isMastered, historyLogs[] (cap 30).
- qElo schema: qElo (default 1200), qEloSource ('uncalibrated'|'learned'|'gem-stamped'), qEloStampedBy/qEloStampedAt, solveCount, lastSolvedAt.
- Mode/skip ledger: skips, lastSkippedAt, skipReasons[], modeRetired; anti-cheat flags stampBatchSuspiciousDistribution/stampBatchSuspiciousStdev; tags[].
historyLogs[i] shape (submitPracticeLog): { id:'log-'+Date.now(), timestamp ISO, result 'correct'|'incorrect', autonomy, frictionTypes JSON STRING, timeSpentMins rounded 1 dp, performanceQ, newInterval, newEaseFactor }. (_parseFrictionTypes also accepts legacy raw arrays.)
Manual-add template (addErrorBlock): id 'err-manual-'+Date.now(), extractedText "Manual Logged Friction Point", options [], correctAnswer "", type "text", status 'error', timeTaken 0, solution "", SR defaults (interval 0, EF 2.5, nextReviewAt now, target 5 m, mastered false, logs []), qElo 1200/qEloSource 'uncalibrated'/stamps null/solveCount 0.
Friction reasons enum: SR_FRICTION_TYPES ['PERFECT','CALC','FORMULA','CONCEPT','APPROACH']; labels Perfect Execution / Calculation Error / Formula-Property Lapse / Conceptual Gap / Application-Approach Blank; weights 1.20/0.85/0.60/0.35/0.15.
Dominant-friction -> card tag mapping (submitPracticeLog): severity weights {PERFECT:5, CALC:4, FORMULA:3, CONCEPT:2, APPROACH:1}; dominant = LOWEST-weight selection; mapping PERFECT->calculation, CALC->calculation, FORMULA->conceptual, CONCEPT->conceptual, APPROACH->misread. TAG_STYLES colors: calculation #f59e0b, conceptual #f87171, misread #a78bfa (rgba(...,0.1) backgrounds); TAG_LABELS Calculation Error / Conceptual Gap / Misread Constraint.

B.2 SM-2 variant - exact formulas/constants
----------------------------------------------
Engine lives in storage.js (matrix imports computeSR et al.); constants in A.8. Math summary:
- Wf = min(selected friction weights); empty -> 0.60.
- q = clamp(A*3.0 + max(0, 2.0 - timeSpentMins/targetTimeMins), 0, 5); autonomy A in {1.0 independent, 0.5 hint_used, 0.0 solution_read}; speed bonus decays linearly with time ratio Rt.
- EF' = EF + (0.1 - (5-q)(0.08 + (5-q)*0.02)); adjustment floored at 0 for q>=3.0; global floor 1.3; default 2.5.
- Intervals: correct 0->1 d, 1->3 d, else ceil(I*EF'*max(Wf,0.35)); incorrect max(1, floor(I*Wf)). Mastery: I>30 && EF>2.5 && correct.
- Severity weighting effect: growth multiplier makes APPROACH cards ~8x slower than PERFECT (0.15 vs 1.20; growth floor 0.35); lapses compress interval with raw weight, minimum 1 day.

B.3 Chapter health model
--------------------------
_matrixChapterHealthContinuous(questions) - local mirror of app.js _getChapterHealth ('Continuous Non-Linear Biological Memory Construct', Bjork New Theory of Disuse):
- Per-question inputs: easeFactor (default 2.5), qElo (default 1200, must be >0), lastReviewedAt (JIT hydration: latest historyLogs[].timestamp; else solved -> now-86400000 ms; else error/wrong -> now; else now).
- Formula: S_i = max(0.5, easeFactor); RS_i(t) = exp(-ln2 * deltaDays / S_i); health = (sum qElo_i*RS_i)/(sum qElo_i) * 100; clamped [10,100]; empty set -> 50.
- Surfaced: (a) Chapter Decay Grid bars+% ; (b) window.getChapterHealth(subject, chapter) bridge (filters vault questions of that subject+chapter; exposed so Daily Briefing ordering matches the grid); (c) indirectly Elo migration via window._getChapterHealth (app.js bridge) with benign fallback 50 in _applyResult.

B.4 Due status logic + counters/badges
----------------------------------------
- Source: getDueStatus (storage.js A.8): <=0 d ready, <=3 d due_soon, else scheduled, mastered override; invalid date degrades to 'ready' instead of 'Due in NaNd'.
- Board usage: card attribute data-sr-status; groups ordered EM_STATUS_ORDER {ready:0, due_soon:1, scheduled:2, mastered:3} with headers EM_GROUP_META (🟢 'Due Now - act on these today'; ⏳ 'Due Soon - within 3 days'; 🗓️ 'Later - parked in memory'; 💤 'Mastered - resting, no action needed'); within group sort ascending easeFactor. Filter pills match statuses; badge labels _dueLabel: 'Due now' / 'Due in Nd' / 'In Nd' / 'Mastered'.
- O(1) counters/TTLs: NO TTL badge cache exists in either file. Present mechanisms: (a) storage bumps window.__jmaxDataDirty after every commit (dirty signal for memoized nav rings/badges, consumed elsewhere); (b) submitPracticeLog calls window.__ckBumpTodayFix(subject) on correct commits so the CK engine's 'fixed today' ring updates O(1) rather than rescanning logs on its 1 s tick; (c) rollover watcher refreshErrorDashboardIfStale: setInterval 60000 ms + visibilitychange + focus, gated by _lastRenderedDate (date-based staleness, not TTL); (d) daily-queue snapshot caching is day-scoped. _todayKeyCache (line 2235) declared but never used (dead variable).

B.5 Daily Core/Fix Queue curation
-----------------------------------
- Caps DAILY_QUEUE_LIMITS = { physics:5, maths:5, chemistry:10 }.
- Snapshot locked ONCE per LOCAL day; solving marks done, pulls NO replacement until tomorrow. Three layers: (0) memory _dailyQueueSnapshot {date, ids[]}; (1) localStorage 'jeemax_daily_queue_snapshot' storing {date:'YYYY-MM-DD', ids:[string ids]} - survives refreshes, corrupt payload warns + regenerates; (2) fresh pipeline.
- Pipeline: candidates = items with errorReason && status in {error, solved, wrong}; STRICT filter getDueStatus(q).status === 'ready' (isolates from scheduled/due_soon leakage; malformed q excluded defensively); bucket by normSubjKey; EACH SUBJECT sorted ASCENDING easeFactor (weakest recall first, default 2.5); slice caps; concatenated order physics -> maths -> chemistry. NO additional weights and NO paper-distribution slicing exist in this version.
- Completion: _isCompletedToday(q) = some historyLog with result 'correct' whose local-date(timestamp) === today.
- UI: toggleDailyQueue() flips _dailyQueueActive; activateDailyQueue() forces ON (Daily Briefing landing). Active: #daily-queue-btn 'active', #daily-queue-badge inline, .subject-folder opacity 0.35, status pills cleared, title '⚡ Daily Core Queue'. _renderDailyQueueCards(): empty -> "Queue's clear" empty state (bolt icon, button 'Back to Matrix' -> toggleDailyQueue()); else per-subject divider rows (.daily-queue-subject-divider[data-subject]; icons ⚛️ Physics / 📐 Maths / 🧪 Chemistry) with track bar width pct-done, text 'X/Y done · Z to go' or '✓ complete', plus 'N tracked' all-time count; cards via _buildErrorCardHTML with class 'daily-queue-done' injected when completed; initErrorLazyLoaders() rewired. filterErrors() routes here while active. openErrorMatrix() deactivates queue on subject switch (restores folders, 'all' pill, #filter-status='all', hides badge).

B.6 Chapter Decay Grid rendering
----------------------------------
renderChapterDecayGrid() -> container #chapter-decay-grid.
- Data: vault questions grouped by key subject+'||'+chapter (fallback 'Uncategorized'); per chapter: questionCount, avgEF (mean easeFactor default 2.5), health via _matrixChapterHealthContinuous; sorted WEAKEST FIRST. Empty -> centered muted message 'No chapter data available yet. Log errors to see decay analysis.'
- Responsive geometry (viewBox width == container px width, crisp 1:1 text): cw = max(220, clientWidth || 600); compact cw<440; tight cw<560; SHOW_META cw>520; ROW_H 38, PAD 4, LEFT 10, gap G 12, PCT_W 44, META_W 116, RIGHT 8; LABEL_W 84/120/168; TRACK_H 18, TRACK_R 5; trackW = max(40, cw - LEFT - LABEL_W - 2G - PCT_W - (meta ? G+META_W : 0) - RIGHT); svgH = chapters*38 + 8.
- Rows: label truncated to maxName 10/16/24 chars + ellipsis; background track rgba(255,255,255,0.035) stroke rgba(255,255,255,0.06); fill width max(3, health% * trackW); tiers - health >75 var(--glow-green) + glow filter url(#decay-glow-green) (feGaussianBlur stdDeviation 3.5 + flood #22c55e 0.45 merge), 45-75 var(--glow-yellow), <45 var(--glow-red) opacity 0.88; percent text Space Grotesk 12 px bold; meta cell 'Nq · EF x.xx' (Plus Jakarta Sans 10px) only when SHOW_META; fill CSS transition width 0.6s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1). One innerHTML write.

B.7 SR practice drawer (full UX + submit pipeline)
----------------------------------------------------
openPracticeDrawer(qId): resolves percent-encoded or raw ids (_findQByHandlerId); closes prior drawer; seeds _drawerState {qId, targetTimeMins: q.targetTimeMins||5, stopwatchSeconds 0, ...}. Builds fullscreen overlay div.sr-practice-overlay#sr-practice-overlay (backdrop click closes) containing div.sr-practice-drawer.sr-practice-modal#sr-drawer-<encoded-id> role=dialog aria-modal=true:
- Lifeline ribbon #sr-lifeline-ribbon (shown on rAF if window.__lifeline.getStatus().active): '🌊 Lifeline active for 1× solve. Aim 80%+ first.' + dismiss calling window.__lifeline.dismissForCurrentSolve().
- Header: title '<Chapter> · Practice'; sub '<subject> · <dueLabel>'; actions: streak visualizer #sr-streak-visualizer with canvas#sr-streak-canvas (16x16), Elo slot #sr-elo-header-slot, optional 👁 Hide Image btn (#sr-hide-img-btn), close ✕ (closePracticeDrawer()).
- Question stage #sr-question-stage: media block #sr-question-media - inline data-URL img OR Drive-lazy img (#sr-question-img with data-drive-id/data-qid, encoded SVG 'Loading image…' placeholder 300x180); diagram block label '📐 Diagram' (#sr-question-diagram-img, click -> window.openLightbox); question text .latex #sr-question-text or 'No question text on file'; optional hint block (#sr-hint-toggle -> srToggleHint toggles #sr-hint-body WITHOUT revealing the answer; aligns with hint_used tag).
- Answer stage #sr-answer-stage (_renderAnswerStage): MCQ -> label 'Select your answer' or 'Select all that apply' (multi iff Array.isArray(correctAnswer)); options .sr-mcq-option[data-letter][data-option] with letter chip (.sr-mcq-letter), KaTeX-hydrated text (.sr-mcq-text), optional per-option figure from q.optionImageUrls[_optionLetter]; #sr-confirm-btn 'Confirm Answer' disabled until >=1 selection. Non-MCQ WITH answer -> '🔍 Reveal Answer' btn (#sr-reveal-answer-btn -> srRevealAnswer swaps in 'Correct answer: X. Did you get it right?' + self-report pair; KaTeX hydrated on the strong element only). No answer on file -> immediate self-report 'No answer on file - were you correct?' with ✔ Yes-correct / ✖ No-incorrect buttons.
- ANSWER TYPES ACCEPTED: single MCQ letter; multi MCQ letters (array answers); free-response self-report after reveal; no-answer self-report. Auto-grade (srConfirmAnswer): sorted selected letters deep-equal resolveMcqCorrectLetters(q).
- resolveMcqCorrectLetters(q) (export) priority order: (1) array entries with leading letter prefix '(B)'/'B)'/'B.'/'B:' or bare single letters; (2) bare letter list 'B', 'B, C', 'B C', 'B and C'; (3) compact 'AB'/'ACD' (2-4 letters); (4) leading prefix on a string like 'B) ...'; (5) fallback EXACT normalized text match vs option texts (each option's own prefix stripped). Every candidate letter validated against options.length (the I inside a fraction like \frac{I}{4} cannot become option I on a 4-option MCQ); no options -> [].
- Tagging stage #sr-tag-stage (revealed AFTER result): divider 'Now log your attempt ↓'; Autonomy Level pill group [data-group=autonomy]: 🧠 Independent / 💡 Hint Used / 📖 Soln Read (srSetAutonomy); Friction Type pills .sr-friction-pill one per SR_FRICTION_TYPES (multi-toggle srToggleFriction); Time Spent row: stopwatch #sr-stopwatch-btn with #sr-stopwatch-display MM:SS + pulse dot #sr-pulse-dot (srToggleStopwatch), Manual toggle #sr-manual-toggle + number input #sr-manual-input (min 0 step 0.5) + unit span (srToggleManualTime/srUpdateManualTime), static 'Target: <N>m'.
- Moment of truth (_applyResult - fired by Confirm/self-report, NOT deferred to Log Attempt): freezes stopwatch (resultLocked=true; frozenTimeMins = stopwatchSeconds/60; pulse dot hidden); result banner into #sr-result-zone (✅ Correct/❌ Incorrect + answer rendered via window.answerMathHTML when auto-graded; '(self-reported)' suffix otherwise; auto-cropped solution figure q.solutionImageUrl beneath); marks MCQ options .correct-mark/.wrong-mark + disables pointer events; hides #sr-confirm-btn (or whole answer stage for pure self-report).
  Gamification: incorrect -> window.triggerRedFlash() + window.playWrongSound(); 20% chance window.triggerStreakShield() ELSE AppState.practiceCorrectStreak = 0. Correct -> practiceCorrectStreak++; branch order: window._justWonBounty flag (consume; showNormalGlow) -> body.overheat-active (changeCount(normSubjKey(subject), 2) + showSupercharged + deactivateOverheat) -> AppState.bounty.payoffCount > 0 (decrement + saveAllAsync + showSupercharged) -> default showNormalGlow + playCorrectSound + 15% chance showSupercharged. Then window.updateStreakVisualizer().
  Elo: if window.calculateEloMigration exists and q.subject - calls window.calculateEloMigration(subject, actualSeconds = round(frozenTimeMins*60), score = correct?1:0, health = window._getChapterHealth(subject, chapter) or 50, q); stashes result on _drawerState.eloResult; saveAllAsync() IMMEDIATELY (Elo mutation persisted before submit). Injects persistent header chip .elo-header-chip.elo-up/.elo-down into #sr-elo-header-slot: sign + Math.round(deltaSubject) plus '[' + window.getRankTierDetails(newSubjectElo).name + ']'. If tierChanged: window.burstEmojis(drawer center x/y, 40, ['🎉','😄','🔥','✨','🥳','🎊','💯','🌟','😎','🏆'], 1.6) + window.playSuperSound(). Deferred window.renderEloMatrix() via setTimeout 0 (protects flash/glow frames).
- Footer: live summary #sr-footer-summary (result word colored, autonomy text, friction count, minutes 1 dp) + #sr-submit-btn 'Log Attempt' disabled until result && autonomy && frictionTypes.length > 0; timeSpent >= 0 (0-second answers allowed).
submitPracticeLog() pipeline (updates, in order):
  1. Locks q.firstAttemptResult = result ONLY if no prior historyLogs AND no existing value (accuracy counts first attempts only).
  2. computeSR(q, {result, autonomy, frictionTypes copy, timeSpentMins rounded 1 dp}); timeSpent = manual minutes OR stopwatchSeconds/60.
  3. Pushes historyLog (shape B.1); caps logs at 30.
  4. Correct -> window.__ckBumpTodayFix(subject) (guarded O(1) CK ring bump).
  5. Writes q.currentInterval/newEaseFactor/nextReviewAt/isMastered from srResult.
  6. Dominant friction -> q.errorReason remap (mapping B.1).
  7. Status: correct && status!=='solved' -> q.status='solved' + changeCount(normSubjKey(subject), 1); incorrect -> q.status='error'.
  8. Study time injection: seconds = round(timeSpent*60); if >0 && subject - alias-normalized key (math/mathematics/trailing-space variants -> maths) must exist in studySecs -> studySecs[key]+=seconds; window.updateStudyTimeHeader().
  9. saveAllAsync(); closePracticeDrawer().
  10. Double-rAF then _staggeredChain([renderErrorMatrixFromBank, (filterErrors + renderErrorResolutionDashboard + window.renderMomentumCandles), window.renderEloMatrix]) - heavy rebuilds in own macrotasks with rAF yields so drawer-close animation, flashes, streak flame, Elo chip animate cleanly.
closePracticeDrawer(): pauses stopwatch; resets _drawerState (incl. resultSource 'auto'|'self', selectedOptions[], imageHidden); removes overlay (chip goes with it).
Other exported handlers: srSelectOption(el) (single vs multi selection logic + confirm gating), srConfirmAnswer(), srSelfReport(result), srRevealAnswer(), srToggleImage() (toggles whole #sr-question-media; button text Show/Hide Image), srToggleHint(), srSetResult(value) (legacy [data-group=result] toggle - current tag stage renders no such buttons), srSetAutonomy, srToggleFriction, srToggleStopwatch (ignored once resultLocked), srToggleManualTime, srUpdateManualTime(val).
Stopwatch: setInterval 1000 ms increments _drawerState.stopwatchSeconds, repaints display; started on drawer open; permanently frozen at decision instant.

B.8 Error resolution dashboard / momentum
-------------------------------------------
renderErrorResolutionDashboard(): maintains ONLY the vault-rail squashed-today readout - scans all questions' historyLogs counting logs with result 'correct' and local date === today, bucketed by normSubjKey; writes #rail-today-total and #rail-today-physics/-chemistry/-maths, toggling .is-zero (a zero day must not glow). Comment records that the old stage-top strip (per-subject bars + 15-DAY SPARKLINE) was REMOVED; the plain sparkline survives only as intermediate data carrier that app.js candlestick renderer window.renderMomentumCandles() reads back and REPLACES (re-chained after every dashboard render so candles never regress to sparkline). Rollover watcher _startRolloverWatcher (once per page): _lastRenderedDate=today; setInterval(refreshErrorDashboardIfStale, 60000); document visibilitychange(visible) + window focus also refresh. refreshErrorDashboardIfStale() re-renders ONLY when local date changed, then re-chains renderMomentumCandles. Window length: none computed in this file anymore (15-day sparkline exists only in comments).

B.9 Chapter progress list (dashboard card)
--------------------------------------------
renderChapterProgressList() -> #chapter-progress-list. Rows from registry AppState.chapters[physics|chemistry|maths]: key = normSubjKey(subject)+'::'+encodeURIComponent(chapter) (old subject+'||'+chapter join corrupted pairing); totals = all bank questions per key; solvedCounts = status==='solved'; SELF-HEAL adds rows for orphaned bank chapters missing from registry. pct = round(solved/total*100), 0 when total 0. Sort ascending pct, then descending total, then name localeCompare - weakest/untouched first. MAX_ROWS = 10; overflow footer '+ N more · weakest first'. Row markup: .cp-row (.cp-row-void at 0%), .cp-chip glyph P/C/M, .cp-name, .cp-bar with .cp-fill (.cp-fill-mid >=50%, .cp-fill-full at 100%), .cp-meta 'solved/total', .cp-pct (.cp-pct-void at 0%). Click -> openChapterProgress(subj, encodedName) (export): decode name; AppState.currentSubject = subj; window.openChapterDetail(name); window.switchTab('practice', nav element '[data-tab="practice"]'). Empty -> 'No chapters yet. Add one in Grind Station to start the grind.'
Related: getLowestHealthQuestion() (export, Pillar-2 Checkpoint lockdown): among vault questions with getDueStatus 'ready' returns lowest-easeFactor one; none due -> lowest EF across ENTIRE bank; null only when bank empty.

B.10 Elo migration entry points
---------------------------------
Definitions are NOT in these files - window.calculateEloMigration, window._getChapterHealth, window.getRankTierDetails, window.renderEloMatrix are app.js-provided. matrix.js CONSUMES:
- window.calculateEloMigration(subject, actualSeconds, score, health, question) -> object; matrix reads .deltaSubject (number), .newSubjectElo (number), .tierChanged (boolean). Called once per attempt in _applyResult with the FROZEN decision time; try/catch (fault logged; eloResult null -> no chip/celebration).
- window._getChapterHealth(subject, chapter) -> number 0-100; fallback 50 ('benign mid-default') when bridge missing.
- Engine tuning constants are EXPORTED FROM storage.js (A.8): ELO_GEM_STAMP_TUNING, CALIBRATED_SOLVE_THRESHOLD, ELO_BANDS/getEloBand, BAND_TARGET_TIME, MODE_TUNING, PRACTICE_MODES.
matrix.js DEFINES two bridges: window.getChapterHealth(subject, chapter) (same continuous health model as the grid; Daily Briefing) and window._getDailyQueueSnapshot() (locked queue ids in priority order; [] on failure).

B.11 Keys, events, window.* bridges (matrix.js)
-------------------------------------------------
localStorage: 'jeemax_daily_queue_snapshot' = JSON {date:'YYYY-MM-DD', ids:[questionId strings]} (getItem + setItem; quota/private-mode failures warn, fall back to memory).
IndexedDB: accessed only indirectly through storage.js helpers (saveAllAsync/changeCount/recordCloudTombstone/fetchMediaFromDrive). No direct idb* calls in matrix.js.
Events: document keydown (_emKeyHandler: '/' focuses #matrix-search-input when #view-errors active and target not editable; Escape inside search clears via window.clearMatrixSearch() or blurs; wired once, guarded by window.__emKeysWired); backdrop click-to-close on #sr-practice-overlay; document visibilitychange(visible) + window focus -> refreshErrorDashboardIfStale; IntersectionObservers: lazy card images (.lazy-error-img, rootMargin '200px 0px 800px 0px', observer recreated per render, img._lazyToken race guard, off-screen imgs freed back to 140x90 encoded-SVG LAZY_IMG_PLACEHOLDER) and filter dock (.error-filters gains .emf-docked when sentinel #emf-scroll-sentinel leaves viewport, rootMargin '-1px 0px 0px 0px'). No dispatchEvent/CustomEvent anywhere.
window.* CONSUMED (all typeof-guarded): __lifeline (.getStatus/.dismissForCurrentSolve), __ckBumpTodayFix(subject), _justWonBounty, _getChapterHealth, calculateEloMigration, getRankTierDetails, renderEloMatrix, renderMomentumCandles, answerMathHTML, _wrapBareLatex, katex, triggerRedFlash, playWrongSound, playCorrectSound, playSuperSound, showNormalGlow, showSupercharged, deactivateOverheat, triggerStreakShield, updateStreakVisualizer, burstEmojis, updateStudyTimeHeader, openLightbox, clearMatrixSearch, populateAiDumpChapters, openChapterDetail, switchTab.
window.* DEFINED: getChapterHealth, _getDailyQueueSnapshot, __emKeysWired (guard flag). Exported functions attach to window via the shell's inline-handler wiring.
Key DOM ids touched: #view-errors, #error-list-container, #error-matrix-title, #daily-queue-btn, #daily-queue-badge, #filter-type, #filter-status, #filter-tag, #matrix-search-input, #matrix-meta, #em-nomatch, #emf-scroll-sentinel, #folder-count-physics/-chemistry/-maths, #err-block-<id>, #sr-history-<id>, #sr-chevron-<id>, #add-error-modal, #new-err-chapter, #new-err-type, #err-img-success, #lightbox-modal, #lightbox-img, #chapter-decay-grid, #chapter-progress-list, #rail-today-total, #rail-today-physics/-chemistry/-maths, plus drawer ids in B.7 (#sr-practice-overlay, #sr-drawer-<id>, #sr-lifeline-ribbon, #sr-streak-visualizer, #sr-streak-canvas, #sr-elo-header-slot, #sr-hide-img-btn, #sr-question-media/#sr-question-img-wrap/#sr-question-img/#sr-question-diagram-img, #sr-question-text, #sr-hint-toggle/#sr-hint-body, #sr-answer-stage, #sr-confirm-btn, #sr-reveal-answer-btn, #sr-result-zone, #sr-tag-stage, #sr-stopwatch-btn/#sr-stopwatch-display/#sr-pulse-dot, #sr-manual-toggle/#sr-manual-input/#sr-manual-unit, #sr-footer-summary, #sr-submit-btn).
Module-eval side effects: wires keyboard handler, filter dock, starts waitForDriveToken(() => initErrorLazyLoaders()).

Other behaviors worth recording:
- openErrorMatrix(subject, element): deactivates queue, manages .subject-folder active/opacity, sets AppState.currentErrorSubject lowercased, title '<Subject> Matrix', renderErrorMatrixFromBank + filterErrors. _updateFolderCounts writes per-subject vault counts into #folder-count-* and toggles .is-zero.
- filterErrors(): reads #filter-type/#filter-status/#filter-tag; per-card match on attributes data-type/data-sr-status/data-subject + .error-chapter/.error-tag text; toggles .hidden; updates #matrix-meta ('N of M shown · K due now · Clear filters button calling clearMatrixFilters'); collapses empty .em-group-head headers and rewrites their .emg-count; zero-match injects/removes #em-nomatch guidance card ('Nothing matches those filters' + Clear-all-filters button).
- removeErrorLog(id): confirm('Confirm deletion of this friction point and all its attempt history?'); removes from bank, recordCloudTombstone(id), saveAllAsync, closes drawer, staggered rebuild incl. renderChapterDecayGrid.
- _staggeredChain(tasks): sequential macrotask runner with rAF -> setTimeout(0) yields between tasks (each chunk 10-60 ms; protects compositor animations).
- Modal helpers: _openModal(id) display:flex + rAF '.active'; _closeModalStr(id) removes '.active' then display:none after 300 ms.
- Card builder extras: bounty highlight class 'bounty-active-error' when AppState.bounty active && !done && date===today && questionId matches; sr-stats-row shows interval d / ease 2 dp / target m with explanatory titles; attempt dots = last 5 logs REVERSED with tooltips "<date>\nTime: Xm\nFriction: <label>"; expanded history panel lists ALL logs newest-first with per-log EF.
- _esc/_jsId hygiene: entity escaping PLUS percent-encoding of ids inside inline JS handlers (attributes HTML-decode BEFORE handler compilation, so a quote in an id could inject JS); receivers try raw then decodeURIComponent lookup.
- _safeImgSrc(url): allows only data:image/(png|jpeg|jpg|gif|webp|svg+xml);base64, https://, blob:; escapes quotes; else ''.
- _renderKatexIn(el): wraps delimiter-less fragments via window._wrapBareLatex then renders block/inline math through katex renderToString throwOnError:false, displayMode for block forms.

B.12 Public (exported) functions of matrix.js (one line each)
---------------------------------------------------------------
- openErrorMatrix(subject, element): switch Errors view to a subject; folder/title/badge management; queue deactivation; re-render + filter.
- openPracticeDrawer(qId): build/show full-screen SR practice drawer; start stopwatch.
- closePracticeDrawer(): stop/reset drawer state; remove overlay.
- resolveMcqCorrectLetters(q): derive validated correct MCQ letters (priority rules B.7).
- srSelectOption(el): MCQ selection toggle (single/multi) + confirm gating.
- srConfirmAnswer(): grade selection vs correct letters -> auto _applyResult or self-report prompt.
- srSelfReport(result): commit self-reported correct/incorrect.
- srRevealAnswer(): reveal stored answer (+solution figure) then self-report (non-MCQ with answer on file).
- srToggleImage(): show/hide drawer media stage.
- srToggleHint(): reveal/hide hint block.
- srSetResult(value): legacy result-pill setter ([data-group=result]).
- srSetAutonomy(value): set autonomy pill.
- srToggleFriction(ft): toggle friction-type pill.
- srToggleStopwatch(): start/pause stopwatch (blocked after resultLocked).
- srToggleManualTime(): switch stopwatch <-> manual minutes input.
- srUpdateManualTime(val): parse manual minutes into drawer state.
- submitPracticeLog(): full commit pipeline (B.7).
- removeErrorLog(id): confirm-delete entry + tombstone + staggered rebuilds.
- filterErrors(): apply filters + meta/no-match/group-header maintenance; routes to queue renderer when active.
- toggleDailyQueue(): flip Daily Core Queue view.
- activateDailyQueue(): force queue ON (no-op when already active).
- addErrorBlock(): create manual error entry from add-error modal; rebuild board + decay grid.
- renderErrorMatrixFromBank(): full board render - status-grouped, urgency/EF-sorted cards, folder counts, guided empty state.
- toggleCardHistory(qId): expand/collapse attempt-history panel.
- initErrorLazyLoaders(): (re)create card-image IntersectionObserver (load near viewport, free off-screen).
- openLightbox(src): populate #lightbox-img; open #lightbox-modal.
- renderChapterDecayGrid(): SVG chapter-health bars (B.6).
- renderChapterProgressList(): dashboard completion card (B.9).
- openChapterProgress(subj, encodedName): jump from progress card into Grind Station chapter view.
- getLowestHealthQuestion(): lowest-EF due question for Checkpoint lockdown (whole-bank fallback).
- refreshErrorDashboardIfStale(): date-rollover-aware dashboard refresh + candle re-chain.
- renderErrorResolutionDashboard(): squashed-today rail counters (B.8).

CROSS-FILE NOTES / REQUESTED ITEMS NOT PRESENT IN THESE TWO FILES
- Nav-badge O(1)/TTL caching: only hooks exist (window.__jmaxDataDirty bump in storage.js; window.__ckBumpTodayFix call in matrix.js); the cache itself lives elsewhere.
- Fix-Queue weights/paper-distribution slicing: ABSENT; curation is readiness + weakest-easeFactor + caps {P:5, M:5, C:10}.
- Gemini prompt templates: not in storage.js (prompt is a call parameter; templates belong to ingestion callers such as app.js).
- SM-2 engine, due-status derivation, qElo band/tuning tables: defined in storage.js, imported by matrix.js.
- localStorage usage: matrix.js uses exactly ONE key ('jeemax_daily_queue_snapshot'); storage.js uses NONE.

JEEMAXXING MASTER DOC — FOCUS / SOUND / EFFECTS MODULES
