A. pomodoro.js (1286 lines)
===============================================================

A.0 Module header / imports
- ES module. Imports from ./storage.js: formatTime, formatStudyDuration, saveAllAsync, studySecs. Imports GalleryBreak from ./gallery-break.js and NightGuard from ./nightguard.js.
- window.__pomodoro bridge (set at load): getTimerStartTime() -> module timerStartTime (Date.now() ms at start/resume, or null); isRunning() -> pomoState !== 'IDLE'; getConfig() -> readPomoConfig(); getLedger() -> getFocusLedger(). app.js uses isRunning() to lock the chapter-question filter while a session is live, and getConfig() for Daily Briefing pre-fill/auto-arm.

A.1 Timer state machine
- States (module var pomoState): 'IDLE', 'STUDY', 'BREAK', 'STOPWATCH'. Initial 'IDLE'.
- Other core vars: secondsLeft, totalSecondsForState, currentSession=1, totalSessions=1, studySubject='physics', isPaused=false, visualMode='bar', isStopwatchMode=false, dynamicSubject=false, timerStartTime=null, timerTotalSeconds=0, stopwatchAccumulated=0, timerEndTriggered=false, pausedElapsed=0, lastTickAt=null, lastSavedStudyMinute=0, _resetPomoTimer=null, _blockCreditedSecs=0, _pomoPendingAction=null, _blockEloSnap=null.
- SUBJECT_KEYS = ['physics','chemistry','maths']; isValidSubjectKey() gates all subject values; anything invalid falls back to 'physics'.
- Timing model: setInterval(executeTimerTick, 1000). Every tick derives elapsed from Date.now()-timerStartTime (wall clock), so background throttling cannot drift. tickDelta() returns floor((now-lastTickAt)/1000) clamped to >= 1 s for per-second credit.
- Transitions:
  - startTimer(): only from IDLE. Cancels pending _resetPomoTimer; NightGuard.logSessionStart(); syncFilterLock(); reads #pomo-subject; disables all '#view-pomodoro .pomo-input, .pomo-select' (re-enables #pomo-subject if dynamicSubject); hides #btn-start; initAudioContext() + playStartChime(); branches: stopwatch -> transitionToStopwatch(), else parses #pomo-sessions (default 1), currentSession=1, transitionToStudy(). Sets timerStartTime=Date.now(); timerEndTriggered=false; savePomoConfig() (config persists ONLY when a session actually starts, not on bare toggles).
  - transitionToStopwatch(): pomoState='STOPWATCH'; secondsLeft=0; stopwatchAccumulated=0; timerTotalSeconds=0; status "Stopwatch: SUBJ"; widget shown, data-state='study', mini-status text 'STOPWATCH' class 'mini-status study'; deep-work ON; FocusSound.autoStart(); ring set to 1 (full); btn-quit label "Stop"; progress bar width 100%.
  - transitionToStudy(): reads #pomo-study (default 50) -> timerTotalSeconds = value*60; status "Studying SUBJ (n/N)"; mini-status "STUDY n/N"; deep-work ON; _blockEloSnap=_eloSnapshot(); FocusSound.autoStart(); ring 0; mini end time updated; btn-quit "Quit" wired to quitTimer(); _blockCreditedSecs=0.
  - transitionToBreak(): reads #pomo-break (default 10) -> timerTotalSeconds=value*60; status "Break Time ☕ (n/N)"; mini-status 'BREAK' class 'mini-status break'; deep-work OFF; _blockEloSnap=null; btn-quit relabeled "Skip Break" and REBOUND from quitTimer() to skipBreak(); calls GalleryBreak.begin() (try/catch) at the very end.
  - pauseTimer(): clears interval; fire-and-forget saveAllAsync(); isPaused=true; lastTickAt=null. STOPWATCH: stopwatchAccumulated += elapsed. Countdown: pausedElapsed = elapsed (resume back-dates timerStartTime = now - pausedElapsed*1000). Deep-work OFF (body.pomo-active removed, window._pomoRunning=false); widget data-paused set; status "Stopwatch Paused"/"Timer Paused"; btn-pause becomes "Resume".
  - resumeTimer(): isPaused=false; timerEndTriggered=false; re-anchors timerStartTime (stopwatch: now; countdown: now - pausedElapsed*1000, pausedElapsed=0); restores status lines; deep-work ON again for STUDY/STOPWATCH only; restarts 1 s interval.
  - handleTimerEnd() (secondsLeft <= 0, guarded by timerEndTriggered): hides widget, deep-work OFF. STUDY branch: NightGuard.logSessionEnd(); ledger complete-block with deepSecs = clamp(_blockCreditedSecs, 0, timerTotalSeconds); hydrateFocusStats(); if currentSession < totalSessions -> popup "🍅 Focus Session Done!" icon 🧘 msg "Take a break. You earned it." pending action startBreakAfterPopup (with session receipt); else "🏁 All Focus Blocks Complete" 🎉 "Pomodoro cycle finished. Great work!" pending finishAllAfterPopup. BREAK branch: currentSession++; if GalleryBreak.isActive() -> playBell() then GalleryBreak.finish(startStudyAfterBreakPopup); else fallback popup "☕ Break Over" ⚡ "Ready for session N of M?" -> startStudyAfterBreakPopup.
  - skipBreak(): clear interval, save, GalleryBreak.abort(); if sessions remain currentSession++ + transitionToStudy() else finishAll().
  - addBreakTime(extraMinutes): only while BREAK; adds extraMinutes*60 to timerTotalSeconds AND secondsLeft; refreshes displays + mini end time. Wired to the break-option buttons inside #break-actions (e.g. "+5 min"); because target eases both ways, added time visibly re-covers the burn reveal.
  - finishAll(): clear interval, save, hide widget, display "00:00", status "All sessions complete!", then resetPomoUI().
  - resetPomoUI(): pomoState='IDLE'; unlocks #question-filter; GalleryBreak.abort(); closes modal; clears pending action and _resetPomoTimer; deep-work OFF; re-enables all .pomo-input/.pomo-select in #view-pomodoro; shows #btn-start, hides #btn-pause/#btn-quit (quit text restored to "Quit"); progress/beaker zeroed; clears timerStartTime/timerEndTriggered/pausedElapsed/lastTickAt/lastSavedStudyMinute; display: stopwatch mode -> "00:00" + "Ready to Track"; else formatTime(studyMin*60) + "Ready to Focus"; widget shown with data-state='idle'; ring 0; hydrateFocusStats(); _syncPopControls().
- Page visibility catch-up (document 'visibilitychange', when returning visible and running/not paused): STOPWATCH adds elapsed wall seconds into stopwatchAccumulated and credits them as study seconds; countdown recomputes secondsLeft from wall clock, updates timer-display/mini-time/progress/ring/GalleryBreak.setProgress, and fires handleTimerEnd synchronously (with fire-and-forget saveAllAsync first) if remaining hit 0 while away.

A.2 Config options + persistence
- Form inputs: #pomo-study (minutes, default fallback 50), #pomo-break (default 10), #pomo-sessions (rounds, default 1), #pomo-subject (select: physics/chemistry/maths), #stopwatch-toggle-btn (text On/Off; toggleStopwatchMode flips isStopwatchMode, tears down any live run via _finalizeQuit(false) — NO forfeit booked for a mere mode toggle — resyncs UI, resetPomoUI()), #dynamic-subject-btn (class 'on' toggled; title strings: "Dynamic mode ON: switch subject mid-session — time is tracked per subject" / "Dynamic mode OFF: subject is locked for the session").
- Stopwatch UI sync (_syncStopwatchUI): button green styling when on (background rgba(34,197,94,0.2), border rgba(34,197,94,0.4), color #4ade80) vs neutral (rgba(255,255,255,0.08)/0.1/#fff); hides '.pomodoro-controls .input-group' items at indices 1, 2, 3 (display none vs flex) — index 0 stays visible.
- Storage key 'jeemax_pomo_config' (POMO_CONFIG_KEY): written by savePomoConfig() at session start as JSON { subject, study, break, sessions, stopwatch, dynamic } (ints parsed from inputs with ||defaults). readPomoConfig() sanitizes: invalid subject -> 'physics'; study/break/sessions Math.max(1, int||default 50/10/1); booleans coerced. Corrupt payload -> null (never crashes boot). applyPomoConfig() writes values back onto inputs and reflects stopwatch/dynamic via pure helpers _syncStopwatchUI/_syncDynamicSubjectUI (deliberately NOT the public toggles, which have side effects); returns false when nothing stored.

A.3 Study-seconds accrual
- creditStudySeconds(subject, deltaSecs): ignores <1 s; adds into shared studySecs[subject]; updateStudyTimeHeader(); when floor(studySecs[subject]/60) exceeds lastSavedStudyMinute watermark, saves whole app via saveAllAsync() (once per whole minute per subject).
- During STUDY ticks credited = min(deltaSecs, secondsLeft) so end-frame overshoot never overcounts; also accumulates into _blockCreditedSecs (feeds ledger "deep").
- updateStudyTimeHeader(): #top-study-time shows "Hh Mm" or "Mm" for physics+chemistry+maths combined; #stat-hrs-physics/-chemistry/-maths show formatStudyDuration(per-subject seconds).
- Stopwatch mode credits deltaSecs every tick; the visibility handler credits caught-up elapsed directly.

A.4 Deep Work Focus Ledger
- localStorage key 'jeemax_focus_ledger' (FOCUS_LEDGER_KEY). Schema: { d: 'YYYY-MM-DD' (local date via _todayKey()), done:int, forfeit:int, chain:int, best:int, deep:int (seconds) }. Loader validates d === today else returns fresh zeros; corrupt JSON treated as fresh day.
- done = blocks completed to planned end (or stopwatch stops >= 300 s). forfeit = blocks abandoned mid-study. chain = consecutive completes without forfeit (resets on quit-forfeit). best = best chain today. deep = seconds actually spent in study phases (pause-excluded).
- _ledgerCompleteBlock(secsDeep): done++, chain++, best=max(best,chain), deep += max(0,secsDeep|0), save.
- _ledgerForfeit(secsDeep): forfeit++, chain=0, deep += max(0,secsDeep|0), save.
- getFocusLedger() export returns shallow copy (UI hydration/QA).
- Chain rules: quitting a live STUDY block through the confirm books a forfeit (chain -> 0). Mode toggles and non-STUDY quits pass countForfeit=false (no chain damage). STOPWATCH stop counts as a COMPLETE block only if stopwatchAccumulated >= 300 s (5 min); otherwise its seconds are added to deep silently (an earlier double-count bug is documented in comments).
- hydrateFocusStats(): fills #fs-done, #fs-deep (formatStudyDuration), #fs-chain, #fs-best, #fs-forfeit; renders #chain-pips with slot count = max(5, chain, min(best,10)); each span.chain-pip gets 'lit' when i < chain and 'tip' on i === chain-1. Runs at boot (guarded try/catch for DOM stubs) and after every ledger change/reset.

A.5 Quit-friction confirm
- quitTimer(force): no-op when IDLE. If !force && pomoState==='STUDY' && !timerEndTriggered -> requestQuitConfirm() (returns); otherwise _finalizeQuit(pomoState==='STUDY').
- requestQuitConfirm(): freezes the clock exactly like pause (interval cleared; elapsed respects already-paused state via pausedElapsed; isPaused=true; lastTickAt=null; pausedElapsed=elapsed). Sets pending action to _finalizeQuit(true). Modal: #notify-title "Break the chain?", #notify-icon "⛓️", #notify-message `${formatTime(elapsed)} of ${SUBJECT} still counts — but your ${chain}-block chain resets to zero.` Receipt rows (#notify-receipt): "Kept" = "${formatTime(elapsed)} logged to SUBJECT"; "Lost" = chain>0 ? "chain of N → 0" : "nothing — no chain live"; "Forfeits today" = String(forfeit+1). Shows #notify-secondary ("Keep Going") with style.display='block'. Deliberately NO bell (warning, not reward). Hides btn-start/btn-pause/btn-quit, deactivates #break-actions, hides mini widget.
- notifyKeepGoing() (export, wired to secondary button): closes modal, hides secondary, clears pending action, unhides mini widget, resumeTimer().
- _finalizeQuit(countForfeit): clears interval; NightGuard.logSessionEnd(); saveAllAsync(); GalleryBreak.abort(); window.__cnsLoad.onPomodoroQuit(studySubject) (CNS Load integration, try/catch); deep-work OFF; widget unpaused; closes modal + secondary; if countForfeit && STUDY -> _ledgerForfeit(elapsed using pausedElapsed when paused); else if STOPWATCH -> the >=300 s complete-block rule above; timerEndTriggered=true (blocks later handleTimerEnd); status text "Session Forfeit." (countForfeit) or "Tracking Stopped."; hydrateFocusStats(); schedules resetPomoUI() after 1000 ms via _resetPomoTimer.

A.6 Session receipts + commitment projection
- _receiptRows(rows) -> rows.map(r => '<div class="receipt-row"><span>'+k+'</span><b>'+v+'</b></div>').join('').
- _blockCompletionReceipt(plannedSecs) rows: "Block" = formatTime(plannedSecs)+' · '+SUBJECT; "Chain" = "N deep block(s) without a forfeit"; "Deep today" = formatStudyDuration(deep); plus "Earned this block" = signed delta of AppState.elo[subject] vs snapshot (snapshot taken at transitionToStudy via _eloSnapshot() reading window.AppState.elo.global/[subj], null-safe; subject from snapshot so dynamic-mode switches attribute correctly). Shown inside the end-of-study modal via showTimerNotification(..., receiptHTML); #notify-receipt unhidden, else hidden.
- updateProjection() (#pomo-projection): bound to 'input' on #pomo-study/#pomo-break/#pomo-sessions. Empty when study<=0 or rounds<=0. totalMin = study*rounds + chill*max(0,rounds-1); label "Hh Mm" or "Mm". Exact text: `${study}m × ${rounds} round${rounds>1?'s':''} ≈ ${label} committed · every solve banks ×1.5 ELO while you're locked in`.

A.7 Bell / chime audio synthesis
- Persistent AudioContext bellAudioCtx created lazily by initAudioContext() (window.AudioContext||webkitAudioContext); resumes when 'suspended'. Called from startTimer() inside the user gesture.
- playBell(): gated by window.FX.wantSound(); ducks the soundscape via window.FocusSound.duck(); volume read from window.FX.prefs.volume (number) else default 0.7; bails if vol<=0 (exponential ramps reject 0 targets). Singing-bowl pair of sine oscillators straight to destination: A3 220 Hz gain 0.18 decay 2.20 s; E4 329.63 Hz gain 0.10 decay 2.45 s. Gain envelope: setValueAtTime(0.0001) -> exponentialRampToValueAtTime(g*vol, now+0.06) soft attack -> exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, now+dur); osc.stop(now+dur+0.05). Replaced an older sharp 880 Hz blip.
- playStartChime(): same gating/volume (no duck). Two staggered sines: C4 261.63 Hz at +0 ms gain 0.10 dur 1.10 s; G4 392.00 Hz at +180 ms gain 0.10 dur 1.30 s; attack 50 ms, exp decay to 0.0001.

A.8 Notifications / end-modal
- showTimerNotification(title, icon, message, nextAction, receiptHTML): stores module _pomoPendingAction (replaces old window._pomoPendingAction); sets #notify-title/#notify-icon/#notify-message textContent; receipt innerHTML + hidden=false/true; hides #notify-secondary; playBell(); adds class 'active' to #timer-notify-modal; hides #btn-start/#btn-pause/#btn-quit, removes 'active' from #break-actions, adds 'hidden' to #pomo-mini-widget.
- confirmTimerNotification() (export): removes 'active', hides secondary, runs pending action or falls back to resetPomoUI().

A.9 Visualizer
- visualMode starts 'bar'. toggleVisualizer() flips bar<->beaker; #vis-bar display block/none and #vis-beaker inverse. Bar: #pomo-progress style.width = percent%. Beaker: #pomo-beaker-fill style.height = percent%. percent = elapsed/total*100; stopwatch mode pins #pomo-progress width 100% with background var(--gradient-glow). toggleMiniWidget() toggles class 'collapsed' on #pomo-mini-widget.

A.10 Ambient Sprint Widget (always-visible pill)
- Container #pomo-mini-widget. Attribute API: data-state = 'idle' | 'study' | 'break' (syncWidget); data-paused attribute present when paused; data-pop attribute while the config popover is open; classes 'collapsed' and 'hidden'.
- Progress ring: #mini-ring-arc strokeDashoffset = MINI_RING_CIRC*(1-p) where MINI_RING_CIRC = 2π×15.5 ≈ 97.389 (r=15.5 matches index.html); p clamped 0..1; idle 0, stopwatch pinned 1.
- Fields: #mini-time (countdown formatTime; idle preview formatTime(popMinutes*60)); #mini-status ('SPRINT' idle / 'STOPWATCH' / 'STUDY n/N' / 'BREAK'; class 'mini-status study' or 'mini-status break'); #mini-subject (SUBJECT uppercase); #mini-end ("ends HH:MM" wall clock = timerStartTime + timerTotalSeconds*1000; blank when paused/IDLE/STOPWATCH/no anchor); #mini-tally ("N done today" when focusLedger.done>0 else ''); #mini-reason ('tap ▶ to lock in' when done==0 else ''); #mini-pause (widgetPauseToggle(): true toggle — resume shows '▮▮', pause shows '▶'; no-op when IDLE).
- Config popover: openPomoPop() — when a session is running the pill body instead calls window.switchTab('pomodoro', null); otherwise opens #pomo-pop (.open) + data-pop. closePomoPop() reverses. Document-level click closes when clicking outside the widget (module-level listener, stub-guarded).
- Popover internals: #pop-minutes and #pop-rounds numeric readouts; #pop-reason suggestion label — >=45 min 'deep zone', >=25 'classic sprint', else 'quick strike'; #pop-subjects containing buttons .pop-sub[data-subject] (active class mirrors studySubject); #pop-dynamic chip with 'on' class mirroring dynamicSubject.
- Seeding (_seedPopConfig, lazy once): popMinutes = live #pomo-study value OR persisted cfg.study OR 25; popRounds = live #pomo-sessions value OR cfg.sessions OR 1; then clamps minutes to [5,120], rounds to [1,8]. (Live form wins so ▶ never runs a stale length.)
- popSetSubject(sub): validates, drives #pomo-subject via changeStudySubject() (or bare assignment under stubs), resyncs. popAdjustMinutes(delta)/popAdjustRounds(delta): clamp-adjust then WRITE THROUGH into #pomo-study / #pomo-sessions (form stays source of truth), updateProjection(), resync. startSprintFromWidget() (shared launch for pill ▶ and popover Start): seeds, copies popMinutes/popRounds/subject into the form inputs, closePomoPop(), startTimer(). popStart() = closePomoPop() + startSprintFromWidget().

A.11 Dynamic subject mode
- toggleDynamicSubject(btn) flips dynamicSubject. _syncDynamicSubjectUI(): toggles 'on' class + title on #dynamic-subject-btn; toggles body class 'pomo-dynamic' (CSS keeps the setup strip editable during a run); while running sets #pomo-subject.disabled = !dynamicSubject.
- Behavior: each tick's credited second goes to whichever subject is active at that moment (per-second attribution); changeStudySubject() updates #mini-subject and live status line ("Stopwatch: X" / "Studying X (n/N)").

A.12 Body classes, ×1.5 window, integrations
- _setDeepWorkActive(on): toggles body class 'pomo-active' AND window._pomoRunning boolean. Contract: app.js's _getDeepWorkBlockMultiplier() grants ×1.5 ELO on solves while either flag is set. Set ON only in transitionToStudy/transitionToStopwatch/resume (STUDY+STOPWATCH); OFF on pause, break, quit, reset, timer end — "the badge never lies".
- body.pomo-dynamic: set whenever dynamicSubject is on (independent of running state).
- Consumes: NightGuard.logSessionStart()/logSessionEnd(); GalleryBreak.begin/setProgress/finish/abort/isActive; window.FocusSound.autoStart() (study + stopwatch starts) and .duck() (bell); window.FX.wantSound() and FX.prefs.volume (bell/chime); window.AppState.elo (receipt snapshot); window.__cnsLoad.onPomodoroQuit(subject); window.switchTab('pomodoro', null).
- Locks #question-filter (disabled unless IDLE) via syncFilterLock() on start/reset.

Plus window.__pomodoro (see A.0) and window._pomoRunning.

===============================================================
B. focus-sound.js (796 lines) — Focus Soundscape engine (v5.2)
===============================================================

B.1 Purpose + architecture
- Plays pre-rendered audio only: seamless WAV loops generated offline (scripts/gen-ambient-sounds.mjs v3: equal-power seams, AGC, matched loudness) plus CC0 recordings (rain, café) fetched from assets/sounds/. Nothing synthesized at runtime.
- Guard: IIFE, window.__focusSoundInit latch prevents double init.
- Master WebAudio graph (created once in ctx()): masterIn(GainNode) -> depthFilter (lowshelf @110 Hz) -> brightFilter (highshelf @3500 Hz) -> masterGain -> DynamicsCompressor -> destination. Compressor is safety-only: threshold −10 dB, knee 10, ratio 3, attack 0.004 s, release 0.2 s (sits ABOVE program level so ambience never pumps).
- Per-preset graph: layers -> tone filter (lowpass, Q 0.5, frequency 19000 = transparent until Tone knob moves it) -> buildOut gain (preset trim) -> masterIn. Mono sources widened by a subtle Haas widener widenTo(): ChannelMerger(2) with right ear delayed 16 ms at wet gain 0.85; stereo passes untouched.

B.2 Presets (SOUNDS map; assets under assets/sounds/)
- rain — label 'Rain', icon 🌧️, files ['rain.mp3','rain-roof.mp3'], real:true, trim 0.82, hint 'Real · heavy downpour ⇢ calm roof rain'.
- ocean — 🌊 ocean.wav, trim 1.0, 'Slow swells + distant breakers'.
- stream — 🌲 stream.wav, trim 0.95, 'Gurgling brook + soft plops'.
- fire — 🔥 fire.wav, trim 1.05, 'Warm ember bed + gentle crackle'.
- cafe — ☕ cafe.mp3, real:true, trim 1.0, 'Real · café murmur'.
- wind — 🍃 wind.wav, trim 0.9, 'Soft gusts + leaf rustle'.
- drone — 🧘 drone.wav, trim 1.15, 'Detuned hum, slow breathing'.
- brown — 🟤 brown.wav, trim 1.0, 'Deep rumble'.
- pink — 🌸 pink.wav, trim 0.95, 'Balanced, soft'.
- white — ⬜ white.wav, trim 0.85, 'Bright, even hiss'.
- `real:true` presets go through the crossfade-loop/grain pipeline; WAV loops use plain src.loop=true (seam baked into the file). `trim` is applied where the graph meets the master bus (headroom).

B.3 Volume model + knobs
- prefs (localStorage 'jeemax_focus_sound_prefs'): DEFAULTS { v:2, sound:'rain', volume:60, depth:50, brightness:50, density:100, playing:false, autoSession:true }. KNOB_DEFAULTS volume 60, depth 50, brightness 50, density 100; KNOB_LABELS Volume/Depth/Brightness/Tone.
- One-time migration: pre-v2 payload without `v` and density===50 (old linear Tone default) snaps density to 100; deliberately-set values survive.
- Master gain curve: targetGain() = max(0.0001, (volume/100)^1.6).
- Shelf shaping: shelfDb(v100,maxDb) = sign(x)*|x|^1.4 * maxDb with x=(v/100−0.5)*2 ∈ [−1,1] — soft power taper (old ±15 dB linear shelves caused boom/clipping; now Depth ±8 dB, Brightness ±7 dB). applyMix() pushes both shelves and master via setTargetAtTime τ=0.06.
- Tone knob (density): density01() = clamp(density/100,0,1). Non-rain presets: lowpass cutoff f = 1500 * (19/1.5)^min(1,d/0.85), capped 19000, τ 0.1 — dark⇢airy, FULLY OPEN by 85% travel. Rain: equal-power crossfade between layers — gHeavy.gain=sin(d·π/2), gCalm.gain=cos(d·π/2), τ 0.1 (calm roof rain ⇢ heavy downpour).
- Slider drags debounced to localStorage via saveSoon() (120 ms); double-click snaps knob to KNOB_DEFAULTS and statuses "<Label> reset to default".

B.4 Fades / crossfades / duck timings
- FADE_IN_SECS 1.4 (linear master attack on cold start: master 0.0001 -> target over 1.4 s).
- FADE_OUT_SECS 0.5 (pause/release; hard shutdownAll after 0.5 s + 80 ms).
- XFADE_UP_SECS 0.9 (new bed rise on preset switch: out.gain 0.0001 -> trim via setTargetAtTime τ=XFADE_UP/3).
- XFADE_DOWN_SECS 0.55 (old bed exponential ramp to 0.0001, stopped after 0.55 s + 120 ms; retireGraph guards g.dying).
- DUCK_HOLD_MS 1500 (duck(): master dips to targetGain()*0.25 in ~0.09 s, holds 1500 ms, swells back via setTargetAtTime(targetGain(), τ 0.4); token-guarded against superseded/paused ducks). Called by pomodoro playBell().

B.5 Real-recording playback (loopers)
- Buffers lazy-loaded via fetch('assets/sounds/'+src) -> decodeAudioData; memoized in bufferCache{} (HTTP cached by service worker).
- MIN_REAL_SEC = 45. Recordings shorter than 45 s are expanded by makeGrainLoop(buffer,45): overlap-add grain canvas — grain length = clamp(duration*0.18, 0.65, 1.5) s; hop = 62% of grain; canvas rounded UP to whole hops (wrap seam window-exact); equal-power rise/fall windows (N=128 sin/cos); circular placement. v5.2 calm-grains: global loudness envelope baked in first (frame RMS over 0.5 s frames, inverse clamped 0.55–1.8 ≈ ±5 dB, box-blur radius 5 frames ≈ ±2.5 s smoothing), then grain positions DRIFT along the take via reflected random walk with steps 0.35–0.9 s. Composite cached per source buffer (__grainKey) in compositeCache Map; OOM falls back to the original short buffer.
- startRealLoop(buffer,out,stopFns): successive full-buffer playthroughs whose tails crossfade into heads. fade = clamp(dur*0.12, 0.8, 3.0) s (dur/4 if buffer too short); 96-point equal-power EQ_IN/EQ_OUT curves via setValueCurveAtTime; scheduler setInterval 500 ms, horizon now+2.5 s, step dur−fade; STARTS CLAMPED TO THE FUTURE (>= now+0.06 s) so post-iOS-interruption catch-up can't stack in-phase blasts; per-source stop at start+dur+0.03; diag counter diagCycles++.
- Rain builds TWO stacked real loops (heavy + calm) into a stable busOut blend node.

B.6 Lifecycle + session bridge
- start(): unsupported -> status '⚠️ WebAudio unsupported in this browser.'; engine fail -> '⚠️ Audio engine refused to start — reload and try again.'; sets prefs.playing=true, saves, loadAndPlay('start').
- pause(): master fades to 0.0001 over 0.5 s then shutdownAll(); prefs.playing=false; saves.
- toggle(): playing ? pause() : start().
- loadAndPlay(mode): token guard loadToken beats rapid preset clicks; status 'Loading X…' / 'Switching to X…'. 'switch': new graph rises while retireGraph(old) fades every other graph; success status '▶ Label · hint'. Failure paths keep whatever plays alive: switch-fail status `⚠️ Couldn't load LABEL (msg) — kept current sound.`; cold-start failure sets prefs.playing=false and statuses `⚠️ Couldn't load audio (msg) — try again online once.`
- autoStart(): called by pomodoro transitions (STUDY and STOPWATCH). No-op unless prefs.autoSession && !prefs.playing.
- setAuto(v): flips+saves autoSession (wired to #sc-auto checkbox).
- Auto-resume: listeners on document visibilitychange, window pageshow/focus/pointerdown and AudioContext statechange resume a suspended ctx whenever prefs.playing (covers sleep + iOS 'interrupted').

B.7 Status/error surfacing
- setStatus writes textContent of #sc-status. Idle line: 'Idle — pick a bed and hit play.' Playing line: '▶ Label · hint'. Knob reset line: '<Label> reset to default'.

B.8 UI wiring (element ids)
- #sc-presets — chip buttons rendered by renderPresets(): button.sc-preset[data-sound] with .sc-preset-icon, .sc-preset-label, and a .sc-preset-real 'REAL' badge for recorded beds; title=hint; click -> selectSound(id, autoplay=true).
- #sc-power — power button (wired inline via onclick="FocusSound.toggle()"): class 'on' when playing, glyph '❚❚'/▶, aria-label 'Stop soundscape'/'Play soundscape'.
- Sliders #sc-volume, #sc-depth, #sc-brightness, #sc-density with paired value spans #sc-volume-val etc.; each paints --sc-fill percentage + aria-valuetext; titles explain the knob (density: 'Tone — dark & muffled ⇢ fully open by 85%. On Rain: calm roof rain ⇢ heavy rain (double-click to reset)'). Density slider live-drives live.density on the newest graph.
- #sc-auto — checkbox reflecting prefs.autoSession.
- Boot: waits for document.body; FORCES prefs.playing=false at boot (autoplay policy honesty), renderPresets, wireSliders, sc-auto checked, reflect.

B.9 Storage keys
- 'jeemax_focus_sound_prefs' only (shape in B.3). Audio HTTP caching is service-worker territory; decoded buffers memoized in-memory only.

B.10 Public API — window.FocusSound
{ prefs (live object), toggle, start, pause, select: selectSound(id, autoplay?), supported(), autoStart(), setAuto(v), duck(), _diag() -> { cycles, composites }, _state() -> { graphs, dying, alive, sound }, _buildTestLoop(src) -> Promise<AudioBuffer> running the exact expansion path }. Consumers: pomodoro.js (autoStart, duck); QA scripts (_diag/_state/_buildTestLoop).

===============================================================
C. metronome.js (553 lines) — floating practice metronome (v2)
===============================================================

C.1 Feature overview
- Self-installing floating FAB (bottom-right; comment notes cp-hub owns bottom-left) opening a compact popover: BPM readout, range slider, hold-to-repeat ± steppers, TAP tempo, beat-count stepper, three voice chips, volume slider, beat dots.
- Sample-accurate "Tale of Two Clocks" lookahead scheduler: setInterval only queues beats ahead on the AudioContext clock; schedule-ahead window widens when document.hidden so 1 Hz throttling can't drift tempo.
- v2 "study-soft" sound design: every hit fades in over 2–10 ms (no hard onsets), energy kept ≤ ~800 Hz–mid, peaks whisper-trimmed (~¼ of v1); accents lead by timbre/pitch, not volume.

C.2 Prefs
- localStorage 'jeemax_metronome_prefs'. DEFAULTS { v:2, bpm:100, beats:4, volume:55, voice:'soft' }. Clamps: bpm 30–250 (BPM_MIN/BPM_MAX), beats 1–7, volume 0–100, voice validated against VOICES.
- One-time v1→v2 migration (fires when stored object lacks `v`): untouched default volume 70 snapped to 55; VOICE_MIGRATE maps click->soft, beep->chime; marks v:2. Migration gate reads the STORED object (not merged defaults).

C.3 Voices (exact synthesis parameters)
Shared primitives: blip (osc + linear attack + exp decay to 0.0008), tone (same, softer edge), knock (white-noise burst through bandpass, Q parameterized), thock (highpassed noise transient); shared noise buffer = 0.2 s white noise.
- soft 🌙 'Felt-piano tick — default study voice': blip sine accent?784 Hz:523 Hz peak accent?0.20:0.13 decay 0.075 s attack 0.005; thock highpass accent?1700:1350 Hz peak accent?0.050:0.032 decay 0.016 attack 0.002.
- wood 🪵 'Mellow woodblock — rounder and quieter': knock bandpass accent?1450:1050 Hz Q accent?5.5:4.5 peak accent?0.32:0.22 decay 0.055 attack 0.002; blip triangle accent?494:392 Hz peak accent?0.085:0.058 decay 0.030 attack 0.003.
- chime 🎐 'Warm two-sine hum — softest, bell-ish': two sines f = accent?784:659 Hz and f*2.005 (slightly sharp octave partial), peaks accent?0.16:0.11 and accent?0.045:0.030, decays 0.17 s / 0.10 s, attack 0.010. Accent = chord change, not a volume jump.
- Voice switching applies from the NEXT scheduled beat (never pulls nextBeatTime backwards).

C.4 Audio scheduling constants + transport
- Graph: single AudioContext + master GainNode = volCurve(volume) = (v/100)^1.6 -> destination. Each scheduled beat gets its own per-beat bus gain (clean mixing).
- TICK_MS 25 (scheduler cadence visible), AHEAD_VISIBLE 0.12 s queued while visible, AHEAD_HIDDEN 3.2 s while tab hidden, TICK_HIDDEN_MS 400 (interval stretched when hidden). Per-tick scheduling guard 64 beats. Accent when beatIndex % beats === 0. First beat at currentTime+0.08 s ("tiny breath").
- Visual sync: drawQueue of {time,accent}; rAF drawLoop flashes dot when event time <= currentTime+0.005; dot lit 110 ms (classes 'on' / 'on-accent'); index math (beatIndex − drawQueue.length − 1 + beats*4096) % beats.
- visibilitychange: rebuilds interval at correct cadence; pre-queues through the throttle wall before hiding; resumes suspended ctx (iOS/Safari).
- start/stop/toggle/isRunning; stop cancels interval+rAF, clears queue, repaints.

C.5 UI (all elements built programmatically, appended to body)
- #metro-fab (button.metro-fab, aria-label 'Open metronome', title 'Metronome (M)'): inline SVG metronome (paths .metro-body and .metro-arm with pendulum circle) + #metro-fab-badge showing current BPM. While playing gets class 'playing'; CSS var --metro-swing set to (60/bpm)s for arm animation.
- #metro-pop (div.metro-pop, role dialog, aria-label 'Metronome'), class 'open' when shown: .metro-tag 'Metronome'; #metro-dots (i.metro-dot per beat, first has .accent); hero #metro-dec '−' / #metro-bpm-num / 'BPM' / #metro-inc '+'; #metro-range (range 30–250 step 1); transport #metro-play '▶'/'▮▮' (aria Start/Stop metronome, class stop-face when running), #metro-tap 'TAP', #metro-voices chip group (button.metro-chip[data-voice] icon-only, aria-pressed true/false, title = hint); footer Beats mini-stepper #metro-beat-dec / #metro-beat-num / #metro-beat-inc and Vol slider #metro-vol (0–100).
- Hold-to-repeat steppers (attachHold): immediate fire, 380 ms dwell, then repeats every 80 ms; cleared on pointerup/pointercancel/pointerleave/window blur.
- Tap tempo: taps[] capped at 7 entries; gap > 2000 ms resets; needs >= 3 taps; bpm = round(60000 / mean interval); TAP button flashes (.flash class) on success.
- Double-click resets: #metro-range -> DEFAULTS.bpm (100); #metro-vol -> DEFAULTS.volume (55).
- Slider fill painted inline: linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(255,178,36,.9) pct%, rgba(255,255,255,.14) pct%).
- Panel management: openPanel/closePanel/togglePanel; document pointerdown outsideClose; fab/pop get/remove .open.
- Keyboard (document keydown; ignored with meta/ctrl/alt or typing in input/textarea/select): 'M' toggle panel; ' ' (Space) start/stop while panel open; 'Escape' close panel. No visible close chrome by design.

C.6 Integration points
- Deliberately standalone: no storage.js/cloud coupling ("Cloud / storage.js intentionally untouched"), no pomodoro/FocusSound hooks. Lives alongside everything as a global utility; prefs device-local.
- window.Metro API: { togglePanel, open, close, start, stop, toggle, setBpm(n)->clamped number, setBeats(n), setVoice(k) (builds UI first), isRunning }. Guard latch window.__metroInit.

===============================================================
D. fx.js (303 lines) — centralized sound/visual/haptic FX
===============================================================

D.1 Design contract
- Pure additive overlay: defines window.FX + capture-phase event delegation + Settings wiring; NEVER overrides existing app functions. If the file fails to load, every gate elsewhere evaluates window.FX === undefined and original behavior persists. Guard latch window.__fxInit.
- Prefs: localStorage 'jeemax_fx_prefs'. Shape { sound:true, hover:false, effects:true, haptics:true, volume:0.7 }. vol() validates 0..1 else 0.7.
- Reduced motion: on FIRST RUN ONLY (no stored prefs yet), matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)') matching sets effects=false. Stored preference afterwards wins.
- applyBody(): toggles class 'fx-effects-off' on document.documentElement (html) when effects are off — CSS and other modules (gallery-break visuals) key off this.

D.2 Synth (zero assets)
- Lazy AudioContext; unlocked/resumed on first pointerdown/keydown (autoplay policy).
- tone({type,f,f2,dur,g,at}): oscillator, optional exponential frequency ramp f->f2, gain envelope setValueAtTime(0.0001) -> exponentialRamp(peak=g*vol(), now+0.008) -> exponentialRamp(0.0001, now+dur); osc.stop(now+dur+0.03).
- noise(dur,g): generated white-noise buffer -> bandpass 1400 Hz Q 0.7 -> same envelope shape (attack 10 ms).

D.3 Complete SFX catalog (name: recipe)
- click: square 200->120 Hz, 0.05 s, g 0.10.
- soft: sine 330 Hz, 0.06 s, g 0.07.
- confirm: sine 660->990 Hz, 0.12 s, g 0.14.
- delete: sawtooth 240->90 Hz, 0.14 s, g 0.11.
- select: triangle 520 Hz, 0.05 s, g 0.09.
- tab: triangle 440->560 Hz, 0.09 s, g 0.10.
- modalClose: sine 520->260 Hz, 0.14 s, g 0.08 + noise(0.10, 0.03).
- toggle: square 760->980 Hz, 0.045 s, g 0.07.
- blip: sine 1200 Hz, 0.03 s, g 0.05.
- tick: triangle 900 Hz, 0.03 s, g 0.07.
- hover: sine 1500 Hz, 0.02 s, g 0.025.
- success: sine arpeggio [523, 659, 783] Hz, each 0.12 s g 0.12, staggered i*0.07 s.

D.4 Triggers (event delegation, capture phase)
- Click delegation on document (capture): skips targets inside #fx-test-sound / #fx-test-fx (own listeners), skips .fx-switch/.cpcc-switch (change listener handles them), skips raw INPUT/SELECT/TEXTAREA. Resolves host via closest(COMBINED). If nothing matched and target IS .modal-overlay (backdrop click closing a modal) -> sound('modalClose') + haptic([6]).
- RIPPLE_HOSTS selector (ripple allowed; elements safe for position:relative + overflow:hidden): .btn, .matrix-pill, .subject-folder, .subject-card, .cal-day, .daily-queue-master-btn, .counter-btn, .sr-mcq-option, .mcq-option, .cp-option, .sr-toggle-btn, .sr-friction-pill, .ingestion-toggle-btn, .sr-self-btn, .cp-selfreport-correct, .cp-selfreport-wrong, .chapter-item, .question-card, .file-upload-label, .file-upload-btn, .btn-break-opt, .sr-practice-btn, .sr-submit-btn, .sr-confirm-btn, .btn-start, .pomo-settings-btn, .mini-collapse-btn, .collapse-btn, .cal-nav-btn, .sr-history-toggle, .cpcc-add-btn, .cpcc-test-btn, .cpcc-save-btn, .cpcc-time-x, .show-solution-btn, .hide-toggle, .cp-abandon, .cp-grace-ignite, .cpcc-ignite-btn, .manual-sync-btn.
- SOUND_ONLY (sound + haptic, no ripple — absolute buttons/tooltip hosts/emoji/nav/badges): .nav-item, .badge, .mood-emoji, .close-btn, .sr-drawer-close, .cpcc-close, .delete-btn, .card-close-btn, .delete-chapter, .delete-segment-btn. COMBINED = union for host resolution.
- classify(host) mapping (sound name, vibration pattern, extras):
  - .counter-btn -> tick, [3], plus bump animation: nearest .tp-row (or parent) .counter element re-triggered with class fx-bump.
  - .btn-danger or .cpcc-time-x -> delete, [20,40,20].
  - close/delete family (.close-btn, .sr-drawer-close, .cpcc-close, .delete-btn, .card-close-btn, .delete-chapter, .delete-segment-btn) -> delete, [12,30,12].
  - success family (.btn-success, .cp-grace-ignite, .cpcc-ignite-btn, .cpcc-save-btn, .sr-submit-btn, .sr-practice-btn, .sr-confirm-btn, .btn-start, .btn-primary) -> confirm, [8,30,8].
  - soft family (.btn-secondary, .btn-break-opt, .cal-nav-btn, .sr-history-toggle, .cp-abandon, .cpcc-add-btn, .cpcc-test-btn, .file-upload-btn, .file-upload-label, .show-solution-btn, .hide-toggle, .manual-sync-btn, .pomo-settings-btn, .mini-collapse-btn, .collapse-btn) -> soft, [6].
  - .nav-item -> tab, [6]. Everything else matched -> select, [5].
- Ripple: span.fx-ripple sized max(w,h)*1.15 px, centered on click coordinates when given else element center; appended to host; removed on animationend or 700 ms timeout; skipped entirely when effects off.
- Hover sounds (off by default; requires prefs.hover && prefs.sound): mouseover on .nav-item, .subject-card, .subject-folder, .compact-subject-card, .badge, .matrix-pill; 55 ms throttle + same-element dedupe; mouseout clears memory.
- Keyboard focus blip: focusin on .pomo-input, .pomo-select, .matrix-search, .cp-integer-input only when :focus-visible (mouse focus silent).
- Change cues: select or checkbox change (excluding ids starting 'fx-' so toggles don't double-fire) -> select + haptic [5].

D.5 Settings wiring (element ids)
- Checkboxes: #fx-sound, #fx-effects, #fx-haptics, #fx-hover (wireBool: reflect checked, change -> save + applyBody + haptic[6] + sound('confirm') for the sound key else sound('toggle')).
- Range: #fx-volume (wireRange: value = round(vol()*100); input throttled 90 ms -> prefs.volume = clamped int/100; blip feedback).
- Test buttons: #fx-test-sound -> sequence select, confirm, success staggered 120 ms + haptic [6,40,6,40,12]. #fx-test-fx -> centered ripple + appends div.green-glow-overlay (removed on animationend) + window.burstEmojis(cx, cy, 22, ['✨','🎉','', '💯','🌟'], 1.2) when effects on and burstEmojis exists (note: the array literally contains one empty-string entry in source), + sound('success') + haptic [10,30,10].

D.6 window.FX public API
{ prefs (live object), sound(name), haptic(pattern), ripple(el,cx,cy), wantSound(), wantHoverSound(), wantEffects(), wantHaptic(), setPref(k,v) (save + applyBody) }.
Consumers named in source: gates in app.js, pomodoro.js (playBell/playStartChime use wantSound + prefs.volume), candlestick chart; gallery-break.js consumes FX.wantSound and the html.fx-effects-off class.

===============================================================
E. gallery-break.js (1057 lines) — "The Burn Reveal" break ritual
===============================================================

E.1 Concept + ownership
- When a pomodoro break starts, the app dissolves from screen center, slowly developing a public-domain painting underneath "like a print coming up in a developer bath". Reveal radius tracks break progress 1:1 (pausing freezes the bloom mid-spread). At full reveal a quote fades in and a Continue button blooms back into the next session.
- Owned/driven entirely by pomodoro.js: begin() at transitionToBreak(); setProgress(0..1) every countdown tick while pomoState==='BREAK' (also from the visibility-change catch-up); finish(onContinue) at handleTimerEnd BREAK branch (only when isActive()); abort() on skipBreak, _finalizeQuit, resetPomoUI (safe no-op when idle).
- Exports: ES export const GalleryBreak = { begin, setProgress, finish, abort, isActive }; ALSO mirrored to window.GalleryBreak. Imports idbGet/idbSet from ./storage.js.

E.2 Trigger conditions recap
- begin(): hard-resets any stale overlay (abort(true)), increments session generation token _gen, resets _target/_display to 0, samples prefers-reduced-motion, reseeds organic texture, builds DOM, picks painting, kicks async art load (stale loads dropped via generation check), starts paper-crackle audio, starts rAF loop.
- finish(onContinue): if inactive, immediately invokes callback; else sets _finishing=true, _target=1, stores callback.
- abort(immediate=false): increments _gen (invalidates in-flight art/quote timers), stops crackle, cancels rAF, removes window resize listener, clears refs; immediate=true -> el.remove(); else opacity transition fade of abortFadeMs 350 ms with delayed removal (+60 ms) guarded so a newer overlay is never removed.

E.3 Painting pool (Wikimedia Commons; PAINTINGS array, all public domain)
Each entry: id, title, artist, year, file (Commons filename), bundled quote, optional quote attribution:
1. starry-night — The Starry Night, Vincent van Gogh, 1889, 'Van Gogh - Starry Night - Google Art Project.jpg' — Van Gogh stars/dream quote, by van Gogh.
2. great-wave — The Great Wave off Kanagawa, Hokusai, 1831, 'Tsunami by hokusai 19th century.jpg' — Hokusai "at seventy-three…" + "Keep going.", by Hokusai.
3. wanderer-fog — Wanderer above the Sea of Fog, Caspar David Friedrich, 1818, 'Caspar David Friedrich - Wanderer above the sea of fog.jpg' — "The climb is lonely. The view is not." (no attribution).
4. impression-sunrise — Impression, Sunrise, Claude Monet, 1872, 'Monet - Impression, Sunrise.jpg' — bird-sings quote + tag, by Monet.
5. fighting-temeraire — The Fighting Temeraire, J.M.W. Turner, 1839, 'Turner, J. M. W. - The Fighting Téméraire tugged to her last Berth to be broken.jpg' — paint-what-I-see quote + tag, by Turner.
6. wheatfield-cypresses — A Wheatfield, with Cypresses, Vincent van Gogh, 1889, 'Vincent van Gogh - Wheat Field with Cypresses - Google Art Project.jpg' — small-things quote, by van Gogh.
7. pearl-earring — Girl with a Pearl Earring, Johannes Vermeer, 1665, '1665 Girl with a Pearl Earring.jpg' — "Mastery is quiet…" (no attribution).
8. ninth-wave — The Ninth Wave, Ivan Aivazovsky, 1850, 'Aivazovsky, Ivan - The Ninth Wave.jpg' — wave/shore line (no attribution).
9. sierra-nevada — Among the Sierra Nevada, Albert Bierstadt, 1868, 'Albert Bierstadt - Among the Sierra Nevada, California - Google Art Project.jpg' — rest/progress line (no attribution).
10. shin-ohashi — Sudden Shower over Shin-Ōhashi, Hiroshige, 1857, 'Hiroshige Atake sous une averse soudaine.jpg' — storms-pass line (no attribution).
Quotes are BUNDLED in code (no network needed for quotes).

E.4 Rotation + caching (storage keys)
- localStorage 'jeemax_gallery_rotation_v1': JSON { order:[permutation of 0..9], next:index }. _pickPainting() validates (order array of exact PAINTINGS.length, numeric next < length); reshuffles Fisher-Yates when invalid; picks order[next], then increments next and persists. Guarantees each of the 10 paintings once per cycle.
- IndexedDB art cache via storage.js idbSet/idbGet: keys 'galleryArt:' + painting.id (ART_CACHE_PREFIX 'galleryArt:'), value = image Blob. Later breaks therefore work fully offline.

E.5 Art pipeline tiers
1) IDB blob -> createImageBitmap(blob) when available, else <img> via object URL (revoked on load/error).
2) Miss/expired -> fetch Commons API: https://commons.wikimedia.org/w/api.php?action=query&format=json&origin=*&prop=imageinfo&iiprop=url&iiurlwidth=1600&titles=File:<encoded file>; takes imageinfo[0].thumburl || url; fetches it; stores Blob in IDB best-effort.
3) API/fetch failure -> display-only direct load of https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:FilePath/<file>?width=1600 with referrerPolicy='no-referrer' (not cached).
4) Total failure (offline etc.) -> _proceduralArt(): 1600×1000 canvas, vertical gradient sky #0b1030 -> #1b2a5e -> #3b2a56, 4 aurora bands (lineWidth 60−b*10; colors rgba(80,255,190,.20)/rgba(120,180,255,.16)/rgba(255,170,220,.12)/rgba(140,255,140,.10); sinusoidal y with 240 px and 90 px periods), 180 random stars. "The ritual never breaks."
- _computeArtTint(): downsamples art to 12×12, averages RGB, lifts toward warm white (lift weights r .45 / g .50 / b .42) -> _artTint drives aura/motes/rays/sheen palette; tainted-canvas/missing-art fallback warm ivory rgb(255,231,201).

E.6 Reveal-radius mechanics (tied to break progress)
- _target set by setProgress(p) (clamped 0..1) = fraction of break elapsed. _display eases toward _target each rAF frame by factor TUNING.easePerFrame 0.045; snaps when |diff| < 0.0004. Pausing the timer stops setProgress calls -> bloom freezes (aura keeps breathing).
- Radius: baseR = easeInOutSine(_display) × maxR, maxR = hypot(w,h)/2 × 1.15 (overshoot clears corners at 100%). Painting drawn cover-fit and perfectly static (motion belongs to the edge).
- Soft-edge system (offscreen alpha mask stamped onto art with destination-in):
  - Organic boundary: pathSamples 96 points, Catmull-Rom smoothed closed Path2D; radial modulation = breatheAmp 0.004 slow breathing + 5 wobble octaves {lobes/amp/speed/phase}: {2, 0.030, 0.00021}, {3, 0.017, −0.00013}, {5, 0.008, 0.00034}, {9, 0.011, 0.00055} traveling ripple, {15, 0.006, −0.00080} fine shimmer.
  - Feather band: feather = max(featherMinPx 52, r × featherFrac 0.16); edgeR = r + feather×0.45; radial gradient alpha 1 -> 0 across the band.
  - Ghost halo: faint moisture mask ahead of the edge (ghostAlpha 0.07) at r + feather×1.4 with +6000 ms time offset for different undulation.
  - Bleed spots: bleedSpots 9 seep-through reveals ahead of the edge (size 10–36 px, alpha 0.18–0.40, pulse 0.7–1.0, slow angular drift), scale-clamped with radius (spotScale 0.6–1.6 of r/520).
  - Fibrous mottling: mottleCount 12 dots inside the fade band (size 14–44, alpha 0.10–0.24, distFrac 0.84–1.06).
- Pointer enforcement: overlay style.pointerEvents = 'auto' once _display > blockThreshold 0.30, else 'none' — the bloom itself blocks input to the app.
- Atmosphere layers (each gated on !_reduceMotion and !_wantEffects() where noted):
  - Print grain: alpha grainAlpha 0.055, 160 px noise tile (values 118–178), jittered a few px every ~180/230 ms (static when reduced motion); composited source-atop so it lands only on revealed pixels.
  - Wet sheen: sheenAlpha 0.10 radial bloom while |target−display| > 0.0015, decays ×sheenDecay 0.985/frame ("dries out").
  - God rays: raysCount 4 beams, raysAlpha 0.06, spin raysSpin 0.000025 rad/ms (random directions), tinted to art; baseR >= 40 required.
  - Museum vignette: vignetteMax 0.42 darkening toward screen edges (radial from 0.45×maxR to 1.02×maxR, color rgb(4,5,9)), scaled by _display and a 2600 ms breathe; runs in BOTH partial and fully-open states.
  - Aura: outer halo stroke lineWidth 24, shadowBlur 40 (22 reduced-motion), alpha auraOuterAlpha 0.10 × breathe (1400 ms sine; static 0.8 when reduced); up to glowBlobs 22 cached soft light sprites (96 px sprite, re-keyed per tint) riding the rim with a circling travel wave (glowBase 0.10 + glowTravel 0.22) and individual flicker, sprite size 56–100 px; thin inner shimmer lineWidth 2.5 alpha auraInnerAlpha 0.16.
  - Dust motes: moteCount 26 particles orbiting/bobbing inside the revealed light (bob 4–14 px, twinkle), art-tinted halo + warm-white core.
  - Bokeh bloom: ONE-SHOT at finish when _display >= 0.985 (bokehCount 16 particles; life 2600–5000 ms; drift/rise/twinkle), then they settle over the finished print; skipped for reduced motion/effects-off; fires once per break (_bloomFired).
- Canvas sizing: DPR capped at 2; separate identically-sized offscreen _maskCanvas.

E.7 Quote ritual + Continue button
- At finish with _display >= 0.995: arms generation token, setTimeout(_showQuote, quoteDelayMs 600), guard class gb-show on the box.
- _showQuote(): pointerEvents 'auto'; fills #gb-plaque with `${title} · ${artist}, ${year}`, #gb-quote with the painting's quote, #gb-quote-by with '— '+by or ''; adds gb-visible; plays _chime(); after continueDelayMs 2000 adds gb-ready (Continue appears).
- Chime: dedicated throwaway AudioContext; soft C-major arpeggio sines [523.25, 659.25, 783.99] Hz, staggered i*0.12 s, attack 30 ms to peak 0.06, decay 1.4 s, stop at +1.5 s; context closed after 2600 ms. Gated by _wantSound().
- Continue click (#gb-continue, _onContinueClick): ignored if !_active or already reversing; removes gb-visible/gb-ready; stops crackle; REVERSE BLOOM — own rAF loop eases _display from current value to 0 over reverseDurationMs 1800 ms with easeInOutSine (painting recedes to cover the app, symmetry); at k>=1 abort(true) then invokes stored callback = pomodoro's startStudyAfterBreakPopup (unhides the mini widget, transitionToStudy()). Cancelled if abort happened meanwhile.

E.8 Audio: paper-crackle bed
- Skipped entirely for reduced motion or when FX sound disabled (_wantSound(): window.FX.wantSound respected, defaults true when FX missing/throws).
- Own AudioContext + 2 s looping buffer: soft noise bed amplitude ±0.25 with sparse pops (probability 0.0015 of full-scale random samples) -> bandpass 900 Hz Q 0.8 -> gain starting 0. Intensity tracks spread rate: level = min(1, |target−display| × 30) × crackleGain 0.30 via setTargetAtTime τ 0.25 — louder while actively spreading, near-silent frozen/finished. Stop: fade τ 0.15 then src.stop()+ctx.close() after 600 ms.

E.9 Reduced-motion + FX gating summary
- begin() caches matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)'). Reduced motion zeroes boundary modulation (no wobble/breathe), disables sheen, rays, motes, vignette, bokeh, grain jitter, halves aura blur, and skips crackle entirely. Independent gate _wantEffects() checks absence of html.fx-effects-off (the class fx.js maintains), disabling fancy visual layers; sound layers additionally honor FX.wantSound.

E.10 Element ids / classes
- Overlay root: #gallery-break-overlay (any pre-existing duplicates force-removed on build).
- Children: #gb-canvas (main canvas), #gb-quote-box (div.gb-quote-box; classes gb-show = armed guard, gb-visible = shown, gb-ready = Continue available), #gb-plaque, #gb-quote, #gb-quote-by, #gb-continue (button.gb-continue).
- Internal (not in index.html): offscreen mask canvas, cached 96 px glow sprite, 160 px grain tile.

E.11 Edge cases handled
- Offline first break: Commons API + FilePath both fail -> procedural aurora painting; quotes always local; IDB cache makes subsequent breaks offline-proof.
- Rapid begin/abort cycles: _gen token drops stale art loads and stale quote timers; stale overlay nodes removed synchronously; faded-out node removal checks document.body.contains.
- Pause: reveal freezes mid-char (aura still breathes); Add-break-time re-covers (target eases downward too). Skip/quit: abort fade 350 ms. Resize: canvas + mask resized, listener removed on teardown.
- No two overlays can coexist; double-begin hard resets. Canvas ops guarded (tainted canvas -> ivory tint; createBuffer failures fall back to source buffer).

E.12 Tuning constants quick reference (TUNING object)
easePerFrame 0.045 · blockThreshold 0.30 · reverseDurationMs 1800 · abortFadeMs 350 · quoteDelayMs 600 · continueDelayMs 2000 · pathSamples 96 · featherFrac 0.16 · featherMinPx 52 · breatheAmp 0.004 · glowBlobs 22 · glowBase 0.10 · glowTravel 0.22 · bleedSpots 9 · mottleCount 12 · ghostAlpha 0.07 · grainAlpha 0.055 · grainSize 160 · moteCount 26 · auraOuterAlpha 0.10 · auraInnerAlpha 0.16 · vignetteMax 0.42 · sheenAlpha 0.10 · sheenDecay 0.985 · raysCount 4 · raysAlpha 0.06 · raysSpin 0.000025 · bokehCount 16 · crackleGain 0.30.
