------------------------------------------------------------------------------
A. checkpoint.js — 4-PILLAR ACCOUNTABILITY CHECKPOINT SYSTEM (HARDENED)
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

A.1 Overview and pillar framing
- Header comment brands it "4-PILLAR ACCOUNTABILITY CHECKPOINT SYSTEM — HARDENED (vanilla JS port)", mirroring src/lib/checkpoint.ts, with "All 8 exploits patched". The file never enumerates pillars 1-4 by name; the only in-code pillar reference is a section comment "Lockdown overlay (Pillar 2 + 3 + Fix 7: all question types)". Operationally the four legs are: scheduled checkpoints (time-based), idle detection, Proof-of-Work lockdown practice, and Protocol Zero penalties.
- ES module loaded as type="module" from index.html.
- Imports from './storage.js': AppState, idbGet, idbSet, saveAllAsync, fetchMediaFromDrive (idbGet and saveAllAsync are imported but never used; idbSet used once; AppState used for questionBank lookup and driveAccessToken). Imports from './matrix.js': openPracticeDrawer (unused), renderErrorResolutionDashboard, getLowestHealthQuestion.

A.2 Constants and config defaults
- STORAGE_KEY = 'checkpoint:config' (localStorage)
- PENALTY_KEY = 'checkpoint:protocolZero' (localStorage + both IDBs)
- STATE_KEY = 'checkpoint:state' (localStorage)
- TICK_MS = 1000 (declared; interval never started — see A.13)
- CLOCK_TOLERANCE_MS = 30_000 (30 s rollback tolerance)
- DRAW_THRESHOLD = 4 (pixels; squared compare dx*dx+dy*dy < 16 is ignored)
- BACKGROUND_GAP_THRESHOLD = 10_000 ms of tick silence = background suspension
- DEFAULT_CONFIG:
    enabled: false
    checkpoints: ['11:00', '17:00', '21:00']
    idleThresholdMin: 120
    graceMin: 15
    preNotifyMin: 10
    powMin: 10          (Proof-of-Work minutes)
    powIdlePauseSec: 45 (stylus idle pause)

A.3 Storage keys (dual-write IDB + localStorage)
- localStorage keys: 'checkpoint:config', 'checkpoint:state', 'checkpoint:protocolZero'.
- Dedicated IndexedDB: database name 'checkpoint-db', version 1, one object store 'kv' (out-of-line keys; put(value,key)). Helpers openIDB() (memoized via module var idbDB; resolves null on any error so everything degrades gracefully), cpIdbSet(key,value), cpIdbGet(key).
- Writes: setConfig() -> LS write + cpIdbSet('checkpoint:config') + persistState(). persistState() -> LS write (console.warn on quota/private failure) + cpIdbSet('checkpoint:state'). addPenaltyDate() -> LS write + cpIdbSet(PENALTY_KEY) + ALSO mirrors into the app's main IDB via storage.js idbSet(PENALTY_KEY, dates) ("belt-and-braces").
- persistState() serialized payload: phase, armedCheckpointTime, graceEndsAt, powStartedAt, powAccumulatedPausedMs, powPauseStartedAt, powMin, powIdleSec, powPaused, activeQuestionId, processedToday (array), processedDate, lastKnownNow, lastActivityAt.

A.4 Full state machine
Phases: 'disarmed' -> 'monitoring' -> 'pre-notify' -> 'grace' -> 'active' -> 'completed' | 'penalty'.
Core loop tick() (1 Hz by design):
1. Clock rollback (Fix 3): if now < lastKnownNow - 30000 -> clockRollbackDetected=true; firePenalty('Clock manipulation detected — Protocol Zero engaged.'); else lastKnownNow = max(now,lastKnownNow).
2. Background gap (Fix 1): gap = now - lastTickAt; if > 10000 ms then backgroundGapSec=floor(gap/1000); while in grace and past graceEndsAt -> firePenalty('Grace expired while you were away...'); while monitoring/pre-notify/disarmed -> checkMissedCheckpoints() which is an INTENTIONAL NO-OP (race-condition fix; arguments kept for signature compatibility). Else backgroundGapSec=0. lastTickAt=now.
3. Idle: idleMs from lastActivityAt (Infinity when null); day rollover resets processedToday and preNotifiedFor when processedDate != todayKey().
4. Checkpoint evaluation (Fix 5 revised) — runs when phase ∈ {disarmed, monitoring, pre-notify} and cfg.enabled. For each configured time t: occurrence occ = today at hh:mm; key = today + ':' + t (e.g. '2025-06-01:11:00'); skip if processedToday has key or now < occMs. ABSOLUTE TEMPORAL BARRIER: deadlineMs = occMs + graceMin*60000 (calendar-anchored epoch, does NOT slide on reopen).
   (a) engagedAfter = lastActivityAt > occMs -> mark processedToday, safe.
   (b) idle >= idleThresholdMin AND now > deadlineMs -> mark processed; firePenalty('Absolute grace window for HH:MM (N min from HH:MM) expired while offline — Protocol Zero engaged.')
   (c) idle >= idleThresholdMin within window -> mark processed; armCheckpoint(t, deadlineMs); break.
   (d) otherwise NOT marked processed — retried each tick until threshold crossed (bugfix note: previous version wrongly marked non-fired checkpoints processed).
   If !cfg.enabled -> phase='disarmed'.
5. Pre-notify: finds next upcoming occurrence across all times; if minToCp <= preNotifyMin (10) and > 0 and not preNotifiedFor.has(today+nextStr): sets phase='pre-notify'; Notification('⚠ Slump Sentry', 'Checkpoint at HH:MM — get to work now.'); showPreNotifyBanner(nextStr, ceil(minToCp)). Banner auto-dismisses after 60000 ms; manual dismiss button too.
6. Phase switch:
   - disarmed/monitoring/pre-notify -> normalize to monitoring (enabled) or disarmed.
   - grace: now >= graceEndsAt -> firePenalty('Grace window expired — Protocol Zero engaged.')
   - active: if !powPaused, powIdleSec += 1 per tick; at powIdleSec >= cfg.powIdlePauseSec (45) -> powPaused=true, powPauseStartedAt=now. If getPowRemainingSec(now) <= 0 -> firePenalty('Proof-of-Work timer expired — Protocol Zero engaged.').
7. persistState(); emit().
Transitions:
- armCheckpoint(cpTime, absoluteDeadlineMs): phase='grace'; graceEndsAt bound to the caller's absolute epoch when supplied (>0), else fallback Date.now()+graceMin*60000 (the test button passes no deadline, hence relative); Notification '🚨 Checkpoint ARMED'; showGraceModal(cpTime); persistState().
- initiateCheckpoint() [allowed from grace/pre-notify/monitoring]: q = getLowestHealthQuestion() (matrix.js); activeQuestion=q; phase='active'; PoW clock reset: powStartedAt=Date.now(), powAccumulatedPausedMs=0, powPauseStartedAt=null, powMinSnapshot=cfg.powMin, powIdleSec=0, powPaused=false, draw coords reset; requestWakeLock(); hideGraceModal(); FALLBACK: if q is null, shows a self-report lockdown object {id:'fallback', chapter:'Self-Directed Practice', subject:'general', easeFactor:2.5, status:'error', type:'self-report', correctAnswer:'', options:[], imageDataUrl:null, driveImageId:null, extractedText:'No due error questions found...'}. persistState().
- submitAnswer(answer) [active only]: hideLockdown, releaseWakeLock, evaluateAnswer(q,answer). Correct -> phase='completed'; toast 'ok' '✅ Checkpoint cleared. Discipline logged.'; after 3000 ms back to monitoring/disarmed. Incorrect -> firePenalty('Answer incorrect — Protocol Zero engaged.').
- abandonCheckpoint() [active only]: hideLockdown, releaseWakeLock, firePenalty('Checkpoint abandoned — Protocol Zero engaged.').
- firePenalty(reason): addPenaltyDate(todayKey()); phase='penalty'; clears activeQuestion/armedCheckpointTime/graceEndsAt/powStartedAt; hides lockdown + grace modal; releaseWakeLock; Notification('💀 PROTOCOL ZERO', reason); showPenaltyModal(reason,date); persistState(); after 8000 ms hide modal and return to monitoring/disarmed.
evaluateAnswer(q, answer) semantics: self-report returns boolean answer (non-boolean => false); integer compares parseFloat(q.correctAnswer) with tolerance <1e-9; multi compares sorted uppercase letter arrays (equal length required); single uppercase string equality; missing q => true.
getQuestionMode(q): 'self-report' when !q, q.type==='self-report', correctAnswer missing/empty-string/empty-array, or unknown shape; 'multi' when Array.isArray(correctAnswer); 'single' when /^[A-D]$/i; 'integer' when /^-?\d+(\.\d+)?$/.
PoW remaining math (Fix 6, timestamp-based): getPowRemainingSec(now) = ceil((powMinSnapshot*60000 - (now - powStartedAt - powAccumulatedPausedMs - [now-powPauseStartedAt if paused]))/1000), floored at 0 — immune to focus-event tick spam because remaining derives from absolute timestamps, not tick counts.

A.5 Exploit defenses table (verified against header comment FIX 1–8)
| # | Exploit | Defense implemented |
| 1 | Silent Death (background kill) | lastTickAt gap detection (>10 s); Wake Lock ('screen') during active; Notification API on arm/pre-notify/penalty; visibilitychange/focus resume guard named in header. NOTE: the resume catch-up handler checkMissedCheckpoints() is now a deliberate no-op; all catch-up math unified in tick()'s absolute-deadline logic. |
| 2 | Palm/Scroll fake activity | reportDrawingActivity(x,y) requires movement >= 4 px (Euclidean, squared compare vs DRAW_THRESHOLD^2) before crediting activity. |
| 3 | Time-Travel | lastKnownNow high-water mark; rollback > 30 s (CLOCK_TOLERANCE_MS) triggers Protocol Zero both live (tick) and on restore. |
| 4 | Force-Close mid-checkpoint | Entire state machine persisted atomically via persistState(); restoreState()/restoreStateFromRaw() rehydrate and resume modals or expire grace. |
| 5 | 1-Second Ghost Miss | processedToday Set keyed per calendar day+time; todaysOccurrence() never rolls to tomorrow; revised a–d logic keeps un-crossed checkpoints pending. |
| 6 | Focus-Spam Speedrun | Timestamp-based PoW timer (powStartedAt + accumulatedPausedMs); rapid ticks compute identical remaining; cannot fast-forward. |
| 7 | Multi-Correct Lockout | submitAnswer handles single/multi/integer/self-report; multi uses toggle UI (☐/✓ letters); integer input; self-report two-button honest evaluation. |
| 8 | LocalStorage Amnesia | IndexedDB primary + LS cache dual-write for config, state, penalties; restorePenalties() reads IDB on startup and re-caches to LS; survives localStorage.clear() and private-mode quota eviction. |

A.6 Protocol Zero penalty effects
- addPenaltyDate(date): appends YYYY-MM-DD to PENALTY_KEY list if absent (dedupe), triple-writes (LS + checkpoint-db + app main IDB), then re-renders BOTH graphs via renderErrorResolutionDashboard() ("Error Momentum sparkline + main predictive graph") and dispatches window event 'checkpoint:penalty' {detail:{date}} so app.js repaints the main predictive graph.
- Penalty modal copy states the effect: "A hard zero has been written to your 15-Day Error Momentum Engine AND your main predictive graph ... permanent jagged red valley that will take 2 weeks of flawless execution to smooth out", with ASCII preview '▁▁▁▂▃▄▅▆▇█▇▆▅▼0▼▅▆▇' where ▼0▼ is styled red (#f87171).
- restorePenalties(): on startup reads IDB; if dates exist, re-caches to LS, re-renders dashboard, dispatches 'checkpoint:penalty' {detail:{date: dates[dates.length-1]}}, emit().
- Test button 'Clear Penalties': removes LS key, cpIdbSet(PENALTY_KEY, []), re-renders graphs, dispatches 'checkpoint:penalty' {detail:{date:null}}.
- Shields: none implemented in this file (no shield data structures exist).

A.7 Proof-of-Work drawing/typing detection specifics
- reportDrawingActivity(x, y) (exported): ignores moves with dx²+dy² < 16 (< 4 px, anti palm/scroll); on acceptance stores lastDrawX/Y, sets lastActivityAt=now, resets powIdleSec=0, un-pauses PoW (adds now-powPauseStartedAt into powAccumulatedPausedMs, clears pause state), emit().
- reportTypingActivity() (exported): identical credit without movement threshold.
- Lockdown hint text tells the user: "Draw on the scratchpad (outside lockdown) to keep the timer ticking. Only movement > 4px counts."
- While active and unpaused, tick() itself increments powIdleSec every second; crossing cfg.powIdlePauseSec (default 45 s) pauses the countdown until activity resumes.

A.8 Wake Lock + Notifications
- requestWakeLock(): navigator.wakeLock.request('screen') inside try/catch; called on initiateCheckpoint. releaseWakeLock() on submit, abandon, penalty.
- notify(title,body): fires only if 'Notification' in window && permission === 'granted'. Master toggle in Control Center requests permission when still 'default'.

A.9 restoreState() atomicity / recovery semantics
- Two-stage hydrate: restoreStateFromRaw(null) applies the LS snapshot synchronously first, then async cpIdbGet(STATE_KEY) re-applies IDB as source of truth when it resolves. Same pattern for config (loadConfig) guarded by _configDirtySinceBoot — user edits made in the first few hundred ms are never clobbered by the stale in-flight IDB read (audit item [55]).
- On restore: clock rollback check against stored lastKnownNow (tolerance 30 s) -> penalty path with toast kind 'danger' '💀 CLOCK ROLLBACK DETECTED — Protocol Zero engaged.' then 6000 ms later return to monitoring/disarmed. Otherwise lastKnownNow=max(now,stored).
- processedToday restored only if ps.processedDate === today, else reset empty.
- Restores armed/grace/PoW fields; re-finds activeQuestion in AppState.questionBank by String(id) match.
- Grace expiry while away (now >= graceEndsAt) -> firePenalty('Grace expired while you were away — Protocol Zero engaged.'). Live grace (still valid + armedCheckpointTime) -> showGraceModal(armedCheckpointTime) rehydrated; comment block documents that graceEndsAt is an ABSOLUTE calendar-anchored epoch so refresh computes the exact same mathematical duration with zero dilation — reload cannot extend/reset the window, only honor or expire it.
- phase==='active' with a resolved question -> re-shows lockdown overlay.
- Ends with persistState().

A.10 Floating control center + overlay UI (ids/classes)
Control Center panel #cp-control-panel (.cpcc-* classes), sections:
- Status row: #cpcc-phase (class cpcc-phase-<phase>), #cpcc-next ("HH:MM (Nm)"), #cpcc-idle ("12m"/"∞").
- Badges: #cpcc-rollback-badge (cpcc-badge-danger '🕐 Clock Rollback Detected'), #cpcc-bgap-badge (cpcc-badge-warn '📱 Backgrounded Ns' with #cpcc-bgap-sec).
- Master toggle: checkbox #cpcc-enabled inside .cpcc-switch/.cpcc-slider; change -> setConfig({enabled}) + Notification permission request + message '🚨 Sentry ARMED'/'○ Disarmed'.
- Ignite zone #cpcc-ignite-zone (visible during grace) with #cpcc-ignite '🔒 INITIATE CHECKPOINT' + #cpcc-grace-timer live text.
- Checkpoint times: #cpcc-times-list rows (.cpcc-time-row: input[type=time] + remove btn .cpcc-time-x '✕'); '+ Add Time' #cpcc-add-time capped at MAX 8 times.
- Threshold inputs (min attributes): #cpcc-idle (min 5), #cpcc-grace (min 1), #cpcc-prenotify (min 1), #cpcc-pow (min 1), #cpcc-idlepause 'Stylus Idle Pause (sec)' (min 10).
- Save #cpcc-save: validates times with /^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/, requires >= 1 valid, parseInt fallbacks ||120 /||15 /||10 /||10 /||45.
- Test Controls: #cpcc-test-arm ('Arm Checkpoint Now', calls armCheckpoint('TEST'), requires enabled), #cpcc-test-prenotify (banner 'TEST'/10 min + notification), #cpcc-test-idle (sets lastActivityAt = now-(idleThresholdMin+5)*60000 and calls tick() directly), #cpcc-test-clear (see A.6).
- Message line #cpcc-msg (classes cpcc-msg-ok/warn), auto-clears after 3500 ms.
- Close #cpcc-close and backdrop click close; updateControlPanel() live-updates statuses only while panel display === 'flex'.
Floating ignite button #cp-ignite-float (with inner #cp-ignite-timer), display:flex during grace with live formatClock(rem) countdown, hidden otherwise. NOTE: the bottom-left hub button (variable cpHub) creation was removed from injectControlCenter(), and nothing in-file calls openControlPanel() — see A.13.
Grace modal #cp-grace-modal (.cp-grace-modal > .cp-grace-box): icon 🚨 (.cp-grace-icon), title .cp-grace-title 'CHECKPOINT ARMED', sub .cp-grace-sub 'Timestamp HH:MM hit while you were idle.', label .cp-grace-timer-label 'Grace remaining', live timer #cp-grace-timer-val (template default '15:00'; real countdown from graceEndsAt via setInterval 500 ms; class .critical toggled under 60 s), warning .cp-grace-warning 'If this hits zero, Protocol Zero scars your 15-Day graph.', ignite button #cp-grace-ignite-btn, hint showing cfg.graceMin. Sets body.style.overflow='hidden'; hideGraceModal clears interval + overflow.
Penalty modal #cp-penalty-modal (.cp-penalty-box): 💀 icon, title 'PROTOCOL ZERO', reason line, scarred date line, description paragraph, ASCII graph preview, Acknowledge button #cp-penalty-ack-btn.
Pre-notify banner #cp-prenotify-banner (.cp-prenotify-banner): text span .cp-prenotify-text '⚠ Slump Sentry: Checkpoint at HH:MM in ~N min. Get to work now.', dismiss .cp-prenotify-dismiss '✕', auto-remove after 60000 ms.
Lockdown overlay #checkpoint-lockdown (.checkpoint-lockdown): header .cp-lockdown-header/.cp-lockdown-title '🔒 CHECKPOINT LOCKDOWN'/.cp-lockdown-sub '<typeLabel> · answer + draw to exit'; typeLabel map: single 'Single-correct MCQ', multi 'Multi-correct (select ALL that apply)', integer 'Integer / numeric answer', self-report 'Self-evaluation (no answer key on file)'. Timer zone: .cp-timer-label 'Proof-of-Work Timer', #cp-timer-value (formatClock mm:ss), #cp-timer-paused '⏸ PAUSED — DRAW ON SCRATCHPAD'. Body: .cp-question-meta '⚠ <chapter> · EF <easeFactor> · <subject>' (EF defaults '2.5' via Number||2.5), image zone .cp-question-image-zone (img.cp-question-image; pending variant #cp-question-img.cp-img-pending with inline SVG 'Downloading question image…'), question text .cp-question-text from q.extractedText, options container #cp-options with buttons .cp-option (+ .cp-multi for multi) each containing .cp-opt-letter rendered '(A)' / '☐ (A)' / '✓ (A)', integer zone #cp-integer-zone with input#cp-integer-input.cp-integer-input, self-report zone #cp-selfreport-zone with .cp-selfreport-hint and buttons #cp-sr-correct '✓ I solved it correctly' / #cp-sr-wrong '✗ I got it wrong', scratchpad hint .cp-scratchpad-hint. Footer: #cp-abandon 'Abandon (triggers Protocol Zero)', submit #cp-submit (disabled until an answer is chosen; hidden for self-report which submits via its own buttons; replaced by .cp-footer-spacer).
Image handling: q.imageDataUrl used when length > 100, attribute-safe escaped ("->&quot;", "<->&lt;") against self-XSS; else lazy Drive load via fetchMediaFromDrive(q.driveImageId, AppState.driveAccessToken) polling up to 20 attempts x 500 ms for the token, caches fetched b64 back onto q.imageDataUrl.
Options parsing: /^\(?([A-Da-d])\)?[\s\.\)]*(.*)$/ splits letter/text from strings like 'A) Foo'; fallback synthesizes letters A,B,C… by index; blank texts filtered out.
Toast #checkpoint-toast (.checkpoint-toast, modifier cp-toast-<kind>; kinds used: 'danger', 'ok'), visible 6000 ms per call.
escapeForHtml(): local minimal escaper (&, <, >, ", ') used for lockdown strings.

A.11 Events emitted (payloads)
- window CustomEvent 'checkpoint:state', detail: { phase: string, powRemainingSec: number|null (null unless phase==='active'), powPaused: bool, clockRollbackDetected: bool, backgroundGapSec: number, config: getConfig() }. Dispatched from emit() — i.e., every tick, every activity report, and every config change.
- window CustomEvent 'checkpoint:penalty', detail: { date: 'YYYY-MM-DD' } on new scar and on IDB restore (last stored date), or { date: null } when penalties cleared via test control.
Consumed hooks: none listened to internally; relies on window.__checkpoint.getPhase() being polled by boot-sequence.js conflict detection.

A.12 Public/window API
window.__checkpoint = {
  getConfig, setConfig,
  reportDrawingActivity,      // (x, y)
  reportTypingActivity,
  initiate: initiateCheckpoint,
  submit: submitAnswer,
  abandon: abandonCheckpoint,
  getPhase: () => phase       // used by boot-sequence conflict check
}
ES exports additionally: getConfig, setConfig, reportDrawingActivity, reportTypingActivity, initiateCheckpoint, submitAnswer, abandonCheckpoint.

A.13 CRITICAL RUNTIME STATUS — monitoring loop deactivated
init() (waits for document.body via rAF) contains a block titled "TODAY'S LOOP: REMOVED": the 1 s monitoring tick + its restore/penalty paths are gone because that loop kept re-writing Protocol-Zero hard-zeros into the 15-day Fix Streak and re-arming on every reload. Concretely: tickInterval is declared but NEVER assigned; restoreState() is defined but NEVER invoked; no visibilitychange/refocus listeners are bound. Stored penalties are intentionally KEPT as plain graph history ("never re-armed, never re-written"). init performs: loadConfig(); injectControlCenter(); injectIgniteButton(); one-time renderErrorResolutionDashboard() repaint; window.__checkpoint assignment. Net effect: phase stays 'disarmed' at runtime; the entire machinery above is dormant unless driven manually through the panel's test controls (e.g., Simulate Idle invokes tick() directly) or the window.__checkpoint API. Because hub-button creation was also removed and openControlPanel() has no call site, the panel element exists appended but inert (hidden).

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
B. cns-load.js — CNS Load Index + "Junk Reps" Multiplier
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

B.1 Purpose and contract
- Computes a continuous CNS_LOAD score in [0, 1] per subject from rolling accuracy windows, temporal-divergence (τ) history, and session length; applies a stepped ELO yield multiplier to prevent grind-to-zero. PURE READ over studySecs/AppState.elo — never mutates them. Called from calculateEloMigration() BEFORE the ELO delta is finalized; multiplier applies to SUBJECT ELO GAIN ONLY (NOT qElo drift). Persists per-subject rolling history in localStorage.
- Dependencies: AppState.elo (window), studySecs (via bridge window._studySecsForCns set up by app.js).

B.2 Formula and telemetry inputs
CNS_LOAD = clamp01(0.50 * t_severity + 0.30 * accuracy_collapse + 0.20 * tau_inflation + 0.05 * streak_strain_bonus)
- t_severity = clamp01((sessionMinutes − 90) / 90), sessionMinutes = (studySecs[subject] − _sessionBaselineSecs)/60. Anchors: 90 min -> 0, 135 min -> 0.5, 180 min -> 1.0. SESSION_NORMAL_MIN = 90. Baseline snapshot taken at pomodoro quit (onPomodoroQuit) and at daily reset.
- accuracy_collapse = clamp01(max(0, acc50 − acc10)) over accLog ({correct,ts}, trimmed to ROLLING_50 = 50 entries); requires >= ROLLING_10 = 10 entries else 0.
- tau_inflation = clamp01((median(last TAU_WINDOW = 8 taus) / median(all taus from last BASELINE_WINDOW_MS = 7 days)) − 1) × 2 — scale: 50% slower = 1.0. Baseline needs >= 3 entries. τ per solve = actualTime / chapterAvgTime (computeTau; T_act<=0 -> 1; T_avg floor 1 s). tauLog entries {tau, ts}, trimmed to TAU_WINDOW*2 = 16.
- streak_strain_bonus = clamp01((streak − STREAK_STRAIN_THRESHOLD 10) / 20) reading the DOM element '#top-streak' digits; 0 below 10-day streaks, 1.0 at >= 30 days.

B.3 Yield table (value range [0,1]; bands lo-inclusive/hi-exclusive; last hi 1.01)
| CNS_LOAD | ELO yield multiplier | tauCap | badge | cssClass | label |
| 0.00–0.30 | 1.00 | null | 🌿 | cns-green | Soil moisture adequate. Roots still absorbing. |
| 0.30–0.55 | 0.85 | 1.50 | 🪴 | cns-warn | Photosynthesis slowing. Sap dropping. Pace the watering. |
| 0.55–0.75 | 0.65 | 1.25 | 🥀 | cns-amber | Drought setting in. Trees can survive, but new ones won't take. |
| 0.75–0.90 | 0.40 | 1.10 | 🪦 | cns-red | Cannot plant new seeds today. Existing saplings will drain if over-watered. |
| 0.90–1.01 | 0.20 | 0.95 | 🌑 | cns-black | Stop grinding — sleep debt is real. |
Comment: tauCap governs the next 5 question picks; anchors tabled from Bompa's supercompensation research.

B.4 Gates, vetoes, caps
- This file does NOT block practice modes directly. Its gating outputs are: the stepped ELO multiplier above, the per-band tauCap, plus two internal guards:
  • Anti-cheat veto: the ×0.20 band requires isCns09Allowed(subject) — the LAST TWO solves must each have had rolling-10 accuracy < 0.40 at their positions (recomputed historically); otherwise band clamps up to ×0.40 (YIELD_TABLE[3]). A single outlier cannot gate the student.
  • Early-days guard: when accLog.length < 10, effective CNS_LOAD is capped at 0.55 (so max ×0.85) — prevents tSeverity alone producing false positives.
- Downstream consumers that DO gate on this score: deload forced eligibility (>= 0.85 for 2 consecutive days, section C) and lifeline activation (fire >= 0.80, disable < 0.40, section D). Nightguard Tier 3 can force CNS = 1.0 externally (section E).

B.5 Decay / recovery rules
- Recovery is implicit: components decay as windows slide (accuracy recovers as good solves enter rolling-10; τ inflation falls as recent medians drop; tSeverity resets via baseline snapshots). Explicit resets: onPomodoroQuit(subject) snapshots current window._studySecsForCns[subject] into _sessionBaselineSecs and clears sessionStart (only meaningful if a pomodoro ran); resetDaily() (midnight) clears sessionStart and SNAPSHOTs (not zeroes) baselines — comment: zeroing caused a spurious post-midnight CNS spike for anyone grinding past 00:00 since studySecs keep accruing across midnight.
- logSolve(subject, correct, timeTaken, chapterAvgTime): pushes accuracy entry (trim 50), computes/pushes tau (trim 16), sets sessionStart if first solve of the local day, updates lastSolveTs, saves.

B.6 Daily tracking + consecutive-day checks
- _updateDailyCnsMax: _global.dailyCnsReadings [{date:'YYYY-MM-DD', maxCns}] keeps the LAST 14 DAYS; each computation caches cnsReading/cnsReadingTs per subject and saves.
- consecutiveCnsDays(threshold, n): the last n CALENDAR days must be strictly consecutive (gap !== exactly 1 day breaks the streak — readings 5 days apart do not count) and all have maxCns >= threshold. Used with (0.85, 2) by deload.

B.7 Reset bridges and window contract
- Reset bridges consumed: window._studySecsForCns (studySecs bridge), DOM #top-streak (streak), external callers invoke onPomodoroQuit (pomodoro quitTimer) and resetDaily (midnight cycle).
- Subject normalization: math/mathematics->maths, phys->physics, chem->chemistry, anything else->physics (ICU-safe manual YYYY-MM-DD keys throughout).
- window.__cnsLoad = CNSLoad = { computeCnsLoad(subject, studySecsObj) -> {cnsLoad(effective), band, multiplier, tauCap, badge, label, cssClass, components{tSeverity, accuracyCollapse, tauInflation, streakStrain}}, logSolve, onPomodoroQuit, getLastCnsReading(subject) (cached, no recompute), getMaxCnsReading() (max cached across physics/chemistry/maths — lifeline's source), consecutiveCnsDays, isCns09Allowed, resetDaily, computeTau, state getter }. Auto-init on import (_load()). Storage key: 'jeemax_cns_load_v1'.

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
C. deload.js — Deload Day Streak Protection
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

C.1 Flavors
- "Earned Rest" = manual deload (badge 🌿, label 'Earned Rest').
- "Bio-demand" = forced deload (badge 🌿🌿, label 'Bio-demand Recovery').
Both preserve streaks and decay CNS Load ("brain consolidating like a muscle between sets"). Anti-cheat: cooldown timers, circuit breaker, 48 h missed-day block. Scheduled from the Config panel via app.js scheduleDeloadFromUi(). Depends on window.__cnsLoad, window.AppState.moodMultiplier, window._deloadDailyHistoryFn.

C.2 Constants (exact tunings)
- LS_KEY 'jeemax_deload_v1'
- DELOAD_COOLDOWN_DAYS = 14
- DELOAD_MIN_STREAK = 7
- FORCED_CNS_THRESHOLD = 0.85
- FORCED_CONSECUTIVE_DAYS = 2
- FORCED_OVERRIDE_WINDOW_MIN = 30 (minutes to override with mood calibration)
- MISSED_DAY_BLOCK_HOURS = 48
- CIRCUIT_BREAKER_MAX = 3 (manual deloads in...)
- CIRCUIT_BREAKER_WINDOW = 30 (...days before lock)
- CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCK = 60 (days locked out after breaker fires)

C.3 State schema (version 1)
{ version:1, manualDeloads:[{date:'YYYY-MM-DD', scheduledAt:ISO}], forcedDeloads:[{date, triggeredAt:ISO, overridden:bool}], lastManualDeloadDate, lastForcedDeloadDate, forcedOverrideTimestamp (epoch ms deadline), forcedOverrideUsed, circuitBreaker:{ manualCountWindow:[{date}], lockedUntil:'YYYY-MM-DD'|null } }
Load merges defaults defensively; save warns on quota failure. Entries older than 60 days pruned by resetDaily(), which also clears stale forced override timestamp/flag.

C.4 Manual scheduling rules (canScheduleManualDeload -> scheduleManualDeload)
Gates in order, each returning {ok:false, reason}:
1. Today already a deload day -> 'Today is already a deload day.'
2. Streak < 7 -> 'Need ≥ 7-day streak. You have N.'
3. Cooldown: daysSince(lastManualDeloadDate) < 14 -> 'Cooldown: N day(s) until next manual deload.' Corrupt stored date yields _daysBetween() === -1 and fail-closed refusal 'Cooldown check unavailable — stored deload date is corrupt.' (comment: returning the threshold itself previously bypassed the gate like NaN did).
4. Circuit breaker lockedUntil > today -> 'Circuit breaker active. Manual deloads locked until YYYY-MM-DD.'
5. 48 h missed-day block: checks yesterday AND two-days-ago entries in window._deloadDailyHistoryFn() history; a missing entry or count===0 counts as missed -> 'Cannot schedule deload within 48h of a missed day. Rebuild momentum first.' Fresh installs with empty history SKIP this check.
On success: pushes {date, scheduledAt ISO}, sets lastManualDeloadDate, records into circuitBreaker.manualCountWindow (pruned to entries >= cutoff 30 days back); if count >= 3 -> lockedUntil = today + 60 days and counter resets. Returns {ok:true, deload:{date, type:'manual', badge:'🌿'}}.
Streak reader _getStreakDays(): prefers ledger history from window._deloadDailyHistoryFn() — builds set of dates with count>0, walks back consecutively from today (or yesterday if today inactive) counting; falls back to parsing digits out of DOM '#top-streak' textContent (which can be stale before async history load — documented).

C.5 Forced scheduling rules (isForcedDeloadEligible -> triggerForcedDeload)
Requires ALL of: today not already deload; streak >= 7; window.__cnsLoad.consecutiveCnsDays(0.85, 2) true (reason otherwise 'CNS_LOAD not ≥ 85% for 2 consecutive days.'); moodMultiplier ((window.AppState.moodMultiplier)||1.0) <= 0.70 — reason 'Mood not in fried state (mood multiplier must be 0.70).'; no forced entry already today. triggerForcedDeload() records {date, triggeredAt, overridden:false}, sets lastForcedDeloadDate, forcedOverrideTimestamp = Date.now()+30*60*1000, forcedOverrideUsed=false; returns overrideWindowMs.

C.6 Override (escape hatch for forced days)
overrideForcedDeload(moodMultiplier) succeeds only if: a non-overridden forced entry exists today; override not already used today; Date.now() <= forcedOverrideTimestamp (30-min window); and moodMultiplier === 1.0 EXACTLY ('Override requires mood calibration to baseline (1.0)'). Marks entry overridden=true, consumes the single daily override.

C.7 Queries / Earned Rest day behavior / streak preservation
- isTodayDeload(); getTodayDeloadStatus() -> {active:true, type:'manual'|'forced', badge:'🌿'|'🌿🌿', label:'Earned Rest'|'Bio-demand Recovery'} or null; isDeloadDate(dateStr) true for manual entries and NON-OVERRIDDEN forced entries (for calendar/heat-map rendering); getDeloadFrame() returns forest-metaphor frame text: manual — 'Took a deload. Your brain is consolidating — like a muscle between sets. Tree-wilted leaves are pruned, not dead. Streak preserved.'; forced — 'Bio-demand recovery active. CNS overload detected — the forest is forcing a rest cycle. Override with mood calibration within 30 min, or let the roots drink.'
- Streak preservation mechanics inside this module: deload dates are exposed so the host app can treat them as non-breaking days; the module's own streak counter treats any history day with count>0 as active and does not count deload flags itself. DST-safe helpers (_daysBackKey uses setDate; _daysBetween parses at fixed T12:00:00) prevent calendar drift corrupting windows.

C.8 UI touchpoints and API
- No DOM injection inside deload.js. Touchpoints: Config panel scheduling via app.js scheduleDeloadFromUi(); streak read fallback from '#top-streak'; badges/labels/frame strings above for whatever host UI renders them.
- export const DeloadEngine = { canScheduleManualDeload, scheduleManualDeload, isForcedDeloadEligible, triggerForcedDeload, overrideForcedDeload, isTodayDeload, getTodayDeloadStatus, isDeloadDate, getDeloadFrame, resetDaily, get state() }; window.__deload = DeloadEngine. Auto-init _load() on import. No events emitted.

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
D. lifeline.js — Flow-State Dynamic Lifeline
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

D.1 Constants
- LIFELINE_FIRE_THRESHOLD = 0.80 (activation)
- LIFELINE_DISABLE_THRESHOLD = 0.40 (auto-disable once recovered mid-session)
- LIFELINE_ELO_MULTIPLIER = 0.65 applied to ELO yield on lifeline-assisted solves
- LIFELINE_LOOKBACK_MIN = 3 (chapter needs >= 3 unsolved questions in the easier band)

D.2 Per-mode P_win offsets (LIFELINE_MODE_OFFSETS)
P_win model: P_win = 1 / (1 + 10^((Q − E)/400)); higher P_win = easier questions (qElo below userElo).
- standard: PwinMin 0.80, PwinMax 0.90 (shift from implicit 0.70–0.85)
- flow:     PwinMin 0.88, PwinMax 0.96 (shift from 0.75–0.85)
- hardcore: PwinMin 0.55, PwinMax 0.72 (shift from 0.35–0.50, "relative relief")
Unknown mode falls back to standard.

D.3 evaluateLifeline(subject, chapter, mode, userElo, questionBank) — pure query
Step order:
1. CNS source: window.__cnsLoad.getMaxCnsReading() — cross-subject MAX, deliberately the same source used by dismiss re-fire and getStatus (comment: per-subject readings disagreed and produced contradictory banners).
2. cnsLoad < 0.40 -> clears _state.active and returns inactive, reason 'CNS below disable threshold' (this IS the deactivation path).
3. cnsLoad < 0.80 -> inactive, reason 'CNS_LOAD NN% < 80%'.
4. Opt-out: _state.dismissedForSolve -> inactive, reason 'Opted out for this solve'.
5. Lookback band requirement: easierQElo = invert PwinMin via Q = E + 400*log10((1/P)−1) (P clamped 0.01–0.99, result rounded; ratio <= 0 returns userElo unchanged). Counts bank questions matching subject+chapter, status 'unsolved' OR undefined OR 'error', finite q.qElo <= easierQElo, and !q.isAnomaly. Fewer than 3 -> inactive, reason 'Only N easier questions available (need ≥3)'.
6. Eligible -> {active:true, pwinMin, pwinMax, reason:'Lifeline eligible', cnsLoad} WITHOUT mutating state; caller must call activateLifeline() separately after the question is actually picked (sets active=true, dismissedForSolve=false).

D.4 Opt-out per solve + re-fire
dismissForCurrentSolve(): sets dismissedForSolve=true, active=false, clears any pending re-fire timer, then setTimeout 20*60*1000 ms (spec: "re-fire after 20 minutes if CNS_LOAD hasn't dropped"): clears dismissal and re-activates ONLY if getMaxCnsReading() still >= 0.80. resetAfterSolve() clears dismissal and kills the timer after a solve completes. tagCurrentSolve(questionId) records currentSolveId and resets dismissal for the next question. isLifelineAssisted(questionId) = _state.active && currentSolveId === questionId (telemetry tagging).

D.5 Yield + status
getEloMultiplier() -> 0.65 when _state.active, else 1.0. getStatus() -> { active: _state.active && !_state.dismissedForSolve && cnsLoad >= 0.80, cnsLoad, dismissed, threshold: 0.80 }.

D.6 UI surfacing, persistence, API
- No DOM code in this file — getStatus() exists specifically "for UI rendering" by host modules (banner consumption lives elsewhere).
- Persistence: NONE. _state = {active:false, dismissedForSolve:false, currentSolveId:null} is in-memory only; no localStorage/IndexedDB keys.
- export const Lifeline = { evaluateLifeline, activateLifeline, dismissForCurrentSolve, resetAfterSolve, tagCurrentSolve, isLifelineAssisted, getEloMultiplier, getStatus, get state() }; window.__lifeline = Lifeline. No events emitted; sole dependency window.__cnsLoad.

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
E. nightguard.js — Post-23:00 "Diminishing Returns" Guard
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DISCREPANCY NOTE: the task brief described this file as overnight maintenance with pending-write flush, midnight rollover steps, guards during inactivity, and intervals. The ACTUAL file contains none of those — no flush logic, no setInterval/setTimeout scheduler, no rollover driver (resetDaily() is exported for EXTERNAL invocation, presumably from the midnight cycle). Documented below is what really exists.

E.1 Purpose and three tiers
When local hour crosses 23:00, stepped ELO yield degradation:
| Tier | Local hours | Multiplier | Badge | cssClass | Dismissal | Label |
| tier1 | 23–01 (wraps midnight) | 0.80 | 🌙 | night-blue | yes, manual only (autoDismissMs 0) | Memory consolidation active in 1h. Tap if you must. |
| tier2 | 01–03 | 0.55 | 🌑 | night-amber | yes, 5 s auto-dismiss (5000) | Hippocampal encoding rate dropping. Sleep is the best tutoring session. |
| tier3 | 03–24 catch-all | 0.20 | 🛌 | night-red | NO — uninterruptible modal; requires caller-enforced 3 s long-press override; forceCns:true (force CNS_LOAD=1.0); skyBlue tint | You are not learning. You are reinforcing noise. Save your hours. |
Tier 3 practical upper bound: hour >= 8 lifts the guard even though spec says "03:00+" (comment: post-sunrise studying passed the most neurochemically harmful window).
Other constants: SLEEP_DEBT_GAP_HOURS = 6, SLEEP_DEBT_CONSECUTIVE = 3, SLEEP_DEBT_MOOD_PENALTY = 0.85, SLEEP_DEBT_WINDOW_DAYS = 7.

E.2 State + storage
LS_KEY 'jeemax_nightguard_v1'. Persisted fields: clockCheatDetected(+clockCheatDetectedAt), lateOverrideCount (weekly), overrideCountResetDate, dismissed(+dismissedAt ISO), forcedCnsActive, sessionRecords [{date:'YYYY-MM-DD', endHour, startHour}] (keep last 30), sleepDebtDates ['YYYY-MM-DD']. Transient fields stripped by _save(): _monotonicBaseline, _lastSeenNow, _lastSeenPerf, _cachedTier, _cachedTierTs, _cachedTierHour. Architecture: PURE READ — never mutates AppState/studySecs/questionBank.

E.3 Clock-cheat detection (checkClockCheat())
Check 1: Date.now() moved backwards > 5 minutes versus tracked _lastSeenNow -> cheat.
Check 1b: forward wall-clock gap > 6 h while local hour is 23:00–08:00, corroborated against performance.now(): device considered asleep if perfElapsed >= wallElapsed * 0.9 (fail-safe default = slept when perf unavailable); large awake gap -> cheat. Comment: normal overnight sleep produces the same wall signature, so monotonic corroboration is mandatory.
Check 2: pomodoro window.__pomodoro.getTimerStartTime() more than 60 s in the future -> cheat.
Effects: locks highest tier (tier3) with label 'Clock manipulation detected. Locked at highest tier.', forcedCnsActive=true; flag AUTO-CLEARS 1 h after detection. Monotonic baseline refreshed hourly. Note: an explicit user override (3 s hold dismissal) still wins even during cheat, and is cleared again at midnight.

E.4 Tier resolution + accessors
resolveTier(): result cached 10 s (also keyed on current hour) to avoid tier-entry delays. Returns null before 23:00 / after dawn cap / when dismissed. Shape: {tier, active:true, forceCns, skyBlue, clockCheat, multiplier, badge, cssClass, dismissible, label, autoDismissMs?}. getMultiplier() (1.0 when inactive), isActive(), shouldForceCns().
dismissCurrentTier(): tiers 1/2 only ('This tier requires long-press override (3s)' for tier3); sets dismissed/dismissedAt, invalidates cache. recordOverride(): caller enforces the 3 s hold; weekly-count reset (lateOverrideCount zeroed if > 7 days since overrideCountResetDate), increments count, sets dismissed.

E.5 Sleep-Debt Ledger
logSessionEnd()/logSessionStart() hooked from pomodoro.js quitTimer/startTimer. Hour recorded as h + minutes/60; records keyed by local date, keep 30 days. Debt computation (_computeSleepDebt, run on every session start): for consecutive dated pairs, gap = (24 − prev.endHour) + curr.startHour when ended-late/started-early (wraps midnight naturally), else startHour − endHour; 0 < gapHours < 6 flags curr.date (dedup + sorted).
getConsecutiveSleepDebtDays(): prunes flags older than 7 days (comment: without pruning ONE bad 3-day stretch held the ×0.85 penalty forever), requires the streak to be LIVE (include today or yesterday), counts backward-consecutive calendar days. getSleepDebtMoodPenalty() -> 0.85 when consecutive >= 3 else 1.0 (feeds moodMultiplier downstream). isTodaySleepDebt().

E.6 Morning cat-banner warnings
getLateOverrideCount(); getOverrideWarning() only before noon (local hour < 12): >= 4 overrides -> '🛌 Used late override Nx this week. CNS likely compromised today.'; >= 2 -> '🌙 Late override used Nx. Consider earlier sessions.'; 1 -> '🌙 Late-night override used. Sleep quality may be affected.'; else null.

E.7 Midnight reset + modal trigger
resetDaily() (external midnight call): dismissed=false, dismissedAt=null, forcedCnsActive=false, tier cache cleared, prunes sleep-debt flags outside the 7-day window.
checkAndShowTier3Modal(): invoked from the cat-banner telemetry loop; only acts when status.tier === 'tier3' AND static DOM element '#nightguard-modal' exists and lacks class 'active'; adds class 'active' to the modal and 'nightguard-tint' to document.body (sky-blue background tint); returns true when shown. The modal markup itself lives outside this module.

E.8 API
window.__nightGuard = NightGuard = { resolveTier, getMultiplier, isActive, shouldForceCns, dismissCurrentTier, recordOverride, logSessionEnd, logSessionStart, getConsecutiveSleepDebtDays, getSleepDebtMoodPenalty, isTodaySleepDebt, getLateOverrideCount, getOverrideWarning, resetDaily, getStatus, checkClockCheat, checkAndShowTier3Modal, get state() }. getStatus() shape: {active, tier:name, multiplier, badge, cssClass, label, dismissible, forceCns, skyBlue, clockCheat, lateOverrideCount, sleepDebtConsecutive}. Auto-_load() at import. No events emitted; no timers scheduled internally.

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
F. boot-sequence.js — "Daily Briefing" cyberpunk boot sequence (P6)
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

F.1 Trigger model + storage flags
Self-wiring IIFE (guard window.__bootSeqInit). Shows ONCE PER LOCAL CALENDAR DAY, first open after the daily reset — hooked into runNewDayCycle in app.js, REPLACING the bare Vibe Check popup. Guard flag: localStorage 'jeemax_boot_seq_date' = 'YYYY-MM-DD', written by finish() — EVEN A FULL SKIP COUNTS AS SEEN. If localStorage.getItem throws, maybeShow() refuses to run (fail-closed).

F.2 Timing constants
RETRY_MS = 5000 (defer retry when a conflicting overlay owns the screen); MAX_DEFER_RETRIES = 6 (~30 s then give up permanently for the session); CHAR_MS = 16 ms per typed character (whole-line instant when reduced-motion); LINE_PAUSE_MS = 200 between lines; 700 ms hold after final line before advancing to S2; arming countdown 3 s at 1 s intervals; body-missing retry 500 ms.

F.3 Conflict deferral (what it gates around)
_conflictingOverlay() returns true when '#nightguard-modal' has class 'active', or window.__checkpoint.getPhase() ∈ {'grace','active','penalty'} (comment: __checkpoint isn't assigned yet on cold boot since checkpoint.js inits on DOMContentLoaded, so the phase check effectively bites only on the live-midnight path; Night Guard covers cold boot). Under conflict, retry every 5 s up to 6 times, then abandon. It gates NOTHING in the app itself — it replaces the Vibe Check and always lets ESC/SKIP through.

F.4 Step machine
STEPS = ['boot','subject','mood','path','pomo','chapter','mode'] (7 steps). HUD progress dots render STEPS.length − 1 = 6 dots (boot is intro, not a goal): .bootseq-dot with .is-done (passed) / .is-active (glowing cyan, scale 1.25). HARDCORE_CAP = 8 mirrors MODE_TUNING.hardcore.capPerDay (live value preferred via window.MODE_TUNING). Context _ctx = {subject, mood, path, chapter, mode}.
Global controls every step: [ESC] aborts whole briefing (writes daily guard, plays modalClose); [⌫ BACKSPACE] one step back (only from step >= mood; MID-ARMING it instead aborts the countdown and replays the timer prompt — a phantom startTimer is impossible); [1-9] select option on choice screens; ANY KEY skips the boot animation. Overlay claims focus({preventScroll:true}).

F.5 Step-by-step visuals/sounds/duration
S1 boot: terminal typing of BOOT_LINES — '> INITIALIZING GRIND PROTOCOL v2.7', '> SPACED REPETITION ENGINE .......... ONLINE', '> ELO CORE .......................... NOMINAL', '> ERROR VAULT ....................... SEALED', '> WELCOME BACK, OPERATOR.' Char-by-char via setInterval CHAR_MS=16 with blinking cursor span .bootseq-cursor (8×15 px cyan #22d3ee, blink 1s steps(2)); 200 ms pause per completed line; reduced-motion prints lines instantly with same pauses; 700 ms after last line -> S2. SFX: 'blip' when the overlay builds. Skip: any key -> immediate _next('subject') (timers cleared first — bugfix note about an orphaned typing chain yanking users back).
S2 subject 'PICK YOUR ARENA, OPERATOR.': ⚛️ Physics / 🧪 Chemistry / 📐 Maths -> _ctx.subject; sfx 'select'.
S3 mood "HOW'S THE BRAIN BATTERY?" (vibe check folded into calibrateMood()): 🥱 Fried desc 'targets × 0.70' (val 'sad') / 😐 Steady 'targets × 1.00' ('neutral') / 🔥 Locked 'targets × 1.20' ('happy'). Calls window.calibrateMood(val) — rescales activeTargets and persists jeemax_mood_multiplier (same as the old popup; fire-and-forget).
S4 path 'WHAT ARE WE COOKING TODAY?': ☣ Error Matrix 'Priority queue — weakest errors first' (sfx 'confirm' -> _launchMatrixQueue()); 📖 Question Bank 'Fresh grind — chapters, flow & hardcore' (sfx 'select' -> continues in-flow). Matrix landing: finish(true) silent-close; awaits window.switchTab('errors') via Promise; force-arms priority queue (activateDailyQueue, fallback toggleDailyQueue — force-arm, never toggle, so a stale on-state isn't flipped off); resolves queue ids via window._getDailyQueueSnapshot(), maps to live bank objects, seeds AppState.currentSubject/currentChapter from qs[0], opens practice with startPracticeWithQuestion(qs, 0); solving advances via app's own Next/Prev loop.
S5 pomo 'LOCK IN WITH A TIMER?': subtitle shows last config from window.__pomodoro.getConfig() formatted 'N min focus · M min chill · K round · stopwatch · SUBJECT', or 'no saved config — defaults 50/10'. Options: ⚡ Lock In 'Arm the timer with your last config' -> _armPomodoro(); ⏩ Raw Dog 'No timer — straight to the grind' -> S6. Arming sequence: unlocks pomodoro AudioContext via window.initAudioContext() INSIDE the click gesture (so ignition chime can play); applyPomoConfig() prefill; sets '#pomo-subject' to the chosen arena subject and calls changeStudySubject(); renders big digit .bootseq-arm-num starting '3' ticking down 1 s per step (sfx 'tick' each), sub-text 'Arming focus timer' or 'Arming stopwatch tracking', config line (subject upper), CANCEL button (.bootseq-skip); hint '[ESC] ABORT ARM · IGNITING IN n'. At 0: sfx 'success' + window.startTimer() -> S6.
S6 chapter 'CHOOSE A CHAPTER, OPERATOR.': lists AppState.chapters[subject] ranked ASCENDING by health from window.getChapterHealth(subject, chapter) (fallback 50) — same model as the app's Chapter Health Grid so ordering matches in-app; each option shows desc 'chapter health N%' and right meta 'N Q' (questionBank count, subjects normalized). Empty bank -> message + button 'FEED QUESTIONS ▸' which finish()es and opens window.openModal('upload-modal').
S7 mode 'SELECT YOUR CHALLENGE LEVEL.': 🎯 Flow State desc 'P_win 0.75–0.85 · gentle cadence'; ⚡ Hardcore desc 'P_win 0.35–0.50 · 1.8× payout · 8/day cap' (cap from live MODE_TUNING) or 'Daily cap reached — come back tomorrow' when disabled; 🧱 Standard 'All questions, no gimmicks'. Gating: hardcore disabled when AppState.hardcoreDailyDate === today && AppState.hardcoreDailyCount >= cap; Flow AND Hardcore disabled when the chosen chapter has 0 untouched questions — untouched mirrors app.js _isUnexecutedModeQuestion: status null/'unsolved'/'unexecuted' AND no historyLogs entries AND no firstAttemptResult ('correct'/'incorrect') AND no telemetry (solveCount>0, lastReviewedAt, lastSolvedAt, timeTaken>0) AND no errorReason; disabled desc 'No untouched questions in this chapter — pick Standard'; disabled buttons use .is-disabled (opacity .4, no hover transform). Choose -> sfx 'confirm' -> _launchQBPractice(): finish(true); awaits switchTab('practice'); selectSubject(subject); openChapterDetail(chapter); empty chapter -> list view empty state; Standard (or mode with no untouched candidates) -> startPracticeWithQuestion(currentChapterQuestions, 0) to avoid tripping the app's 'No more Flow-eligible questions' alert; Flow -> startFlowPractice(); Hardcore -> startHardcorePractice().

F.6 Styling and sounds summary
Styles injected once as <style id="boot-seq-styles">. Overlay div.bootseq: position fixed inset 0, z-index 2147483000, Chakra Petch/IBM Plex Sans monospace stack, layered radial purple/cyan gradients over near-black (#05030f→#0b0618→#070410), inset vignette shadow; ::before 44 px grid masked radially; ::after scanlines animating bootseq-scroll 9 s linear infinite. Panel .bootseq-panel width min(560px,88vw), violet border rgba(139,92,246,.45), backdrop-filter blur(6px), animations bootseq-rise .5s cubic-bezier(.22,.9,.3,1) both + bootseq-breathe 4.5 s ease-in-out .6 s infinite alternate. Title cyan #22d3ee letter-spacing .34em; screen titles #e9d5ff; option buttons .bootseq-opt hover translateX(4px)+cyan glow; focus-visible 2px #22d3ee outline; media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) disables scanline/cursor/panel animations and transitions; max-width 520px tweaks padding and hides the hint. SFX map via _sfx(name)->window.FX.sound(name) (self-gated on sound pref, safe no-op stub): blip=overlay build, select=choices, tick=arming seconds, success=arm ignition, confirm=matrix pick + mode choose, soft=back/abort, modalClose=ESC/SKIP finish.

F.7 Element ids, teardown, public surface
Element ids/classes: style#boot-seq-styles; div.bootseq[role=dialog][aria-modal][aria-label='Daily Briefing'] > div.bootseq-panel > p.bootseq-title 'JEEMaxxing // Daily Briefing', div.bootseq-progress (dots), div.bootseq-body (min-height 224px), footer .bootseq-footer with .bootseq-back '‹ BACK' (hidden until step >= mood), span.bootseq-hint ('[ESC] ABORT · ANY KEY TO SKIP' on boot; '[1-N] SELECT · [ESC] ABORT' otherwise), button.bootseq-skip 'SKIP ▸'; choice grids .bootseq-opts with .bootseq-opt buttons (.bootseq-opt-icon/-label/-desc/-key/-chmeta); arming box .bootseq-arm (-num/-sub/-config).
Teardown safety: _clearTimers() on every step transition/back/finish; finish(noSound) removes key listener + overlay, plays modalClose unless silent, writes the daily guard; build exception path tears down partial mounts and FALLS BACK to the classic Vibe Check via window.openModal('mood-modal') so mood calibration is never lost. Skipping leaves moodMultiplier untouched (documented intentional).
Public API: window.BootSequence = { maybeShow, skip: finish, _test: { isActive, step, ctx, pendingTimers, arming (getters), press(key), armNow(), lsKey, todayKey } } — _test exposes internals for the Node smoke harness.

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
G. theme.js — JEEMaxxing Theme Engine v2
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

G.1 Model
Accent themes (document.documentElement attribute data-theme) × appearance mode (data-mode: 'midnight' | 'dusk'). Pill button dock next to the logo; dropdown re-skins the whole app LIVE by flipping these attributes — the actual CSS custom-property VALUES per theme/mode live in the stylesheet, NOT in this file (theme.js ships only registry metadata + swatch dot colors). IIFE guarded by window.__themeInit.

G.2 Theme registry (all 7, exact values)
| id | name | desc | swatch dots (conic-gradient stops) |
| furnace | Furnace | Amber heat · stock build | #ffb224, #ff7a1a, #3ddcff |
| synthwave | Synthwave | Violet neon haze | #c084fc, #e879f9, #818cf8 |
| glacier | Glacier | Ice-blue deep focus | #38bdf8, #0ea5e9, #22d3ee |
| overgrowth | Overgrowth | Bioluminescent green | #34d399, #10b981, #a3e635 |
| bloodmoon | Blood Moon | Crimson aggression | #ef4444, #f97316, #fbbf24 |
| sakura | Sakura | Rose quartz calm | #f472b6, #fb7185, #c4b5fd |
| stealth | Stealth | Monochrome ops | #e5e7eb, #9ca3af, #4b5563 |
Swatch CSS: conic-gradient(from 210deg, d0, d1, d2, d0). Unknown id falls back to THEMES[0] (furnace).

G.3 Modes
| id | name | icon | desc |
| midnight | Midnight | 🌙 | Deep-night terminal (DEFAULT) |
| dusk | Dusk | 🌆 | Evening glass · ~65% dark |
Mode differences beyond the attribute flip are implemented in CSS; applyMode validates the incoming id against the registry (anything unrecognized silently becomes 'midnight').

G.4 Persistence
localStorage 'jeemax_theme' (default 'furnace') and 'jeemax_mode' (default 'midnight'); reads wrapped in try/catch with defaults on failure. Initial mount applies stored values with persist=false (no redundant writes).

G.5 Live re-skin mechanism + events
apply(id, persist): sets data-theme attr, persists unless told not to, updates the dock button dot background (conic swatch) and title 'Theme: X', toggles .active + aria-selected on panel options (.theme-opt[data-theme-id]), dispatches document CustomEvent 'jeemax:themechange' {detail:{theme:id}}.
applyMode(id, persist): validates id, sets data-mode attr, persists, toggles .active + aria-pressed on .theme-mode-btn[data-mode-id], dispatches document CustomEvent 'jeemax:modechange' {detail:{mode:id}}.

G.6 UI elements
Dock button button#theme-btn.theme-btn (aria-haspopup='listbox', aria-expanded) containing span.theme-btn-dot (swatch), span.theme-btn-label 'Theme', span.theme-btn-chev '▾'; inserted AFTER '.logo-container' inside '#sidebar' (fallback '.sidebar', fallback first child), remount-retried via requestAnimationFrame until the sidebar exists. Panel div#theme-panel.theme-panel role='listbox' aria-label='App theme' appended to body: mode group div.theme-panel-modes (role group) with .theme-mode-btn buttons (icon + name, title=desc), header div.theme-panel-head 'THEME ENGINE', then seven .theme-opt[role=option][data-theme-id] rows each with .theme-opt-dot (inline conic swatch), .theme-opt-name, .theme-opt-desc, ✓ marker span.theme-opt-check.
Behavior: button click toggles open/close (panel.open class + aria-expanded sync); capture-phase document pointerdown closes when clicking outside both; Escape closes; reposition on window resize and scroll (capture). Positioning: left clamped to [12, innerWidth − panelW − 12], top = button.bottom + 10, flipping ABOVE (button.top − panelH − 10, min 12) when overflowing the bottom; assumes panel ~264×440 for measurement fallback.

G.7 Public API
window.JEEMaxTheme = { set(id) — apply + persist; get() — current theme id; setMode(id) — apply + persist; getMode(); themes: THEMES; modes: MODES }. No other events consumed; no timers.

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
H. dashboard-clean.js v2 — Dashboard declutter + layout FAB
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

H.1 Contract
Append-only declutter layer; explicitly makes NO changes to app.js / pomodoro.js / bento.js / storage.js. IIFE guarded by window.__dashCleanInit. Poll-driven boot: tick() runs immediately (or on DOMContentLoaded), retries waiting for targets up to 60 attempts × 400 ms (~24 s).

H.2 Everything it injects/modifies
1. Global banner hiding: adds class 'dc-active' to document.body as soon as body exists — CSS elsewhere hides the cat banner + header badge row. (FACTUAL NOTE: the task brief expected a "Time Bank card"; grep confirms ZERO time-bank references in this file — no such card exists here.)
2. Dashboard-only header hiding: body class 'dc-dash-active' toggled to mirror whether '#view-dashboard' currently has class 'active' ("Grind Dashboard / Welcome back" header hidden by CSS only while the dashboard tab is live). A MutationObserver (attributeFilter ['class'], guard flag dash.__dcViewWatched) keeps the flag synced and also CLOSES the FAB panel whenever the dashboard loses .active.
3. Cloud Link reparent: moves the '.sync-cluster' (queried from '.header-badges .sync-cluster' or anywhere) into the settings card whose <h3> matches /cloud/i; fallbacks: first '.settings-card' inside '#view-settings', then any '.settings-card'. Tags the cluster with 'dc-cloud-row' so it is moved only once.
4. Momentum legend: inside '.dash-card-momentum', inserts div.dc-mom-legend immediately AFTER '.graph-display' containing four items: Bull (i.lg-bull), Bear (i.lg-bear), Target lock (i.lg-target), Projection (i.lg-proj). Idempotent (skips if .dc-mom-legend present).
5. Layout FAB + floating panel: pencil-SVG button #dc-layout-fab (19×19 stroke icon, title/aria-label 'Layout settings', aria-expanded) appended to the dashboard view; click toggles panel class 'open' + fab class 'active'; closes on capture-phase outside pointerdown and on Escape. The "panel" IS the existing bento toolbar: the first '.bento-toolbar' found (dashboard-scoped query, then global) is ADOPTED by adding class 'dc-fab-panel'. If the toolbar never appears within the poll budget, the FAB removes itself.
6. Sidebar collapse persistence: key 'jeemax_sidebar_collapsed' (values '1'|'0'). On boot, saved '1' re-adds class 'collapsed' to '#sidebar'/'.sidebar' and syncs the '.collapse-btn' aria-label/title to 'Expand sidebar' — v4-shell note: chevron SVG kept intact because app.js toggleSidebar owns the label; innerHTML is never nuked here. A MutationObserver on the sidebar's class attribute persists '1'/'0' on EVERY change ("toggleSidebar() flips this class → we persist every change"), guarded by sb.__dcCollapseWatched.

H.3 Flags, keys, API summary
Body classes injected: dc-active, dc-dash-active. Injected classes: dc-cloud-row, dc-mom-legend (children dc-lg, lg-bull/lg-bear/lg-target/lg-proj), dc-fab-panel, fab active. Guard flags: window.__dashCleanInit, sb.__dcCollapseWatched, dash.__dcViewWatched. localStorage: ONLY 'jeemax_sidebar_collapsed'. Public API: window.__dashClean = { open, close } (opens/closes the layout panel). No events emitted; no sounds.

##  PART 7 - VISUAL ENGINES & PWA PLUMBING
##  source: .docparts/06-visuals-pwa.txt
