JEEMaxxing — Visual Engines & PWA Plumbing (Master Documentation Excerpt)

All facts below were read directly from the current files on disk (full reads: grove-islands.js 2560 lines, forest-bg.js 337 lines, candlestick-engine.js 634 lines, sw.js 206 lines; plus manifest.webmanifest, app.js call sites, asset globs).

============================================================================
A. grove-islands.js — "Grove Islands v2: Skyborne Groves" study archipelago
============================================================================

A.1 What it is / contract
- IIFE, single-init guard window.__groveIslandsInit. Header declares it a from-scratch v2 rebuild that preserves every v1 contract.
- User-facing feature: every solved question grows vegetation on a 3D floating island. Solving plants per-subject trees; global ELO unlocks 5 biomes and 10 species; a dashboard card hosts a mini auto-orbiting island; overlays give a Store, an Archipelago map, and a full-screen explorer with orbit/pinch/wheel camera.

A.2 Storage
- localStorage 'jeemax_grove_v1' (LS_GROVE): { activeBiome, activeSpecies, subjectSpecies{physics,chemistry,maths}, daily{'YYYY-MM-DD':{<biomeId>:{count,species[],bySubject{subject:[speciesId,...]}}}} }. Defaults via defaultGrove(): activeBiome 'temperate', activeSpecies 'pine', subjectSpecies {physics:'pine', chemistry:'oak', maths:'pine'}, daily {}.
- localStorage 'jeemax_forest_daily_v1' (LS_DAILY): {'YYYY-MM-DD': {physics, chemistry, maths, updatedAt}} — the daily solve ledger shared with the old forest feature.
- IndexedDB mirror via window._idbMirror.set/get(key) for BOTH keys (survives localStorage wipes). saveGrove() bumps groveRev++, clears periodMemo, writes LS then IDB. restoreFromIDB() merges daily entries with per-subject Math.max + max updatedAt, and restores grove only if LS_GROVE absent. Reset Grove (#gi-reset) clears localStorage AND sets IDB mirror to null, then location.reload().
- Reads (not writes): window.AppState.elo.global (globalElo(), floor, default 1200), window.solved.{physics,chemistry,maths}, DOM counters #physics-count/#chemistry-count/#maths-count.

A.3 Growth physics
- 3 solves = 1 tree (STAGES_PER_TREE = 3). Stage of the partial tree = remainder−1 index into STAGE_SCALE [0.45, 0.72, 1.0] (Sapling/Young/Mature per header).
- Slot spiral: slot i gets rr = SLOT_BASE + sqrt((i+0.5)/slots) * rout, angle = i * 2.39996 (golden angle) ± 0.15 rad jitter; y from terrain heightAt; rot random; size variance 'var' = 0.9..1.12.
- Slots clamp(MIN_SLOTS=6 … MAX_SLOTS=240): slots = clamp(max(6, ceil(agg.count/3)), 6, 240).
- Island radius grows with sqrt(slots): islandRadiusForSlots = max(ISLAND_R=9, 9*sqrt(slots/6)), also floored at MIN_TREE_SPACING(2.6)*sqrt(slots)/(1.24*0.72). viewFor(): rout = min( max(R*0.72, 2.6*sqrt(slots)/1.24), max(0.5, R*0.70 − SLOT_BASE) ). maybeExpand() rebuilds the world when slots count or radius change >0.01, behind a sky-colored fade.
- Aggregation periodCount(biomeId, period): periods today/yesterday(dayOffsetKey 1)/week(6 back)/month(29)/year(364)/all over grove.daily keys; live-today total merged in for non-yesterday periods; memoized in periodMemo keyed groveRev|biome|period|today|liveTotal, memo wiped when >64 entries.
- Attribution: MutationObserver on the three counters → attributeSinceSnapshot() diffs vs lastSubj snapshot; tick() runs every 1500 ms via setInterval. Midnight rollover: if todayKey() != seenDay, reset lastTotal to 0, clear snapshot + memo, and SKIP seeding/attributing that one tick (v1 race fix). seedStore()/restoreAssert() keep LS_DAILY and live counters mutually consistent (max-merge both directions).
- ELO gates — biomes: temperate 0, tropical 1300, autumn 1600, snow 1900, savanna 2200. Species: pine/oak 0, palm/broadleaf 1300, maple/birch 1600, snowpine/icebirch 1900, baobab/acacia 2200. checkElo() plays sndUnlock and fires action toasts ("Travel ⛵" / "Plant 🌱"). subjectSpecies() falls back to the first unlocked species if the chosen one is locked.

A.4 Rendering architecture
- One shared THREE.Scene, TWO renderers: miniRenderer on canvas #gi-card (pixelRatio min(dpr,1.5), PCFShadowMap, sRGB, ACESFilmic toneMapping exposure 1.1) and lazy fullRenderer on #gi-full-canvas (PCFSoftShadowMap, adaptive DPR).
- Adaptive resolution governor: DPR_TIERS [1.75, 1.4, 1.1, 0.85]; adaptDPR keeps emaMs (EMA alpha 0.05), evaluated every 120 frames: emaMs > 26 ms steps tier down-resolution (tier++), < 13 ms steps up.
- Terrain: value-noise fbm (3 octaves, amplitudes 0.9/0.38/0.16 at freq 1/2.3/5.1); radial disc wobbled by Rw = R*(1 + wobA*sin(theta*f1 + seed%7) + wobB*sin(theta*f2 + seed%13)) with wobA 0.05–0.09, wobB 0.03–0.06, f1∈{3,4}, f2∈{6–8}; dome height pow(edge,0.72)*2.05 + TOP_Y − 1.0; a carved stream polyline (Gaussian trough depth 0.62, width max(0.65, R*0.075)); mesh NR=22 rings x NS=72 segments, vertex colors ground→groundVar noise blend, sand when y < SEA_Y+0.55, rock when slope > 0.34, ±0.03 jitter; winding self-heal flips indices if summed normals.y < 0. Rocky underside cone to depth R*0.62+2.2 (dirt 0x5d4a36). Coast foam ring RingGeometry(R*1.01, R*1.12, 72) opacity 0.5. Stream ribbon width 0.42, y = SEA_Y+0.1, opacity 0.85.

A.5 Trees / rocks / water rendering
- SPECIES_BUILDERS: 10 low-poly species built from cylinders/cones/icosahedrons, vertex-painted (jitter seeded mulberry32(hashStr(speciesId))), ~3 units tall at scale 1, merged into ONE BufferGeometry per species (speciesGeometry cached in GEO).
- Instanced tree field (createTreeField): one THREE.InstancedMesh per species present (DynamicDrawUsage, castShadow, frustumCulled=false) → 1 draw call per species regardless of tree count. GPU wind sway injected via material.onBeforeCompile: displacement sw=max(y−0.6,0); x += sin(uTime*1.35+ph)*sw*0.045; z += cos(uTime*1.02+ph*1.3)*sw*0.035, phase ph read from instanceMatrix translation.
- field.sync(want, opts) does full reconciliation keyed by slot index; new entries get scale-spring growth (accel 40*dt toward target, damping exp(−6.5*dt), settled when |Δscale|<0.004 and |vel|<0.02); ceremony drop-in: drop=1 decays over 0.5 s, y-offset drop*2.6, squash 1+drop*0.15 (fast springs squash up to −0.12); unused instances parked at y=−999 scale 0.0001; springs pruned on removal/species change (v1 leak fix).
- Planting FX (createPlantFX, preallocated): 36-particle dirt puff pool (12 puffs/plant, life 0.5–0.85 s, gravity −4.4/s²), 3 shockring meshes (RingGeometry 0.9→1.0, expand scale 0.6→~4.8 easeOutCubic over 0.7 s, fade 0.65→0). Triggered only when ceremony && motionOK() && !travelingFlag.
- Water: sea = CircleGeometry(1,72) MeshLambertMaterial color 0x2c7da6 scaled max(34, R*3.2); gentle bob y = SEA_Y + sin(t*0.9)*0.05 (motion-gated); foam ring hugs each island's coastline.
- Props (instanced per biome config): rock (DodecahedronGeometry), bush (squashed icosahedron), grass (cone), flower (tiny icosahedron), mushroom (hemisphere cap + cylinder stem), snowmound (squashed sphere); counts per biome e.g. temperate: 8 rocks, 12 bushes, 26 grass, 12 flowers, 7 mushrooms; placement rejects spots below SEA_Y+0.25 (8 tries).
- Animals: exactly one resident critter per biome — rabbit (temperate, wander/hop), parrot (tropical, fly bob speed 1.4), hedgehog (autumn, wander), penguin (snow, waddle rotation.z sin(t*6)*0.12), meerkat (savanna, pop-up scale.y). All ticks no-op when !motionOK().

A.6 Sky, clouds/weather, day-night
- Sky shader sphere r=260 (BackSide): uniforms uTop/uHorizon/uNight/uSunDir/uTime; horizon blend smoothstep(−0.06, 0.5, dir.y); procedural sun disc pow(dot,160)*0.5*day + tight core smoothstep(0.9994,0.99985,dot); starfield from floor(dir*160) hash, step(0.9974), masked by uNight and altitude, twinkle sin(uTime*2+st*40).
- Day-night cycle: YES, wall-clock driven. nightFactor01() = clamp(|u−0.5|*2) where u=(hours+min/60)/24 (0 at noon, 1 at midnight; forced 0 under reduced motion). applyEnvironment mixes biome palette toward night DSKY {top 0x0b1026, horizon 0x1a2340, fog 0x10162a} with k=night*0.78; sun color lerps to moonlit 0x8ea2ff, intensity L.sunInt*(0.12+0.88*(1−night)); hemi *(1−0.45night); ambient 0.13+0.09night; moonFill DirectionalLight 0x8ea2ff intensity night*0.5; cloud sprite opacity 0.85−0.5night; fireflies opacity night*0.95. Sun azimuth rotates once per real day ((hrs/24)*2π − π/2). Re-applied at most every 30 s inside loop(). Also re-applied on custom event 'jeemax:modechange' (theme change listener).
- Clouds: 6–8 clusters of 3–5 soft billboard sprites each (scale 1.6–4.0 base), orbiting at radius R*(1.25–1.9), altitude 9–14, angular speed 0.008–0.02 rad/s.
- Weather particles (ParticleField, fully preallocated, biome-configured): temperate rise/glow 110 @size 0.16 speed 0.3 sway 0.5; tropical fall/petal 130 @0.22 speed 0.55 sway 0.9; autumn fall/leaf 160 @0.26 speed 0.85 sway 1.3; snow fall/soft 260 @0.15 speed 0.95; savanna drift/dust 110 @0.15. Fall wraps at y<0.15 → 6.5–8, rise wraps at y>7 → 0.2, drift wraps at ±rMax*1.25.
- Birds: 3 two-triangle-wing circlers (radius R*(1.15–1.65), h = TOP_Y+4.5–8, flap sin(t*9)*0.55); not spawned when reduced motion.
- Fireflies: 70 additive glow points above ground, twinkle sin(t*sp*3+ph), visible only curNight ≥ 0.03.

A.7 Interactions
- Mini card canvas #gi-card: click or Enter/Space opens the full explorer (role="button", aria-label, tabindex 0). Card buttons #gi-btn-map (Archipelago map), #gi-btn-store (store modal), #gi-btn-full (explorer); badge #gi-unlock-badge shows "n/5".
- Full explorer orbit (bindOrbit on #gi-full-canvas): 1-finger drag azimuth az −= dx*0.0055 with fling inertia azVel = −dx*0.0011 decaying exp(−4dt); 2-finger pinch zoom (dist clamped [orbitDistFor(radius)*0.45, 90]); wheel zoom ±7% per notch (preventDefault, passive:false); idle auto-rotate resumes 4000 ms after last pointer input (az += dt*0.07). orbitDistFor(R)=clamp(R*1.9, 20, 75); openFull always resets dist so the camera never spawns inside a big island (v1 fix).
- travel(biomeId): fade overlay #gi-fade to the target biome's sky-horizon color over 400 ms, sndTravel, swap world, fade back after 60 ms. Instant path when !built or reduced motion.
- Escape closes store/map/explorer; backdrop click closes store/map modals.

A.8 DOM ids/classes created (chrome)
- Body-level: #gi-toasts (.gi-toast/.gi-toast-msg/.gi-toast-btn, max 3 stacked, 6200 ms life, .gi-toast-out exit 520 ms), #gi-float (+.gi-floaty spans, 1050 ms), #gi-fade, #gi-full-overlay.gi-full-overlay (.open) containing #gi-full-canvas, .gi-full-top (.gi-full-brand, .gi-elo-pill #gi-full-elo, buttons #gi-full-store/#gi-full-map/#gi-full-close), #gi-full-periods (buttons data-period today/yesterday/week/month/year/all), .gi-full-hud (#gi-full-chip, #gi-full-name, #gi-full-sub, #gi-full-trees, #gi-full-meta), body class gi-full-open.
- Store modal #gi-store-overlay.gi-modal-overlay: #gi-store-close, title "The Grove Market", #gi-store-elo, tabs .gi-tab-btn[data-tab=islands|trees|subjects], bodies #gi-tab-islands/#gi-tab-trees/#gi-tab-subjects; cards .gi-store-card (+.gi-locked/.gi-active) with .gi-store-icon/.gi-store-name/.gi-store-desc/.gi-req progress bar (.gi-req-bar i width % = clamp(elo/threshold*100)) and .gi-store-btn; Subjects tab rows .gi-subj-row with select.gi-subject-select[data-subj].
- Map modal #gi-map-overlay: #gi-map-close, #gi-map-svg (640x440 inline SVG: radial sea gradient giSea, 26 wave squiggles seeded mulberry32(7), dashed quadratic "trade routes" between consecutive biome mapPos with alternating control offsets ±26/±18, islands as ellipse pairs rx=p.r ry=p.r*0.72, per-species SVG glyphs via mapGlyph — pine/snowpine polygon triangle, palm curved-frond paths, acacia trunk rect + flat ellipse, baobab tapered trunk + circle, default trunk rect + circle — active ring stroke r+9, ⛵ marker, locked nodes show 🔒 + "Name · N ELO"), #gi-map-elo footer, #gi-reset.
- Dashboard card mount (mountCard): targets '#view-dashboard .dash-card-momentum' (fallback: any .dash-card whose .box-title matches /momentum/i); hides original card children as .gi-orig, adds class gi-island-active to the card, injects #gi-host with .gi-canvas-wrap (#gi-card canvas + #gi-empty "Answer questions to grow a tree 🌱") and .gi-card-hud (#gi-chip, #gi-name, #gi-sub, #gi-species, #gi-bar inside .gi-bar, #gi-next, buttons). HUD text writes go through setTxt/setHtml caches so identical strings never touch the DOM.
- Static fallback: showFallbackPoster() appends .gi-fallback (biome emoji + gradient background) into #gi-host when WebGL/engine fails; engineFailed sessions warn "Grove runs in static fallback mode".

A.9 Audio cues (WebAudio, lazily created AudioContext resumed on first pointerdown)
- sndPlant: sine 392 Hz 0.09 s + sine 587.33 Hz 0.14 s delayed 0.07 s (gain ~0.04–0.045).
- sndUnlock: sine arpeggio 523.25/659.25/783.99/1046.5 Hz, 0.22 s each spaced 0.09 s.
- sndTravel: 330 Hz + 494 Hz + 659 Hz (last triangle), staggered 0.1 s.
- Envelopes: exponential ramps 0.0001 → gain → 0.0001. Fires on every 3rd solve (before%3===0 && count%3===1) with floaty "🌱 New sapling!".

A.10 Performance techniques
- rAF loop TRULY cancels when idle (raf=null when document.hidden, engineFailed, unbuilt, or neither mini nor full view visible) — v1 woke every frame to do nothing.
- Frame interval budget: 16 ms (~60fps) when fullOpen else 34 ms (~30fps); dt clamp 0.05 s.
- Preallocated pools everywhere (FX particles/rings, weather particles, fireflies, birds, clouds); geometry/material caches GEO/MAT/cGeo; instancing for trees, props; HUD write caching; memoized period aggregation; ResizeObserver on both canvases + window resize; IntersectionObserver (threshold 0.02) on #gi-host; detectMini() poll every 800 ms checks offsetParent + #view-dashboard.active.
- Reduced motion (motionOK()): html.fx-effects-off class OR prefers-reduced-motion:reduce disables ceremony drops, spring animation still settles but drop skipped, water bob, auto-orbit, birds spawn, animal ticks, particle updates, and the day-night darkening (returns 0).

A.11 Public API / hooks
- window.__groveIslands = { travel(biomeId[, instant]), openStore(), openMap(), openFull(), closeFull(), state() → grove, elo() → globalElo, trees() → entry count, view() → {period, slots, agg}, __debug() → scene stats (built, engineFailed, night, sceneChildren/types, worldRadius, worldGroupChildren, miniPos, renderer triangles/calls) }.
- window.__giRefs() → { scene, world, miniCam, fullCam, waterMesh, miniRenderer, fullRenderer }.
- window.__groveCard = { card, host } set at mount.
- Consumes: window._idbMirror, window.AppState.elo.global, window.solved, event 'jeemax:modechange', counter elements #physics-count/#chemistry-count/#maths-count.
- Engine loading: ensureThree() tries './vendor/three/three.module.min.js' first (importWithTimeout 4000 ms), then https://esm.sh/three@0.160.0, cdn.jsdelivr.net three@0.160.0 build/three.module.min.js, unpkg same (9000 ms each); total failure marks engineFailed and shows the poster.

A.12 Boot sequence
buildChrome → mountCard → ensureThree → restoreFromIDB → firstRun detected AFTER IDB restore (v1 fix) → initScene → applyEnvironment(active biome) → buildWorld(biome, viewFor(biome,'today')) → syncTrees(true) → ensureLoop + intervals (tick 1500 ms, detectMini 800 ms) → updateHUD → welcome toast after 900 ms on first run. worldPeriod default 'today' for the card, fullPeriod default 'all' for the explorer.

============================================================================
B. forest-bg.js — "Living World" WebGL wallpaper
============================================================================

B.1 Purpose & isolation
- Optional full-screen 3D wallpaper BEHIND the app UI: time-of-day sky, voxel forest sized by your solved-question history, orbiting camera, juice layer (flowers, pollen, stars, campfire, growth bursts/shockwaves, streak-warmed light). Self-contained IIFE guarded by window.__forestBgInit; explicitly must NOT load lab files forest-juice.js / forest-island-juice.js.
- Failure-safe: if the juice build throws, juice=null and the plain wallpaper still renders; render loop guards every frame against missing objects; engine-load failure shows a monospace warning toast div (⚠ message, bottom-center, 6 s) and reverts to the grid background.

B.2 Storage & persistence keys
- localStorage 'jeemax_forest_bg' = '1'|'0' (enabled flag).
- localStorage 'jeemax_forest_bg_op' = opacity string; default 0.5, clamped 0.1–1; also mirrored to CSS var --forest-bg-op and canvas.style.opacity.

B.3 Toggle UI (injected)
- Button #forest-bg-btn.forest-bg-btn inserted into #sidebar after #theme-btn (fallback .logo-container, then first child): inner HTML span.fb-dot 🌲 + span.fb-lbl "World" + span.fb-chev ▾; retries every 300 ms until sidebar exists.
- Popup #forest-bg-pop.forest-bg-pop appended to body: switch #fb-sw, range #fb-op (min 10, max 100, value 50), note text ("🕒 Sky syncs to your clock · camera orbits · flowers, fireflies, a campfire & stars dress the world · the forest blooms with every solve / + tap · pauses when hidden"). openPop/closePop with aria-expanded; positionPop clamps to 12 px viewport margins; outside pointerdown (capture) and Escape close it.
- While enabled, setGridFade(on) zeroes .bg-grid-overlay and all .ambient-glow opacities.

B.4 Scene constants
- Canvas #forest-bg-canvas fixed, z-index 0, pointer-events:none, opacity transition .7s.
- Camera PerspectiveCamera fov 48, near 0.1, far 900, start (0,95,140) lookAt (0,4,0); slow orbit yaw += dt*0.06 rad/s on radius 140, height 95.
- Pixel ratio guard: min(devicePixelRatio, iPhone|iPod ? 1 : 1.5) (iPads/touch laptops capped 1.5, not punished to 1.0). Clear color 0x070809. FogExp2 density 0.006.
- Terrain: PlaneGeometry 360x360 (half=180), 140x140 segments displaced by heightAt(x,z) = 3-octave value noise ((amps 8 / 2.5 / 0.8 at freq 0.035 / 0.09 / 0.2)) minus a crater max(0, 3.4 − dd*0.5) centered at (−9, 7). Waterline WL = −1.1; water plane color 0x244a60, transparent 0.8, roughness 0.12, metalness 0.4, bobs WL + sin(elT*0.6)*0.04.
- CAP = 6000 trees maximum (even-stride resample beyond that); FRAME = 1000/30 (≈30 fps cap).

B.5 Trees (voxel/Minecraft style)
- Four box-built species merged into shared geometries: spruceGeo (physics), roundGeo (chemistry), goldenGeo (maths, gold foliage 0.98/0.76/0.16), oakGeo (decor). Each box gets hash-based color jitter ±0.07/2; paintBox colors top/side/bottom faces differently.
- Placement: buildSpots grid spacing 2.4 over ±172, requires height ≥ WL+0.25 and local slope hypot/1.2 ≤ 0.6, sorted centre-first.
- Per-tree data mapping (placeTrees): scale base = (0.55 + clamp((qElo−800)/2200,0,1)*1.1 + hash±0.08) * (oak?0.85:1) * bgSizeF(difficulty) where bgSizeF = 0.6 + 0.7*d (d default 0.5); vertical variance sy = 0.82+hash*0.5; oak probability bgOak(): explicit q.oak flag, else FNV-1a hash < 10%.
- Shared treeMat MeshStandardMaterial (vertexColors, roughness 0.82) with onBeforeCompile wind sway: sw=max(y−0.7,0); x += sin(uTime*1.3+ph)*sw*0.030; z += cos(uTime*1.0+ph)*sw*0.024, ph = instanceX*0.6+instanceZ*0.6.
- Rebuild triggers: MutationObserver on #physics-count/#chemistry-count/#maths-count (debounced 250 ms), a 30 s setInterval comparing bgSig() = questionBank.length + '|' + solvedCount + '|' + liveCounterTotal, and manual refresh().
- Data source: solvedBank() filters window.questionBank status==='solved', cached against window.__jmaxDataDirty signature + 60 s TTL; historical() = lastReviewedAt date ≠ today, todayReal() = == today; filler trees pad up to the live counter totals (random subject, qElo 1100–1800, difficulty 0.5).

B.6 Day-night cycle (YES)
- TOD keyframe table at t = 0 / 22 / 50 / 78 / 100 (% of day), each defining skyTop/skyBot/sunColor/sunIntensity/hemi/fog, e.g. midnight {top 0x0a0e1c, bot 0x141a2a, sun 0x3a4a6a, sunI 0.15}, noon {top 0x4a7ec0, bot 0xc4dcec, sun 0xfff2e0, sunI 1.15}, dawn t=22 {bot 0xe8956a}, dusk t=78 {bot 0xe07a44}. applyTOD lerps between neighbors and moves the sun position x = (v/100−0.5)*160, y=70, z=30.
- realTOD() from wall clock; re-applied every 30000 ms in frame(); nightFactor = |u−0.5|*2 drives fireflies/stars/campfire.

B.7 Juice layer (guarded try/catch around whole build)
- Flowers: ≤2600 screen-space speckle Points (size 1.7, sizeAttenuation:false, additive), 5-color palette 0x4cc9ff/0x39d98a/0xffb224/0xff7ab8/0xfff3b0, opacity pulse 0.42+0.16*sin(el*1.3)+night*0.10.
- Pollen motes: 90 drifting points in box ±45 x/z, y 5–34, opacity (0.16+0.10*sin(el*0.7))*(1−night*0.8).
- Star dome: 240 points radius 330–380, height 40–360, opacity night*0.9.
- Campfire: findCamp() scans rings r=14→36 step 4 for flat ground h∈[0.6,2.6] slope≤0.5 (fallback fixed (22,−14)); PointLight 0xff8a3c distance 60 decay 2 flicker 0.7+0.3*|sin(el*9)| intensity (0.5+2.4*night)*flicker; glow sprite scale 16, core sprite scale 5; 50 rising embers (life 1.1–2.4 s, vy 0.6–1.3, color fades 1.0/0.5/0.15 * life fraction).
- Growth bursts: pool of 600 spark particles (queue drained ≤40/frame; 14 sparks per new tree, gravity −2.2/s², life 0.7–1.3 s) plus additive RingGeometry(2, 3.4, 24) shockwave rings scaling 1→7 over 700 ms fading 0.7→0, capped 8 rings per growth event; burst positions taken from the newly added tree slots.
- Streak-warm light: every 2 s parses integer from #top-streak textContent; warmth k=min(1,streak/7)*0.3 lerps sun (and hemi at k*0.6) toward 0xffb066.
- Extra lights: cool rim DirectionalLight 0x66ccff 0.22 at (−120,90,−120), warm fill 0xffe6c4 0.14 at (120,50,−90).
- Ambient night fireflies (separate from juice): 160 amber points across the terrain, opacity night*0.9.

B.8 Loop & reduced motion
- frame(t): exits entirely (raf=null, no re-request) when disabled or document.hidden; restarts via visibilitychange listener → startLoop(). Frame gate `t-last < FRAME` skips work (~30 fps), dt clamp 0.05.
- Reduced-motion handling: NONE in this file — grep confirms no matchMedia('prefers-reduced-motion') or fx-effects-off reference here (that gating lives in grove-islands.js). Its power saving comes from the hidden-tab pause and the 30 fps cap instead.

B.9 Engine load & public API
- loadThree() CDN chain ONLY: esm.sh/three@0.160.0 → jsdelivr npm three@0.160.0 build/three.module.js → unpkg same (no vendored copy — contrast with grove-islands.js).
- window.__forestBG = { show() → setEnabled(true), hide() → setEnabled(false), setOpacity(v), refresh() → force rebuild }.
- Consumes window.questionBank, window.__jmaxDataDirty, DOM ids physics-count / chemistry-count / maths-count / top-streak.

============================================================================
C. candlestick-engine.js — SVG OHLC chart renderer
============================================================================

C.1 Shape & exports
- Pure vanilla ES module, no deps. Exports: drawCandlesticks(svg, counts, opts), countsToOHLC(counts, opts), predictNext(counts, days=5), extractCountsFromSvg(container), CANDLE_COLORS (= COLOR).
- COLOR palette: up "#22c55e", down "#f87171", flat "#8b5cf6" (purple), grid "rgba(255,255,255,0.045)", axis "rgba(255,255,255,0.32)", divider "rgba(139,92,246,0.28)".

C.2 Data shape & OHLC synthesis (countsToOHLC)
- Input: scalar number[] oldest→newest + opts { wickScale=0.45, penaltyFlags=[] }.
- open[i] = close[i−1] (index 0 open = its own value); close[i] = raw[i]; wick = rollingStd(values, i, window=5) * wickScale; high = max(open,close)+wick; low = max(0, min(open,close)−wick). penaltyFlags[i] forces close (and previous day's open) to 0. NaN/corrupt values fall back to previous close. Output rounded to 2 dp, carries isPenalty boolean.

C.3 Projection (predictNext)
- Ordinary least squares over array index; returns { predictions[] (clamped ≥0), slope, intercept (2dp), r2 (3dp) }; fewer than 2 points → zeros. Default horizon days=5.

C.4 drawCandlesticks options & defaults
- width 320, height 80, penaltyFlags [], showPrediction true, predDays 5, compact false (sparkline mode: no axes/gridlines/labels/P0 badges/LOCK line), invert false (true = green means value DOWN, for error counts), valueLabel "solves", valuePrecision 1, labelFn(index,candle), targetValue. (The doc comment also lists opts.getX but the implementation does not destructure/use it.)
- Sets viewBox "0 0 width height" and preserveAspectRatio "none"; clears the SVG first (clearNode).
- Sanitization before consumers: null/NaN replaced by last finite value (or 0) — one NaN used to NaN-poison slope/max/rawMax and blank the whole chart. Single-point series padded with a duplicate candle whose isPenalty is stripped.
- Prediction candles chain opens from the last historical close; wick widens per step vol*0.45*(1+i*0.15); drawn as hollow dashed rects (stroke-dasharray 2 2, opacity 0.6, wick opacity 0.55).
- Layout: padding compact 4 px all around vs normal L34/R14/T12/B20; plotW/plotH floored at 10; history band = 70% of plot width, prediction = 30%; candle bodyW = min(band*0.62, compact?6:14).
- Y-domain includes targetValue; headroom rawMax + 8% range + 0.5, floor max(0, rawMin − 5%).

C.5 Color rules (momentum/target logic)
- Candle color ignores open-vs-close finance delta entirely: green when close meets effectiveTarget (explicit targetValue, else mean of ALL closes incl. predictions); invert swaps >= for <=. isPenalty candles are ALWAYS red; prediction candles use themeColor = momentumColor(pred.slope): > +0.2 green, < −0.2 red, else purple flat.
- "Bull Run" combustion streak: ≥3 consecutive target-meeting HISTORY candles promotes the body rect to class "candle-hyper-charged" (otherwise "candle-normal"); penalties and predictions break the streak.

C.6 Axes, gridlines, decorations
- Grid: 5 horizontal lines at 0/25/50/75/100% of domain with right-aligned axis labels (font 9px 'Space Grotesk'; integers when v≥10 else 1dp).
- Prediction divider: vertical dashed (3 3) purple line at history/prediction boundary + gradient area fill url(#cnd-pred-grad) (vertical linearGradient themeColor 0.22→0 opacity) + dashed polyline (width 1.6, dasharray 4 3, opacity 0.85) through predicted closes.
- "Target Lock" barrier: edge-to-edge cyan rgba(6,182,212,0.6) line dasharray 4 2 width 1.5 at y(targetValue) with right-aligned 8px bold "LOCK" label; drawn beneath candles; domain expanded so it stays visible.
- Penalty markers: red column band (dasharray 3 2, opacity 0.45) + "P0" badge (16x10 red rounded rect at top, white 7px text).
- TODAY marker label above the last historical candle (8px, letter-spacing 1).
- Sparse X labels via labelFn, every ceil(candleCount/7) candles, bottom center 9px.
- defs: filter#cnd-glow (feGaussianBlur stdDeviation 1.6 + feMerge) applied to solid candle bodies; gradient cnd-pred-grad.
- Candle bodies: filled rects rx compact?1:2, opacity 0.92 when close≥open else 0.88, glow filter; wicks stroke width compact?1:1.3 round caps.

C.7 Tooltip + crosshair + hover audio
- Per-svg persistent state svg._candleState { tooltip, lastHoveredIndex } survives redraws; mousemove/mouseleave handlers swapped out via svg._candleHandlers before re-add (fixes handler/DOM accumulation that stalled hover).
- Crosshair: dashed vertical rgba(255,255,255,0.18) and horizontal 0.12 lines + white hover dot r=3 stroked in the candle's color; nearest candle found by x distance.
- Tooltip: single lazily-created div.candle-tooltip appended to svg.parentElement (wrapper coerced position:relative); glass style inline (background rgba(8,8,14,0.96), border rgba(139,92,246,0.35), radius 10px, backdrop-filter blur(10px), 'Space Grotesk'); content = label (opts.labelFn or "Day N") + optional PRED chip (purple) + P0 chip (red) + Open/High/Low/Close grid at valuePrecision digits (High green, Low red) with valueLabel suffix + delta row arrow ▲/▼/■ with abs(change) and percent, colored invert-aware. Positioning flips left of cursor when within tipW=168/tipH=132 bounds; clamped to chart.
- Hover telemetry bleep playHoverTick(metTarget): gated by window.FX.wantHoverSound() (fx.js prefs.sound && prefs.hover); shared lazy AudioContext stored on window._candleTickCtx, closed on pagehide/visibilitychange-hidden (bound once via window._candleTickBound), resumed when suspended; met-target candle → clean sine 880 Hz, missed → sawtooth 220 Hz; gain envelope 0.0001 → 0.03 peak at +5 ms → 0.0001 at +80 ms (exponential ramp floor), oscillator stopped at 90 ms. Fires only when the crosshair lands on a NEW candle index (per-chart, was previously a shared global).
- Returns { slope, r2, themeColor, candles: all } to callers.

C.8 extractCountsFromSvg(container)
- Preferred: container.dataset.momentumCounts JSON array of finite numbers (matrix.js stamps data-momentum-counts). Fallbacks: polyline points → take every y; else rect heights (rounded); else circle cy values; else [].

C.9 Mounting & consumers
- Imported by app.js line 86: import { drawCandlesticks, extractCountsFromSvg } from './candlestick-engine.js'.
- Main graph: app.js renderGraph() → SVG #dynamic-graph inside the dashboard "Momentum Tracker" card (index.html line 275–283). Called with width 360, height 170, showPrediction true, predDays 5, invert false, valueLabel 'Yield Points', valuePrecision 2, labelFn "Mon 12"-style, penaltyFlags from localStorage 'checkpoint:protocolZero' (date array → hard-zero days), counts = Friction-Inverse Cognitive Yield series (granular Y_day where bank-backed, else legacy count * C_macro imputation), targetValue = 0.50*maths + 0.30*physics + 0.20*chemistry weighted yield target. Returned slope/r2 drive trend classes on #view-dashboard: slope < −0.1 → trend-under-liquidation; slope > 0.1 && r2 > 0.7 → trend-bull-market + svg class graph-bull-run; else both cleared.
- Error momentum sparkline: app.js renderMomentumCandles() waits one requestAnimationFrame after matrix.js draws, reads #error-momentum-svg-container via extractCountsFromSvg (needs ≥2 points), replaces contents with a fresh SVG (w = max(container.clientWidth,240), h = 70) drawn with compact:true, invert:true, showPrediction:false, valueLabel 'errors'; updates #erm-avg-label to "avg X.X/day".
- Responsive behavior: viewBox + preserveAspectRatio="none" lets CSS stretch; styles.css sizes #dynamic-graph (height 220px when container ≥760px, 148px at ≤380px; bull-run drop-shadow filter class graph-bull-run).
- Precached by sw.js ('./candlestick-engine.js' in SHELL).

============================================================================
D. sw.js — service worker (v37)
============================================================================

D.1 Version & changelog header
- const VERSION = 'jeemax-v37' (the cache name). Header comments log: v37 Soundscape v5.2 (smoothed loudness envelopes, grain walk, safety-only compressor); v35 iPad audio fixes + Rain/Café MP3 precache; v34 Analysis tab removed (analysis.js gone); v33 Soundscape v4 grain-loop expansion.

D.2 Precache list (SHELL, installed into cache 'jeemax-v37')
- './', './index.html', './manifest.webmanifest', './styles.css'
- Scripts: app.js, storage.js, checkpoint.js, fx.js, matrix.js, pomodoro.js, focus-sound.js, theme.js, dashboard-clean.js, forest-bg.js, grove-islands.js, gallery-break.js, candlestick-engine.js, cns-load.js, deload.js, lifeline.js, nightguard.js, boot-sequence.js
- Icons: icons/icon-192.png, icon-512.png, icon-512-maskable.png, apple-touch-icon-180.png
- Sounds: assets/sounds/rain.mp3, rain-roof.mp3, cafe.mp3 (so the default bed works on first OFFLINE launch of the installed app; big generated WAVs stay runtime-cached)
- KaTeX: vendor/katex/katex.min.js, katex.min.css + 20 vendored woff2 fonts (AMS-Regular; Caligraphic Bold+Regular; Fraktur Bold+Regular; Main Bold/BoldItalic/Italic/Regular; Math BoldItalic/Italic; SansSerif Bold/Italic/Regular; Script-Regular; Size1–Size4 Regular; Typewriter-Regular)
- CDN_SHELL = [] (empty; CDN stuff deliberately NOT install-critical)

D.3 Strategies
- install: caches.open(VERSION) then Promise.allSettled([...SHELL, ...CDN_SHELL].map(cache.add)) — a single 404 can NEVER brick the install (old addAll rejected everything and pinned the stale worker forever, causing the documented "Analysis tab dead / new nav does nothing" failure); then self.skipWaiting().
- activate: delete every cache key !== VERSION, clients.claim(), then FORCE-RELOAD stale pages: clients.matchAll({type:'window', includeUncontrolled:true}) → Promise.allSettled(clients.map(client => client.navigate(client.url || './'))) with every promise rejection swallowed (.catch(()=>{})) — client.navigate() rejects on Safari/WebKit and an unhandled rejection would abort activation entirely. Runs once per NEW version; the reloaded page finds identical bytes so there is no reload loop (the iOS installed-PWA update fix).
- fetch handler:
  - Only GET requests are intercepted.
  - BYPASS rules: url.origin === 'https://www.googleapis.com' OR 'https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com' returns immediately — Drive/Gemini/API calls are NEVER touched. Non-local, non-CDN URLs also passthrough.
  - HTML navigations (isLocal && (req.mode==='navigate' || pathname '/' or '/index.html')): NETWORK-FIRST — fresh shell served every load, ok responses cloned into cache, offline fallback to caches.match(req,{ignoreSearch:true}) then caches.match('./'). (Stale-while-revalidate for HTML was the root cause of devices running old code.)
  - Local files WITH a dot in pathname but NOT matching /\.(js|css|png|webmanifest|ico|woff2?|html|mp3|wav)$/ are ignored entirely (query-noise filter; ignoreSearch handles ?v= hashing elsewhere).
  - Everything else (local static assets + whitelisted CDNs): STALE-WHILE-REVALIDATE — respond with cache.match(req, {ignoreSearch: isLocal}) immediately while a background fetch updates the cache (only res.ok && type basic|cors get put); network failure falls back to the cached copy.

D.4 CDN whitelist (CDN_PREFIXES, matched by startsWith)
- 'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net' (katex/fonts/three fallbacks), 'https://esm.sh' (three primary), 'https://unpkg.com' (three fallback), 'https://accounts.google.com/gsi/client'.

D.5 Message handlers
- NONE. There is no addEventListener('message')/onmessage anywhere in sw.js (verified by grep) — no skipWaiting/message protocol; updates ride install-time skipWaiting + activate-time navigate-reload instead.
- Registered from index.html (~line 1601): guarded by if (!('serviceWorker' in navigator)) return; navigator.serviceWorker.register('./sw.js').catch(...).

  - Preserved identically: storage keys jeemax_grove_v1 + jeemax_forest_daily_v1 + window._idbMirror mirroring; growth math (3 solves = 1 tree, STAGES_PER_TREE=3, MIN_SLOTS=6, MAX_SLOTS=240, golden-angle spiral, radius ∝ sqrt(slots), MIN_TREE_SPACING 2.6); 5 biomes gated at ELO 0/1300/1600/1900/2200 with identical ids/names/mapPos; 10 species with same unlock gates; per-subject planting with locked fallback; Store/Archipelago/explorer overlays, travel fade, unlock toasts, WebAudio cues, HUD progress bar; window.__groveIslands + window.__groveCard contracts (orig exports __groveIslands at its line 1896); props were already InstancedMesh-based in v1 (orig line 961).

F.1 assets/sounds/ (11 audio files + README.md)

F.3 vendor/katex/

Loaded via <script defer src="./vendor/katex/katex.min.js" onerror="window.__katexLoadFailed=true;"> (must precede the app module); consumed as window.katex by app.js's global math engine. katex.min.css + fonts/ with exactly 20 woff2 files (KaTeX_AMS-Regular, Caligraphic Bold/Regular, Fraktur Bold/Regular, Main Bold/BoldItalic/Italic/Regular, Math BoldItalic/Italic, SansSerif Bold/Italic/Regular, Script-Regular, Size1–Size4 Regular, Typewriter-Regular). F.4 vendor/three/
- Contains exactly one file: three.module.min.js — three.js r160 (header "Copyright 2010-2023 Three.js Authors"; internal revision const t="160").
- Actually imported? YES, by grove-islands.js only: ensureThree() dynamic-imports './vendor/three/three.module.min.js' FIRST (4 s timeout), falling back to https://esm.sh/three@0.160.0 → cdn.jsdelivr.net three@0.160.0 → unpkg three@0.160.0 (9 s each). forest-bg.js does NOT use the vendored copy — it loads three@0.160.0 purely from those three CDNs (offline forest-bg relies on SW runtime caching or degrades to the grid background with a toast). index.html never references three directly, and no other script imports it (verified by repo-wide grep). The SW does NOT precache the vendored three file (not in SHELL) — it qualifies for runtime stale-while-revalidate caching as a local .js on first use.


################################################################################
##  PART 8 - PROJECT METADATA, KNOWN ISSUES, AI SPEC & TOOLING
##  source: .docparts/07-docs-tooling.txt
################################################################################

JEEMaxxing — MASTER DOC: PROJECT METADATA, KNOWN ISSUES, AI INGESTION SPEC, DEVELOPER TOOLING
(Compiled by direct full-file reads of: bugs-report.txt, "gemini gem prompt.txt", package.json, skills-lock.json, scripts/loaders/esm-sh-stub.mjs, all 25 scripts/*.mjs headers + bodies, .gitignore, .vscode/settings.json, assets/sounds/README.md, README.md, node_modules manifests.)

PROJECT METADATA SNAPSHOT
- Product: JEEMaxxing ("Locked In") — a client-side JEE exam-prep "grind command center" PWA. Focus timers, question vault with spaced repetition, Elo-style skill rating, living forest visuals, cloud backup (Google Drive), accountability systems (Slump Sentry/Checkpoints, Nightguard).
- Architecture: vanilla ES6 modules; no framework, no bundler, no npm build step; must be served over HTTP (module scripts). Single source of truth is AppState in storage.js; cross-module communication via window.* bridges instead of circular imports; append-only UI modules self-wire via IIFEs with guard flags (e.g. window.__dashCoreInit-style flags like window.__dashCleanInit).
- Approximate module sizes per README module map: index.html ~1,357 lines; app.js ~9,508 (orchestrator); storage.js ~2,039; matrix.js ~2,070; checkpoint.js ~1,408; grove-islands.js ~1,859; candlestick-engine.js ~611; forest-island-juice.js ~814; forest-bg.js ~333; dashboard-clean.js ~200. Other modules: pomodoro.js, cns-load.js, deload.js, lifeline.js, nightguard.js, gallery-break.js, theme.js, fx.js, focus-sound.js, metronome.js, boot-sequence.js, sw.js.
- Persistence: IndexedDB + localStorage, optional Google Drive mirror; service worker sw.js offline cache at v15; math rendering via vendored KaTeX (vendor/katex/); 3D via vendored three.module.min.js (vendor/three/).
- UI: Design System v4 token layer in styles.css; 7 accent themes x 2 appearance modes via theme.js (window.JEEMaxTheme.set / setMode).

1. KNOWN ISSUES — bugs-report.txt (read in full, 508 lines)
- Header facts: "JEEMaxxing — BUG & EDGE-CASE AUDIT REPORT", Generated 2026-08-02; scope: all 19 JS modules, index.html, sw.js, manifest.webmanifest, styles.css; claims every CRITICAL finding was re-verified by direct code trace.
- Severity legend used by the file: [CRITICAL] outright breakage — feature dead or wrong data persisted; [MAJOR] wrong results / data corruption / crash on normal use; [MINOR] edge-case, robustness, perf, or hygiene issue.
- Fix status policy of this file: it does NOT track fixed/open/workaround per item — it is a point-in-time audit. Most CRITICAL and many MAJOR items carry an explicit "Fix:" recommendation line (reproduced below); MINOR items generally do not. Treat every finding as OPEN as of the report date unless separately verified in code. Note: the audit references modules not present in the current tree (leaderboard.js, forest-island.js, forest-island-full.js, forest-juice.js, forest-island-juice.js), indicating post-audit refactors/removals; findings against those files are historical.

CRITICAL — OUTRIGHT BREAKAGE (5 items)
- [1] pomodoro.js:438 — transitionToStudy() never resets timerEndTriggered. Symptom: session 2+ never ends; break end sets the flag true (~L296) so study end condition `secondsLeft <= 0 && !timerEndTriggered` (~L295) can never fire; timer hangs at 00:00 forever while studySecs keeps inflating every tick (no bell, no break, no session advance). Trigger: Study -> Break -> Study (any second study session). Proposed fix: set timerEndTriggered = false inside transitionToStudy(). Module: pomodoro.js. OPEN per report.
- [2] pomodoro.js:499-519 — pause/resume restarts countdown at FULL duration. Cause: pauseTimer() only clears the interval and stores nothing for countdown modes; resumeTimer() resets timerStartTime = Date.now(), so next tick computes secondsLeft = timerTotalSeconds - ~0 = full time. Trigger: pause a 50-min session with 40:00 remaining -> resume shows 50:00. Proposed fix: track a paused elapsed offset (same approach as the STOPWATCH branch which accumulates correctly at L506-508). Module: pomodoro.js. OPEN.
- [3] index.html:354 + matrix.js:1048 — "Daily Fix Queue" button DEAD. toggleDailyQueue() exported from matrix.js but never imported into app.js nor assigned to window; inline onclick resolves in global scope -> click throws "ReferenceError: toggleDailyQueue is not defined"; entire Daily Core Queue feature unreachable. Proposed fix: import in app.js and add window.toggleDailyQueue = toggleDailyQueue. Modules: index.html, matrix.js, app.js. OPEN.
- [4] index.html:487 + storage.js:1040 — "Cache All Images Locally" button DEAD. cacheAllDriveImages() imported into app.js (app.js:27) but never attached to window -> click throws ReferenceError; offline image-cache feature cannot be invoked from UI. Proposed fix: window.cacheAllDriveImages = cacheAllDriveImages. Modules: index.html, app.js, storage.js. OPEN.
- [5] sw.js:95 — Installed PWA fails to open offline. Cause: fetch handler early-returns any same-origin path containing "." that isn't .js/.css/.png/.webmanifest/.ico — which includes /index.html (the manifest start_url), so the pre-cached shell entry (sw.js:14) can never be served. Airplane mode + app icon = network error; contradicts header claim "keeps working offline". Proposed fix: allow .html in the regex or handle event.request.mode === 'navigate'. Module: sw.js. OPEN.

MAJOR — WRONG RESULTS / DATA CORRUPTION / CRASHES (items [6]-[61])
Grading & data integrity:
- [6] app.js:5861-5873 — Multi-select MCQ unsubmittable with special chars. Options rendered with escapeAttribute() into data-option (app.js:3867); at grading el.dataset.option returns ENTITY-ENCODED text ("A &amp; B"), options.indexOf(encoded) = -1 -> option silently filtered -> "Pick at least one option" forever. Any option containing & " ' < > (e.g. "x < 5") breaks it. Single-select safe (DOMParser decode at app.js:3852) — paths inconsistent. Fix proposed: decode dataset value before indexOf (mirror L3852).
- [7] app.js:6783-6785 — Wrong answers count as solved. practiceSubmit patch calls changeCount(subject, 1) on WRONG answers; changeCount (storage.js:309) increments daily solved counter -> daily target bars and error-matrix totals inflated, streak data polluted. Fix proposed: only count actual solves.
- [8] app.js:4616,5021,5169,7214 + storage.js:928 — Hardcore daily cap computed on UTC day. `new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)` (UTC) while bounty/history/streak use todayLocalKey() (local). For India (UTC+5:30) the cap resets at 05:30 local, and 30 min before local midnight a fresh quota is granted. Fix proposed: use todayLocalKey() consistently.
- [9] storage.js:1422 — Streak silently caps at 15 days. getDailyHistory() trims history to 15 entries (history.shift()); streak loop (app.js:1055-1066) walks that window -> real 20-day streak displays as 15 and "breaks" at window edge. Deload's _getStreakDays reads the wrong DOM number too.
- [10] storage.js:309 + 896-906 — String-concat counter corruption. solved/studySecs loaded as `x || 0`; stored STRING "5" (old version / edited backup / corrupt cloud payload) makes solved[subject] + 1 = "51" which then persists. Fix proposed: coerce with Number() on load.
- [11] storage.js:788-847 — No write coalescing / multi-tab protection. saveAllAsync() has no dirty flag or mutex; two tabs each hold their own AppState and last-commit-wins -> one tab's solves silently vanish; no BroadcastChannel/storage-event reconciliation.
- [12] storage.js:844-846,1284-1334 — Cloud sync races. syncStateToCloud() fire-and-forget inside saveAllAsync; throttle gates entry only, not in-flight overlap -> overlapping syncs upload whole bank twice; q.driveImageId mutated mid-iteration -> duplicate Drive files (one orphaned).
- [13] storage.js:1348-1352,1200-1207 — Cloud merge resurrects deleted questions and never persists. Heartbeat merge (every 120s) pushes any cloud question whose id isn't local — locally deleted questions return on every sync; merged bank never saved (app.js:1410 only calls _ui) -> memory-only, lost on tab close.
- [14] storage.js:1231-1234,1402-1405 — Elo merge monotonic inflation. Math.max high-water marks: loss streak on device A never propagates to device B; ratings only ever grow.
- [15] storage.js:834-838 + ~40 call sites — Silent persistence failure. Every IDB quota error (Safari private mode, disk full, image vault bloat) swallowed by .catch(console.error); app keeps running with zero persistence; reload loses everything, no warning. Fix proposed: surface banner/in-app alert on save failure.
- [16] storage.js:34-48 — openDB failure poisons the session. dbPromise never reset on failure; no onblocked/onversionchange handler; one failed open (private mode, eviction) permanently breaks every idbGet/idbSet; initApp's await loadDataAsync() has no try/catch -> app can hang/fail at boot.
- [17] storage.js:109-115 — idbRemove awaits tx.complete. IDBTransaction.complete is a Promise only in Chrome 117+/FF 128+/Safari 17.4+; older Safari (app's iPad target) gets undefined -> delete races commit; reload before commit resurrects deleted Drive token. No onerror/onabort handler either.
- [18] pomodoro.js:265,289 — Study time credited per tick, not per second. studySecs[subject]++ fires per interval tick; browser background throttling (1/5s or stopped) undercounts study time; save triggers are tick-count-based too.

2. AI INGESTION SPEC — "gemini gem prompt.txt" (read in full, 426 lines)
Purpose: the prompt spec pasted into a Gemini "Gem" to generate question dumps that JEEMaxxing ingests into its question bank; every emitted JSON object becomes a deterministic matchmaking input for Flow-State/Hardcore practice modes.

Role instructions (top section "ROLE"):
- The model is addressed as "the JEE-MMR question grader for JEEMaxxing".
- Must be PEDANTIC about bands, target times, tags, and gates. If unsure, choose the LOWER band — "the engine rewards calibration honesty and penalizes over-confidence by clamping to the chapter ceiling (+/-600) and flagging over-chapter-ceiling".

Strict generation constraints (section "STRICT GENERATION CONSTRAINTS (ZERO-REPRINTS & DETERMINISTIC KEYS)"):
1. SINGLE-EMISSION RULE: never restart/re-emit/output partial drafts of a JSON object; each input question maps to EXACTLY ONE JSON object in the array.
2. DETERMINISTIC ID SYNTAX: strict format `jee-[subj]-[chap_abbr]-q[number]` (example `jee-phys-mod-q44`); never padded numbers like `044`, never plain hashes; decide the ID BEFORE opening the `{`.
3. PRE-VALIDATED STRINGS: verify all backslashes escaped before emitting any LaTeX string; emitting a single backslash where a double-backslash is required is a "CRITICAL SYNTAX FAILURE".

Original-question-preservation section:
- Body elided in this file copy with placeholder "[... keep the existing verbatim rule exactly as before ...]".
- ADDITIONALLY block: assign qElo/difficulty/targetTimeMins SOLELY on the underlying problem-solving process (concepts to recall/combine, number of logical steps/manipulations, genuine traps/sign flips/edge cases, mathematical complexity linear/quadratic/ODE/integration). DO NOT inflate difficulty because of: long contextual stories/verbose stems, sub-parts labelled (a)(b)(c), extra plug-in numerical data, words like "LEVEL 3"/formal phrasing. Straightforward single-formula application with long intro is still easy/medium; non-trivial derivations or creative insight is hard/brutal even if stem is short.

Required JSON schema (OUTPUT FORMAT section — strict JSON, one object per question):
- id: stable-id-hash (see ID syntax above).
- type: "mcq" | "integer" | "subjective".
- subject: "physics" | "chemistry" | "maths".
- chapter: canonical name from CHAPTER DICT (verbatim match).
- qElo: integer in [1200, 2400].
- targetTimeMins: float.
- difficulty: "easy" | "medium" | "hard" | "brutal".
- tags: array of 2-5 strings from TAG VOCAB.
- stem: LaTeX/Markdown question body.
- options: ["A) ...","B) ...","C) ...","D) ..."] — omit if type != mcq.
- answer: canonical answer.
- hint: optional 1-liner.
- explanation: 2-4 line worked solution; FIRST line is the KEY STEP.
- model: literal "gem-stamped-v3".
- imageRef: OPTIONAL tag of source screenshot (e.g. "a1") — only when the diagram lives inside an uploaded screenshot or when cropping the whole question (multi-figure case).
- cropBox: OPTIONAL {x,y,w,h} fractions of the screenshot.
- optionImages: OPTIONAL object mapping option letter -> {"imageRef","cropBox"} or bare tag string (EXACTLY-ONE-figure questions only; figures inside individual MCQ options).
- solutionImage: OPTIONAL {"imageRef","cropBox"} (bare tag also OK) — worked-solution figure rendered above the worked solution in the solution popup.
- Rules: required fields MUST be present; NEVER emit NaN/null/undefined; image fields optional and attached ONLY when the figure lives inside an uploaded screenshot.

Diagram auto-crop conventions (section "DIAGRAM AUTO-CROP COORDINATES (imageRef + cropBox) — v3"):
- TWO-PHASE workflow: MAP FIRST, THEN EMIT ("single most important rule").
- Phase 1 DIAGRAM MAP PASS (before writing ANY question JSON): scan EVERY uploaded screenshot top-to-bottom; produce complete FIGURE MAP (# | tag | coords | location, e.g. "a1 {x:0.05,y:0.10,w:0.60,h:0.55} circuit — Q12 stem"). Map is scratch work, NEVER in final output — first byte of reply must still be `[`. Map rules: every figure appears EXACTLY once (missing figures = "#1 mapping failure"); apply the three cases while mapping; anchor each box to visible landmarks then convert to fractions; if exact edges can't be pinned confidently, map the ENTIRE question block (case 3) — never guess tight bounds; after drafting, LOOK AT EACH SCREENSHOT AGAIN and verify each box wraps its figure with ~2-3% outward padding and spills into no neighbour.
- Phase 2 EMIT THE JSON: copy imageRef/cropBox/optionImages/solutionImage VERBATIM from the map; never re-estimate during generation; missing figure -> STOP and return to Phase 1.
- THREE CASES per question: (1) NO FIGURE anywhere -> omit imageRef/cropBox/optionImages entirely. (2) EXACTLY ONE FIGURE -> crop just it: stem figure -> imageRef+cropBox on question; figure inside ONE option -> optionImages {"C":{"imageRef":"a1","cropBox":{...}}}; solution figure -> solutionImage (stem figure + solution figure together is fine). (3) TWO OR MORE FIGURES (two in stem, figures in 2+ options, stem+option) -> DO NOT map individually; emit ONE imageRef/cropBox covering the ENTIRE question block exactly as printed (first stem line, all options, every figure); omit optionImages; whole-question box is ALSO the default whenever unsure; keep tight around THIS question when screenshot stacks multiple questions.
- Tagging convention: label uploaded screenshots a1,a2,a3,... (or s1,s2,...) in upload order; use EXACTLY those labels as imageRef (user re-uploads same screenshots under same tags in JEEMaxxing); user-named tags used verbatim (trim whitespace, keep case); one question -> at most ONE imageRef/cropBox pair; many questions MAY share same imageRef with different cropBoxes.
- Coordinate semantics: x,y = TOP-LEFT corner as FRACTIONS of FULL ORIGINAL screenshot width/height, range [0,1] (NOT centre/bottom-left); w,h = sizes as fractions, range (0,1] (never right/bottom edges; convert corners via w=x2-x, h=y2-y); compute against FULL uploaded screenshot, never the downscaled chat preview.
- Estimation method: ANCHOR FIRST to trusted landmarks; mental 4x4 grid sanity-check (each cell 0.25x0.25) then refine boundaries to 2 decimals. Worked examples: bottom-two-thirds portrait figure {"x":0.03,"y":0.32,"w":0.94,"h":0.65}; small top-centre figure {"x":0.34,"y":0.06,"w":0.32,"h":0.22}; right-half vertically centred {"x":0.52,"y":0.38,"w":0.45,"h":0.30}; whole-question case 3 {"x":0.02,"y":0.02,"w":0.96,"h":0.55}.
- Padding: extend EVERY edge outward ~2-3% (minimum 0.02); round OUTWARD (x/y DOWN, w/h UP) to 2 decimals; never razor-thin boxes touching borders/arrows/labels.
- Verify before emitting: box centred; captions/labels included; x+w <= 1 and y+h <= 1; ENLARGE if uncertain ("slightly generous usable, amputated figure not"); whole-screenshot diagram -> {"x":0,"y":0,"w":1,"h":1}.
- Escaping note: imageRef plain short string, cropBox plain numbers — NO LaTeX escaping, never wrapped in math.

CHAPTER DICT:
- Instruction: use names verbatim; engine normalizes subject but chapter strings must match EXACTLY to avoid silent retags.
- PHYSICS (16): Kinematics, NLM & Friction, Work Energy Power, Circular Rotation, Gravitation, Properties of Matter, SHM Waves, Thermodynamics, Calorimetry, KTG, Electrostatics, Current Electricity, Magnetic Effects, EMI AC, Optics, Modern Physics.
- CHEMISTRY (27) and MATHS (22): elided in this file copy with "(unchanged)" placeholders — the file is a delta/patch over a longer prior spec.

BAND TABLE (drives Flow-State vs Hardcore matchmaking):
- T1_FOUNDATION qElo 1200-1299, P_win vs 1200 user approx 0.45 (easy recall); T2_CORE_MAINS 1300-1399 approx 0.30 (standard mains); T3_STD_MAINS 1400-1549 approx 0.20 (mains-tough); T4_ADV_EASY 1550-1799 approx 0.10 (adv easy); T5_PAPER_ADV 1800-2099 approx 0.05 (hard — HC min); T6_ELITE 2100-2299 approx 0.02 (elite); T7_OLYMP 2300-2400 approx 0.01 (olympiad).
- ENGINE qElo grid is wider: T1 800-1099, T2 1100-1299, T3 1300-1549, T4 1550-1799, T5 1800-2099, T6 2100-2299, T7 2300-2550. Gem's qElo MUST land on the engine grid (example: 1500 -> T3_STD_MAINS).

TARGET TIME BAND x SUBJECT table (may deviate +/-0.5 min), columns Phys / Chem / Maths:
- T1: 2 / 2 / 2; T2: 3 / 3 / 3; T3: 5 / 5 / 5; T4: 7 / 7.5 / 7; T5: 9 / 10 / 9; T6: 11 / 13 / 11; T7: 15 / 16 / 15.
- Chemistry runs ~10-15% slower physically -> engine applies physicsTauBuffer=0.85 time carve-out automatically; targetTimeMins should still respect chapter complexity.

FLOW-STATE / HARDCORE GATES (do not violate):
1. qElo ceiling per chapter = chapter empirical avg +/- 600; engine clamps above-ceiling values and flags "over-chapter-ceiling"; don't try to exceed ceilings — if problem feels T7 but chapter sits mid-T4, classify T4 and note it in explanation.
2. HARD-eligible questions MUST have qElo >= 1800 (T5_PAPER_ADV or above); FLOW-eligible SHOULD sit in 1300-1550 (T2/T3).
3. NEVER emit qElo outside [1200, 2400] (engine clamps outside).
4. NEVER reuse an id; keep stable across re-stamps so bank can re-ingest without duplicating rows.

DIFFICULTY <-> BAND CORRELATION (sanity check):
- easy -> T1-T2 (1200 <= qElo <= 1500); medium -> T3-T4 (1400 <= qElo <= 1700); hard -> T4-T5 (1500 <= qElo <= 1900); brutal -> T5-T7 (1700 <= qElo <= 2400).
- "easy" must NOT have qElo > 1500; "brutal" must NOT have qElo < 1700; mismatch = automatic rejection on preview.

- mechanics: kinematics, nlm, wep, rotation, gravitation, shm, waves, thermo, fluids, em.
- electro: electrostatics, current, magnetic, emi, ac, optics, modern.
- chem-phys: mole, structure, bonding, states, thermochem, equilibrium, kinetics, surface, coordination.

- maths: algebra, trig, coord, vectors, calculus, limits, diff, integration, de, complex, matrices, prob, stat, sequences, pnc, binomial.
- cognitive: recall, application, analysis, synthesis, numerical, conceptual, visualization, multi-step, trick, sanity-check, formula-heavy.
- difficulty-adj (only when true): time-pressure, misdirection, option-trap, two-step-substitution, dimensional-analysis.

REWARD-SIDE CALIBRATION ("engine math will mirror this"):
- explanation MUST contain the KEY STEP as FIRST line; engine mines it for the "What did you learn" chip; no fluff-only.
- For multi-step problems the FIRST step is the differentiator — make it explicit so the engine can credit partial understanding.
- Sign / +C / balanced-nonbalanced / vector-direction traps -> tag "sanity-check".
- Problems crackable by shortcut/trick -> tag "trick" and put the trick on line 1 of explanation.
- Hardcore-eligible questions add tag "boss-fight" so chapter-detail UI surfaces them in the HC pool.

BATCH FORMAT / output constraints:
- For 1000-question batches: ONE JSON array, one object per line (NDJSON also fine). NO markdown wrap unless asked; NO preamble; first byte is `[` or `{`. Output ONLY the JSON array — never the Phase-1 figure map or scratch notes.
- If asked for "raw dump": emit a ```json fenced block, one object per line; the parser pulls qElo, targetTimeMins, tags, difficulty, model directly from output.
- Screenshot tagging reminder: label uploads a1, a2, a3... in upload order, referenced via imageRef.

MANDATORY JSON & LATEX ESCAPING RULES (memorize-this section):
- Every LaTeX backslash inside a JSON string MUST be double-escaped (`\\`); applies to all JSON strings: stem, hint, explanation, answer, options.
- Environments (matrices/aligns/cases): wrap ENTIRE \begin{env}...\end{env} in ONE delimiter pair — $$ ... $$ standalone/display or $ ... $ inline; NEVER split begin/end into separate math groups; NEVER emit <LaTex>-style tags (app doesn't understand them); row break \\ parsed -> emit \\\\ raw ("escape ladder").
- ESCAPE LADDER table rows: desired `\frac` -> parsed \\frac -> raw JSON \\frac; desired matrix row break `\\` -> raw \\\\; desired text newline -> raw \n. NEVER raw `\\n` (parses to literal backslash+n; KaTeX paints red "\n" mid-stem e.g. "\nList"); NEVER raw `\\` immediately before digit/space (collapses matrix rows; red "\1"/"\0"); double-escape rule applies to LaTeX COMMAND backslashes only — newline is a JSON escape, emit \n.
- HARD JSON CRASHES (invalid escapes): in JSON a single `\` must be followed by " \ / b f n r t u; anything else fatal. Examples given: \alpha->\a CRASH, \cos/\cap/\cup->\c, \sum/\sin/\subset->\s, \lim/\le/\lambda/\left->\l, \pi/\pm/\partial->\p, \int/\in/\iota->\i, \mu/\mathbb/\mathrm->\m. Always double-escape: \\alpha, \\cos, \\sum, \\in, \\mathbb.
- SILENT CORRUPTIONS (valid JSON escapes that break LaTeX): \theta,\tau,\tan,\times -> JSON \t TAB -> renders "heta"/"an"; \neq,\nabla,\neg,\not -> \n NEWLINE -> "eq"/"abla"; \phi,\forall -> \f FORM FEED -> "hi"/"orall"; \right,\Re -> \r CR -> "ight"; \bar,\because -> \b BACKSPACE -> "ar".
- TEXT-MODE vs MATH-MODE BLEED: characters OUTSIDE $...$ must be escaped (double-escaped in JSON): currency "$5" -> "\\$5" else LaTeX enters math mode; percentages "50%" -> "50\\%" else LaTeX comments out rest; ampersands/underscores outside math -> "\\&", "\\_".
- CHEMISTRY & UNITS: do NOT use \ce{} (mhchem) unless UI supports it — use \\text{H}_2\\text{O} or \\mathrm{H_2O}. NEVER raw unicode math glyphs; always commands: !=\\neq, <=\\le, >=\\ge, +/-=\\pm, x=\\times, infinity=\\infty, equivalent=\\equiv, arrow=\\to, degree=^\\circ, micro=\\mu.
- NO literal newlines inside JSON string values ("Control character in string" fatal); use \n or keep string on one line.
- BAD examples shown: unescaped "via $4\text{H} \to {}^4\text{He} + 2\nu_e$"; "\Delta m \implies \frac{5}{4}" explanation; "<LaTex>\begin{pmatrix}...</LaTex>" option. GOOD counterparts shown with proper double escaping, including "$\\begin{pmatrix} 1 \\\\ 2 \\end{pmatrix}$".
- Two complete GOOD example objects: jee-phys-cur-q12 (mcq, Current Electricity, qElo 1500, targetTimeMins 5, medium, tags ["current","application","visualization"], imageRef "a1", cropBox {0.05,0.10,0.60,0.55}, solutionImage a2 {0.10,0.50,0.50,0.40}, model gem-stamped-v3) and jee-phys-opt-q31 (Optics, qElo 1600, 7 min, hard, tags ["optics","visualization","multi-step"], WHOLE-QUESTION cropBox {0.02,0.02,0.96,0.55}, no optionImages).

Versioning/changelog inside the file:

- Section header "DIAGRAM AUTO-CROP COORDINATES (imageRef + cropBox) — v3" marks the crop-spec revision.
- Internal elision placeholders ("[... keep the existing verbatim rule exactly as before ...]", CHEMISTRY "(unchanged)", MATHS "(unchanged)") show this file is maintained as a delta over a longer base spec; there is no explicit changelog table.

scripts/loaders/esm-sh-stub.mjs (16 lines):
- An ESM loader resolve hook so Node tests can import real app modules. Reason in header comment: leaderboard.js statically imports supabase from https://esm.sh, which Node's default loader can't fetch.
- Behavior: export function resolve(specifier, context, nextResolve) — any specifier starting with 'https://esm.sh/' is short-circuited to a data:text/javascript URL containing a stub: `export const createClient = () => ({ auth: { getUser: async () => ({ data: null, error: null }) }, from: () => ({ select: () => ({ eq: () => ({ maybeSingle: async () => null }), order: () => ({ limit: () => ({ data: [] }) }) }) }) });` All other specifiers fall through to nextResolve.
- Invocation: registered from smoke-practice-nav.mjs via node:module register(pathToFileURL(.../esm-sh-stub.mjs).href, import.meta.url); the caller documents that register() requires a URL — a plain Windows path parses as scheme "c:" and dies with ERR_UNSUPPORTED_ESM_URL_SCHEME.

4. SCRIPTS INVENTORY (scripts/)
Categories: 2 generators, 2 pure-Node analyzers/auditors, 4 Node smoke tests (stubbed browser globals, import REAL modules), 17 Playwright-based QA harnesses (each spins its own node:http static server on a distinct port serving the repo root, launches headless Chromium via channel fallback msedge -> chrome -> registry lookup, captures console errors + pageerror, tallies pass/fail asserts), plus the loader stub above. All invoked as `node scripts/<name>.mjs` unless wired into npm scripts (section 3).

Generators:
- gen-icons.mjs (104 lines): generates PWA icons (192, 512, plus apple-touch-icon 180 and a maskable variant with 14% padding) as real PNGs using a minimal pure-Node zlib PNG encoder (CRC32 table, IHDR/IDAT/IEND chunks, RGBA rasterizer with polygon fill + lerp gradients); background BG=[5,5,8], EDGE=[18,20,34]. Run: node scripts/gen-icons.mjs.
- gen-ambient-sounds.mjs (429 lines): "v3 (clean-pass)" renderer of the Focus Soundscape WAV loops into assets/sounds (white/pink/brown noise, drone, ocean, stream, fire, wind); 16-bit mono PCM @ 32000 Hz. Header documents measured v2 flaws it fixes: linear seam crossfade causing -3 dB dip + tick (drone seam measured 3.6x mean sample step) replaced by equal-power sin/cos blending + exactly-periodic drone; gunshot-like fire crackles tamed with denser quieter crackles + broadband AGC + soft-knee peak limiter; per-bed loudness normalization (was white -9 dBFS RMS vs ocean -28); DC-blocking; wind wander reduced to +/-12% low-Q. Run: node scripts/gen-ambient-sounds.mjs; audit with analyze-wavs.mjs.

Pure-Node analyzers (no browser):
- analyze-wavs.mjs (103 lines): numeric WAV audit finding artifacts behind "weird/cutting/blowing up" complaints without ears: seam-click ratio (max adjacent-sample step in a +/-60 ms window around the loop point vs mean step), loudness dip around the wrap, crest factor per 500 ms window, DC offset. RIFF chunk walker handles LIST chunks. Usage: node scripts/analyze-wavs.mjs [dir].
- loaders/esm-sh-stub.mjs: see section 3 (Node ESM resolve hook stubbing esm.sh supabase).

Node smoke tests (browser globals stubbed; import the REAL module files):

6. ASSETS/SOUNDS/README.md (39 lines)
- Purpose statement: all focus-soundscape audio bundled locally so the app works offline (service worker caches them on first use).
- Real recordings (CC0 1.0 Universal — public domain, no attribution required), sourced from Internet Archive collections Red_Library_Nature_Rain and SSE_Library_AMBIENCE:
  - rain.mp3 <- archive.org/details/Red_Library_Nature_Rain, source file R22-25-General Rain.mp3.
  - rain-roof.mp3 <- same collection, source R22-17-Smooth Rain on Roof.mp3.
  - cafe.mp3 <- archive.org/details/SSE_Library_AMBIENCE, source RESTAURANT & BAR/AMBRest_Cafe ambience; good walla_CS_USC.mp3.
- Playback expansion: recordings under 45 s are expanded at load time into long grain-loop canvases by focus-sound.js (circular overlap-add, equal-power windows) so there are no splice jumps and the wrap seam is seamless; longer recordings loop through a constant-loudness equal-power crossfade looper. Stereo width is added at playback (Haas widener in focus-sound.js).
- Generated loops (fully synthetic, no external source), created by scripts/gen-ambient-sounds.mjs v3 (`node scripts/gen-ambient-sounds.mjs`) and audited numerically by scripts/analyze-wavs.mjs:
  - white.wav / pink.wav / brown.wav: white / pink (Paul Kellet filter) / brown noise, 36 s loops, equal-power seams, DC-blocked.
  - drone.wav: exactly-periodic partial stack (55 / 82.5 / 110 / 165 / 220 Hz + beat pairs), integer-cycle breathing, faint pink air bed.
  - ocean.wav: brown surge bed under a 6-layer incommensurate swell contour + stochastic breaking-wave events, 68 s.
  - stream.wav: two wandering bandpass brook channels + sparkle + soft clustered bubble plops, 56 s.
  - fire.wav: ember bed + slow roar + dense quiet crackles (soft attack, low Q), AGC-tamed, 60 s.
  - wind.wav: three gently-drifting air layers (+/-12% wander, low Q — no siren howl) + gust envelope + leaf rustle, 60 s.
- Mastering guarantees stated: every loop seam-baked with EQUAL-POWER crossfades (linear fades dip -3 dB on uncorrelated noise tails — that was v2's wrap "wobble"), normalized to per-bed loudness targets (-18 to -23 dBFS RMS so presets switch at matched levels), peak-limited via soft knee (no blow-outs), DC-blocked.
